		private static List<Entity> getNearbyVerdantEngines(ServerLevel level, Entity self, double centerX, double centerZ, double maxDist) {
		if (level == null) return java.util.Collections.emptyList();
		List<Entity> list = new ArrayList<>();
		double maxDistSq = maxDist * maxDist;
		VerdantHiveState hiveState = VERDANT_HIVES.get(level.dimension().location());
		if (hiveState != null) {
			for (VerdantHiveMember member : hiveState.members.values()) {
				if (self != null && member.entityUuid.equals(self.getUUID())) continue;
				double dx = member.currentX - centerX;
				double dz = member.currentZ - centerZ;
				if (dx * dx + dz * dz <= maxDistSq) {
					Entity e = level.getEntity(member.entityUuid);
					if (e != null && e.isAlive() && isVerdantEngine(e) && e != self) {
						list.add(e);
					}
				}
			}
			return list;
		}
		for (Map.Entry<UUID, ActiveVerdantCore> entry : ACTIVE_CORES.entrySet()) {
			if (self != null && entry.getKey().equals(self.getUUID())) continue;
			ActiveVerdantCore core = entry.getValue();
			if (!core.dimension.equals(level.dimension().location())) continue;
			double dx = core.coreX - centerX;
			double dz = core.coreZ - centerZ;
			if (dx * dx + dz * dz <= maxDistSq) {
				Entity e = level.getEntity(entry.getKey());
				if (e != null && e.isAlive() && isVerdantEngine(e) && e != self) {
					list.add(e);
				}
			}
		}
		return list;
	}

	public static final boolean BEDROCK_TESTING_MODE = true;

	public static final int BEDROCK_CRACK_BASE_POUNDS_DEFAULT = 3;
	public static final int BEDROCK_SHATTER_BASE_POUNDS_DEFAULT = 5;
	public static final int BEDROCK_CRACK_MID_POUNDS_DEFAULT = 5;
	public static final int BEDROCK_SHATTER_MID_POUNDS_DEFAULT = 7;
	public static final int BEDROCK_CRACK_OUTER_POUNDS_DEFAULT = 7;
	public static final int BEDROCK_SHATTER_OUTER_POUNDS_DEFAULT = 9;

	public static final int BEDROCK_CRACK_BASE_POUNDS_TESTING = 1;
	public static final int BEDROCK_SHATTER_BASE_POUNDS_TESTING = 1;
	public static final int BEDROCK_CRACK_MID_POUNDS_TESTING = 2;
	public static final int BEDROCK_SHATTER_MID_POUNDS_TESTING = 2;
	public static final int BEDROCK_CRACK_OUTER_POUNDS_TESTING = 2;
	public static final int BEDROCK_SHATTER_OUTER_POUNDS_TESTING = 3;

	public static boolean isBedrockTestingEnabled(CompoundTag nbt) {
		if (nbt != null && nbt.contains("verdant_bedrock_testing_mode")) {
			return nbt.getBoolean("verdant_bedrock_testing_mode");
		}
		return BEDROCK_TESTING_MODE;
	}

	public static int getBedrockCrackPounds(CompoundTag nbt, double distFromCenter) {
		boolean testing = isBedrockTestingEnabled(nbt);
		int base = testing ? BEDROCK_CRACK_BASE_POUNDS_TESTING : BEDROCK_CRACK_BASE_POUNDS_DEFAULT;
		int mid = testing ? BEDROCK_CRACK_MID_POUNDS_TESTING : BEDROCK_CRACK_MID_POUNDS_DEFAULT;
		int outer = testing ? BEDROCK_CRACK_OUTER_POUNDS_TESTING : BEDROCK_CRACK_OUTER_POUNDS_DEFAULT;

		if (nbt != null && nbt.contains("verdant_bedrock_crack_pounds")) {
			base = nbt.getInt("verdant_bedrock_crack_pounds");
		}
		if (distFromCenter <= 1.5) return base;
		if (distFromCenter <= 3.5) return Math.max(base + 1, mid);
		return Math.max(base + 2, outer);
	}

	public static int getBedrockShatterPounds(CompoundTag nbt, double distFromCenter) {
		boolean testing = isBedrockTestingEnabled(nbt);
		int base = testing ? BEDROCK_SHATTER_BASE_POUNDS_TESTING : BEDROCK_SHATTER_BASE_POUNDS_DEFAULT;
		int mid = testing ? BEDROCK_SHATTER_MID_POUNDS_TESTING : BEDROCK_SHATTER_MID_POUNDS_DEFAULT;
		int outer = testing ? BEDROCK_SHATTER_OUTER_POUNDS_TESTING : BEDROCK_SHATTER_OUTER_POUNDS_DEFAULT;

		if (nbt != null && nbt.contains("verdant_bedrock_shatter_pounds")) {
			base = nbt.getInt("verdant_bedrock_shatter_pounds");
		}
		if (distFromCenter <= 1.5) return base;
		if (distFromCenter <= 3.5) return Math.max(base + 1, mid);
		return Math.max(base + 2, outer);
	}

	public static boolean isMaterialAdapted(CompoundTag nbt, Block block) {
		if (nbt == null || block == null) return false;
		String id = BuiltInRegistries.BLOCK.getKey(block).toString();
		String adapted = nbt.contains("verdant_adapted_blocks") ? nbt.getString("verdant_adapted_blocks") : "";
		return adapted.contains(id);
	}

	public static void recordMaterialEncounter(CompoundTag nbt, Block block) {
		if (nbt == null || block == null) return;
		String id = BuiltInRegistries.BLOCK.getKey(block).toString();
		String adapted = nbt.contains("verdant_adapted_blocks") ? nbt.getString("verdant_adapted_blocks") : "";
		if (!adapted.contains(id)) {
			if (!adapted.isEmpty()) adapted += ",";
			adapted += id;
			nbt.putString("verdant_adapted_blocks", adapted);
		}
	}

	public static int getBedrockCrackPounds(CompoundTag nbt, double distFromCenter, Block block) {
		if (isMaterialAdapted(nbt, block)) return 1;
		return getBedrockCrackPounds(nbt, distFromCenter);
	}

	public static int getBedrockShatterPounds(CompoundTag nbt, double distFromCenter, Block block) {
		if (isMaterialAdapted(nbt, block)) return 1;
		return getBedrockShatterPounds(nbt, distFromCenter);
	}

	public static boolean shouldShatterBedrock(CompoundTag nbt, double distFromCenter, int poundCount, Block block) {
		if (isMaterialAdapted(nbt, block)) return poundCount >= 1;
		return shouldShatterBedrock(nbt, distFromCenter, poundCount);
	}

	public static boolean shouldCrackBedrock(CompoundTag nbt, double distFromCenter, int poundCount, Block block) {
		if (isMaterialAdapted(nbt, block)) return poundCount >= 1;
		return shouldCrackBedrock(nbt, distFromCenter, poundCount);
	}

	public static boolean shouldShatterBedrock(CompoundTag nbt, double distFromCenter, int poundCount) {
		return poundCount >= getBedrockShatterPounds(nbt, distFromCenter);
	}

	public static boolean shouldCrackBedrock(CompoundTag nbt, double distFromCenter, int poundCount) {
		return poundCount >= getBedrockCrackPounds(nbt, distFromCenter);
	}

	public static boolean canInfectIndestructible(CompoundTag nbt) {
		if (nbt != null && nbt.contains("verdant_infect_indestructible_blocks")) {
			return nbt.getBoolean("verdant_infect_indestructible_blocks");
		}
		return INFECT_INDESTRUCTIBLE_BLOCKS;
	}

	public static boolean isCeiledDimension(Level level) {
		if (level == null) return false;
		return level.dimensionType().hasCeiling() || level.dimension() == Level.NETHER;
	}

	private static final java.util.Map<Block, Boolean> IS_INDESTRUCTIBLE_CACHE = new java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap<>(256);

	public static boolean isIndestructibleBlock(BlockState state, ServerLevel level, BlockPos pos) {
		if (state == null) return false;
		Block b = state.getBlock();
		if (isGungnirBlock(b)) return false;
		Boolean cached = IS_INDESTRUCTIBLE_CACHE.get(b);
		if (cached != null) return cached;
		if (b == Blocks.BEDROCK || b == Blocks.BARRIER || b == Blocks.REINFORCED_DEEPSLATE
			|| b == Blocks.END_PORTAL || b == Blocks.END_PORTAL_FRAME
			|| b == Blocks.COMMAND_BLOCK || b == Blocks.CHAIN_COMMAND_BLOCK || b == Blocks.REPEATING_COMMAND_BLOCK
			|| b == Blocks.STRUCTURE_BLOCK || b == Blocks.JIGSAW) {
			IS_INDESTRUCTIBLE_CACHE.put(b, true);
			return true;
		}
		try {
			boolean result = state.getDestroySpeed(level, pos) < 0.0F;
			IS_INDESTRUCTIBLE_CACHE.put(b, result);
			return result;
		} catch (Exception ignored) {
			return false;
		}
	}

	public static boolean isEpicenterBlockedByIndestructibleBarrier(ServerLevel level, double cx, double gy, double cz, double verdantY) {
		int bx = Mth.floor(cx);
		int bz = Mth.floor(cz);
		int bottomY = (int) Math.max(level.getMinBuildHeight() + 2.0, gy - 2.0);
		int scanY = (int) Math.min(level.getMaxBuildHeight() - 1.0, verdantY);
		BlockPos.MutableBlockPos pos = new BlockPos.MutableBlockPos(bx, scanY, bz);

		while (pos.getY() >= bottomY) {
			BlockState st = level.getBlockState(pos);
			if (st.getBlock() == Blocks.BEDROCK || st.getBlock() == Blocks.BARRIER || st.getBlock() == Blocks.REINFORCED_DEEPSLATE) {
				return true;
			}
			pos.move(Direction.DOWN);
		}
		return false;
	}

	private static final ResourceLocation VERDANT_BEAM_GRAV_MOD_ID = ResourceLocation.parse("the_backwoods:verdant_beam_gravity");
	private static final Map<UUID, Long> GRAVITY_MODIFIED_ENTITIES = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

	public static double getGravityBeamEffectRadius(CompoundTag nbt) {
		return GRAVITY_BEAM_EFFECT_RADIUS;
	}

	public static double getGravityBeamMaxModifier(CompoundTag nbt) {
		if (nbt != null && nbt.contains("verdant_gravity_max_modifier")) {
			return Math.max(0.1, Math.min(20.0, nbt.getDouble("verdant_gravity_max_modifier")));
		}
		return GRAVITY_BEAM_MAX_GRAVITY_MODIFIER;
	}

	public static boolean isGravityBeamPenetrationEnabled(CompoundTag nbt) {
		if (nbt != null && nbt.contains("verdant_penetration_enabled")) {
			return persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_penetration_enabled", GRAVITY_BEAM_PENETRATION_ENABLED);
		}
		return GRAVITY_BEAM_PENETRATION_ENABLED;
	}

	public static double getGravityBeamPenetrationSpeed(CompoundTag nbt) {
		if (nbt != null && nbt.contains("verdant_penetration_speed")) {
			return Math.max(0.01, Math.min(5.0, nbt.getDouble("verdant_penetration_speed")));
		}
		return GRAVITY_BEAM_PENETRATION_SPEED;
	}

	public static double getShockwaveParticleRadius(CompoundTag nbt) {
		if (nbt != null && nbt.contains("verdant_shockwave_particle_radius")) {
			return Math.max(16.0, Math.min(256.0, nbt.getDouble("verdant_shockwave_particle_radius")));
		}
		return SHOCKWAVE_PARTICLE_RADIUS;
	}

	public static double getShockwaveParticleQuality(CompoundTag nbt) {
		if (nbt != null && nbt.contains("verdant_shockwave_particle_quality")) {
			return Math.max(0.0, Math.min(3.0, nbt.getDouble("verdant_shockwave_particle_quality")));
		}
		return Math.max(0.0, Math.min(3.0, SHOCKWAVE_PARTICLE_QUALITY));
	}

	public static int getSaturationRelocateDelayTicks(CompoundTag nbt) {
		int defaultDelay = (nbt != null && persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_supercharged_mode", false))
			? SUPERCHARGED_SATURATION_RELOCATE_DELAY_TICKS
			: SATURATION_RELOCATE_DELAY_TICKS;
		return Math.max(0, persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_saturation_dwell_delay", defaultDelay));
	}

	public static double getInfectionSaturationThreshold(CompoundTag nbt) {
		if (nbt != null) nbt.putDouble("verdant_infection_saturation_threshold", INFECTION_SATURATION_THRESHOLD);
		return INFECTION_SATURATION_THRESHOLD;
	}

	private static int getVerdantEngineIndex(ServerLevel level, Entity entity) {
		if (entity == null || level == null) return 1;
		VerdantHiveState hiveState = VERDANT_HIVES.get(level.dimension().location());
		if (hiveState == null) return 1;
		List<UUID> activeMembers = new ArrayList<>();
		for (UUID memberUuid : hiveState.members.keySet()) {
			Entity member = level.getEntity(memberUuid);
			if (member != null && member.isAlive()) activeMembers.add(memberUuid);
		}
		activeMembers.sort(UUID::compareTo);
		int memberIndex = activeMembers.indexOf(entity.getUUID());
		if (memberIndex >= 0) return memberIndex + 1;
		return 1;
	}

	public static double getInfectionSpeedMultiplier(CompoundTag nbt) {
		if (nbt != null && nbt.contains("verdant_infection_speed_multiplier")) {
			return Math.max(0.01, Math.min(5.0, nbt.getDouble("verdant_infection_speed_multiplier")));
		}
		return Math.max(0.01, Math.min(5.0, INFECTION_SPEED_MULTIPLIER));
	}

	public static double getBeamParticleQuality(CompoundTag nbt) {
		if (nbt != null && nbt.contains("verdant_beam_particle_quality")) {
			return Math.max(0.0, Math.min(2.0, nbt.getDouble("verdant_beam_particle_quality")));
		}
		return Math.max(0.0, Math.min(2.0, GRAVITY_BEAM_PARTICLE_QUALITY));
	}

	public static double getBeamParticleQuality(ServerLevel level, double x, double z, CompoundTag nbt) {
		double baseQuality = getBeamParticleQuality(nbt);
		if (baseQuality <= 0.001 || level == null) return 0.0;

		double distSq = getNearestPlayerDistanceSq(level, x, z);
		if (distSq > GRAVITY_BEAM_PARTICLE_RENDER_DIST_SQ) return 0.0;

		double tierFactor;
		if (distSq <= 4096.0) {
			tierFactor = 0.65;
		} else if (distSq <= 20736.0) {
			tierFactor = 0.40;
		} else {
			tierFactor = 0.20;
		}

		return tierFactor * (baseQuality / 0.65);
	}

	public static double getBeamParticleRenderDistSq(CompoundTag nbt) {
		if (nbt != null && nbt.contains("verdant_beam_particle_render_dist")) {
			double dist = Math.max(16.0, Math.min(2048.0, persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_beam_particle_render_dist", GRAVITY_BEAM_PARTICLE_RENDER_DIST)));
			return dist * dist;
		}
		return GRAVITY_BEAM_PARTICLE_RENDER_DIST_SQ;
	}

	private static final Map<String, Field> SYNCED_DATA_FIELD_CACHE = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

	private static final TagKey<EntityType<?>> WOODBOUND_TAG_1 = TagKey.create(Registries.ENTITY_TYPE, ResourceLocation.parse("the_backwoods:woodbound_entities"));
	private static final TagKey<EntityType<?>> WOODBOUND_TAG_2 = TagKey.create(Registries.ENTITY_TYPE, ResourceLocation.parse("thebackwoods:woodbound_entities"));

		private static boolean isHomeDimension(ServerLevel level) {
		if (level == null) return false;
		ResourceLocation dimLoc = level.dimension().location();
		if (dimLoc == null) return false;
		String ns = dimLoc.getNamespace().toLowerCase();
		String path = dimLoc.getPath().toLowerCase();
		return ns.contains("the_backwoods") || ns.contains("thebackwoods") || path.contains("backwood");
	}
	private static boolean isWoodbound(Entity entity) {
		if (entity == null) return false;
		String typeKey = BuiltInRegistries.ENTITY_TYPE.getKey(entity.getType()).toString();
		if (typeKey.contains("fractus")) return true;
		return entity.getType().is(WOODBOUND_TAG_1) || entity.getType().is(WOODBOUND_TAG_2) || entity.getTags().contains("woodbound_entities");
	}

	private static long hiveRegionKey(double x, double z) {
		int regionX = Mth.floor(x / HIVE_REGION_SIZE);
		int regionZ = Mth.floor(z / HIVE_REGION_SIZE);
		return ((long) regionX << 32) ^ (regionZ & 0xffffffffL);
	}

	private static int hiveRegionX(long regionKey) {
		return (int) (regionKey >> 32);
	}

	private static int hiveRegionZ(long regionKey) {
		return (int) regionKey;
	}

	private static long[] persistentLongArray(CompoundTag tag, String key) {
		return tag != null && tag.contains(key) ? tag.getLongArray(key) : new long[0];
	}

	private static long calculateHiveWorldRegionCount(ServerLevel level) {
		double rawMinX = level.getWorldBorder().getMinX();
		double rawMaxX = level.getWorldBorder().getMaxX();
		double rawMinZ = level.getWorldBorder().getMinZ();
		double rawMaxZ = level.getWorldBorder().getMaxZ();
		double borderMinX = rawMinX + WORLD_BORDER_CLEARANCE;
		double borderMaxX = rawMaxX - WORLD_BORDER_CLEARANCE;
		double borderMinZ = rawMinZ + WORLD_BORDER_CLEARANCE;
		double borderMaxZ = rawMaxZ - WORLD_BORDER_CLEARANCE;
		if (borderMinX >= borderMaxX) borderMinX = borderMaxX = (rawMinX + rawMaxX) * 0.5;
		if (borderMinZ >= borderMaxZ) borderMinZ = borderMaxZ = (rawMinZ + rawMaxZ) * 0.5;
		long minimumRegionX = (long) Math.floor(borderMinX / HIVE_REGION_SIZE);
		long maximumRegionX = (long) Math.floor((borderMinX == borderMaxX ? borderMaxX : Math.nextDown(borderMaxX)) / HIVE_REGION_SIZE);
		long minimumRegionZ = (long) Math.floor(borderMinZ / HIVE_REGION_SIZE);
		long maximumRegionZ = (long) Math.floor((borderMinZ == borderMaxZ ? borderMaxZ : Math.nextDown(borderMaxZ)) / HIVE_REGION_SIZE);
		long regionWidth = Math.max(0L, maximumRegionX - minimumRegionX + 1L);
		long regionDepth = Math.max(0L, maximumRegionZ - minimumRegionZ + 1L);
		if (regionWidth == 0L || regionDepth == 0L) return Long.MAX_VALUE;
		if (regionWidth > Long.MAX_VALUE / regionDepth) return Long.MAX_VALUE;
		return regionWidth * regionDepth;
	}

	private static boolean isHiveRegionInsideBorder(ServerLevel level, long regionKey) {
		double regionMinX = hiveRegionX(regionKey) * HIVE_REGION_SIZE;
		double regionMaxX = regionMinX + HIVE_REGION_SIZE;
		double regionMinZ = hiveRegionZ(regionKey) * HIVE_REGION_SIZE;
		double regionMaxZ = regionMinZ + HIVE_REGION_SIZE;
		double safeMinX = level.getWorldBorder().getMinX() + WORLD_BORDER_CLEARANCE;
		double safeMaxX = level.getWorldBorder().getMaxX() - WORLD_BORDER_CLEARANCE;
		double safeMinZ = level.getWorldBorder().getMinZ() + WORLD_BORDER_CLEARANCE;
		double safeMaxZ = level.getWorldBorder().getMaxZ() - WORLD_BORDER_CLEARANCE;
		if (safeMinX >= safeMaxX) safeMinX = safeMaxX = (level.getWorldBorder().getMinX() + level.getWorldBorder().getMaxX()) * 0.5;
		if (safeMinZ >= safeMaxZ) safeMinZ = safeMaxZ = (level.getWorldBorder().getMinZ() + level.getWorldBorder().getMaxZ()) * 0.5;
		return regionMaxX > safeMinX && regionMinX <= safeMaxX
			&& regionMaxZ > safeMinZ && regionMinZ <= safeMaxZ;
	}

	private static int countCompletedHiveRegionsInsideBorder(ServerLevel level, VerdantHiveState hiveState) {
		int completedInsideBorder = 0;
		for (long completedRegion : hiveState.completedRegions.keySet()) {
			if (isHiveRegionInsideBorder(level, completedRegion)) completedInsideBorder++;
		}
		return completedInsideBorder;
	}

	private static void syncHiveCompletedRegions(CompoundTag nbt, VerdantHiveState hiveState) {
		if (nbt == null || hiveState == null) return;
		long[] savedRegions = persistentLongArray(nbt, "verdant_hive_completed_regions");
		long[] completedRegions = new long[hiveState.completedRegions.size()];
		int completedIndex = 0;
		for (long completedRegion : hiveState.completedRegions.keySet()) {
			completedRegions[completedIndex++] = completedRegion;
		}
		java.util.Arrays.sort(completedRegions);
		java.util.Arrays.sort(savedRegions);
		if (!java.util.Arrays.equals(savedRegions, completedRegions)) {
			nbt.putLongArray("verdant_hive_completed_regions", completedRegions);
		}
	}

	private static void syncHiveCompletedRegionsToMembers(ServerLevel level, VerdantHiveState hiveState) {
		if (level == null || hiveState == null) return;
		for (UUID memberUuid : hiveState.members.keySet()) {
			Entity member = level.getEntity(memberUuid);
			if (member == null) continue;
			CompoundTag memberNbt = member.getPersistentData();
			syncHiveCompletedRegions(memberNbt, hiveState);
			memberNbt.putInt("verdant_hive_completed_region_count", countCompletedHiveRegionsInsideBorder(level, hiveState));
		}
	}

	private static void syncHiveBorderSnapshotToMembers(ServerLevel level, VerdantHiveState hiveState) {
		if (level == null || hiveState == null) return;
		for (UUID memberUuid : hiveState.members.keySet()) {
			Entity member = level.getEntity(memberUuid);
			if (member == null) continue;
			CompoundTag memberNbt = member.getPersistentData();
			memberNbt.putDouble("verdant_hive_border_min_x", hiveState.lastBorderMinX);
			memberNbt.putDouble("verdant_hive_border_max_x", hiveState.lastBorderMaxX);
			memberNbt.putDouble("verdant_hive_border_min_z", hiveState.lastBorderMinZ);
			memberNbt.putDouble("verdant_hive_border_max_z", hiveState.lastBorderMaxZ);
		}
	}

	private static boolean isHiveSaturationSampleInsideBorder(ServerLevel level, int blockX, int blockZ) {
		double sampleX = blockX + 0.5;
		double sampleZ = blockZ + 0.5;
		return sampleX >= level.getWorldBorder().getMinX() && sampleX < level.getWorldBorder().getMaxX()
			&& sampleZ >= level.getWorldBorder().getMinZ() && sampleZ < level.getWorldBorder().getMaxZ();
	}

	private static double[] getHiveRegionCoverageBounds(ServerLevel level, long regionKey) {
		double minimumX = Math.max(hiveRegionX(regionKey) * HIVE_REGION_SIZE, level.getWorldBorder().getMinX() + WORLD_BORDER_CLEARANCE);
		double maximumX = Math.min((hiveRegionX(regionKey) + 1.0) * HIVE_REGION_SIZE, level.getWorldBorder().getMaxX() - WORLD_BORDER_CLEARANCE);
		double minimumZ = Math.max(hiveRegionZ(regionKey) * HIVE_REGION_SIZE, level.getWorldBorder().getMinZ() + WORLD_BORDER_CLEARANCE);
		double maximumZ = Math.min((hiveRegionZ(regionKey) + 1.0) * HIVE_REGION_SIZE, level.getWorldBorder().getMaxZ() - WORLD_BORDER_CLEARANCE);
		if (minimumX >= maximumX || minimumZ >= maximumZ) return null;
		return new double[]{minimumX, maximumX, minimumZ, maximumZ};
	}

	private static int getHiveCoverageColumnState(ServerLevel level, int blockX, int blockZ, double hoverY) {
		net.minecraft.world.level.chunk.ChunkAccess chunk = level.getChunkSource().getChunkNow(blockX >> 4, blockZ >> 4);
		if (chunk == null) return -1;
		int surfaceY = chunk.getHeight(Heightmap.Types.MOTION_BLOCKING_NO_LEAVES, blockX & 15, blockZ & 15);
		if (surfaceY <= level.getMinBuildHeight() + 5) return -1;
		return isColumnDeeplyInfected(level, blockX, blockZ, Math.max(hoverY, surfaceY + 2.0)) ? 1 : 0;
	}

	private static double auditHiveRegionGrid(ServerLevel level, VerdantHiveState hiveState, long regionKey,
			double hoverY, double[] bounds, int gridSize) {
		boolean[][] validSamples = new boolean[gridSize][gridSize];
		boolean[][] infectedSamples = new boolean[gridSize][gridSize];
		double[][] sampleXPositions = new double[gridSize][gridSize];
		double[][] sampleZPositions = new double[gridSize][gridSize];
		int validCount = 0;
		int infectedCount = 0;
		for (int gridX = 0; gridX < gridSize; gridX++) {
			double sampleXPosition = bounds[0] + (gridX + 0.5) * (bounds[1] - bounds[0]) / gridSize;
			for (int gridZ = 0; gridZ < gridSize; gridZ++) {
				double sampleZPosition = bounds[2] + (gridZ + 0.5) * (bounds[3] - bounds[2]) / gridSize;
				sampleXPositions[gridX][gridZ] = sampleXPosition;
				sampleZPositions[gridX][gridZ] = sampleZPosition;
				int sampleState = getHiveCoverageColumnState(level, Mth.floor(sampleXPosition), Mth.floor(sampleZPosition), hoverY);
				if (sampleState < 0) continue;
				validSamples[gridX][gridZ] = true;
				validCount++;
				if (sampleState > 0) {
					infectedSamples[gridX][gridZ] = true;
					infectedCount++;
				}
			}
		}

		double bestGapScore = -1.0;
		double bestGapX = Double.NaN;
		double bestGapZ = Double.NaN;
		for (int gridX = 0; gridX < gridSize; gridX++) {
			for (int gridZ = 0; gridZ < gridSize; gridZ++) {
				if (!validSamples[gridX][gridZ] || infectedSamples[gridX][gridZ]) continue;
				double gapScore = 0.0;
				for (int neighborX = Math.max(0, gridX - 1); neighborX <= Math.min(gridSize - 1, gridX + 1); neighborX++) {
					for (int neighborZ = Math.max(0, gridZ - 1); neighborZ <= Math.min(gridSize - 1, gridZ + 1); neighborZ++) {
						if (validSamples[neighborX][neighborZ] && !infectedSamples[neighborX][neighborZ]) gapScore += 1.0;
					}
				}
				if (gapScore > bestGapScore) {
					bestGapScore = gapScore;
					bestGapX = sampleXPositions[gridX][gridZ];
					bestGapZ = sampleZPositions[gridX][gridZ];
				}
			}
		}
		if (!Double.isNaN(bestGapX)) {
			hiveState.regionGapX.put(regionKey, bestGapX);
			hiveState.regionGapZ.put(regionKey, bestGapZ);
		} else {
			hiveState.regionGapX.remove(regionKey);
			hiveState.regionGapZ.remove(regionKey);
		}
		return validCount > 0 ? (double) infectedCount / validCount : 1.0;
	}

	private static double auditHiveRegionSaturation(ServerLevel level, VerdantHiveState hiveState, long regionKey, double hoverY, boolean forceAudit) {
		if (level == null || hiveState == null) return 0.0;
		long gameTime = level.getGameTime();
		Long previousAuditTick = hiveState.regionAuditTicks.get(regionKey);
		Double previousSaturation = hiveState.regionSaturation.get(regionKey);
		if (!forceAudit && previousAuditTick != null && previousSaturation != null
				&& gameTime - previousAuditTick <= HIVE_COVERAGE_CACHE_TICKS) return previousSaturation;

		double[] bounds = getHiveRegionCoverageBounds(level, regionKey);
		if (bounds == null) return 1.0;
		double saturation = auditHiveRegionGrid(level, hiveState, regionKey, hoverY, bounds, HIVE_COARSE_SURVEY_GRID_SIZE);
		if (saturation >= HIVE_REPAIR_SURVEY_THRESHOLD) {
			saturation = auditHiveRegionGrid(level, hiveState, regionKey, hoverY, bounds, HIVE_REPAIR_SURVEY_GRID_SIZE);
		}
		hiveState.regionSaturation.put(regionKey, saturation);
		hiveState.regionAuditTicks.put(regionKey, gameTime);
		return saturation;
	}

	private static double sampleHiveCandidateGapDensity(ServerLevel level, double candidateX, double candidateY, double candidateZ) {
		int totalSamples = 0;
		int infectedSamples = 0;
		for (int sampleIndex = 0; sampleIndex < 9; sampleIndex++) {
			double sampleX = candidateX;
			double sampleZ = candidateZ;
			if (sampleIndex > 0) {
				double angle = Math.PI * 2.0 * (sampleIndex - 1) / 8.0;
				sampleX += fastCos(angle) * 72.0;
				sampleZ += fastSin(angle) * 72.0;
			}
			int blockX = Mth.floor(sampleX);
			int blockZ = Mth.floor(sampleZ);
			if (!isHiveSaturationSampleInsideBorder(level, blockX, blockZ)) continue;
			totalSamples++;
			if (isColumnDeeplyInfected(level, blockX, blockZ, candidateY)) infectedSamples++;
		}
		return totalSamples > 0 ? (double) infectedSamples / totalSamples : 1.0;
	}

	private static double[] findHiveCoverageGapTarget(ServerLevel level, VerdantHiveState hiveState, Entity entity, long regionKey,
			double currentX, double currentY, double currentZ, double preferredDx, double preferredDz) {
		auditHiveRegionSaturation(level, hiveState, regionKey, currentY, false);
		Double candidateX = hiveState.regionGapX.get(regionKey);
		Double candidateZ = hiveState.regionGapZ.get(regionKey);
		if (candidateX == null || candidateZ == null) return null;
		if (!isInsideVerdantWorldBorder(level, entity, candidateX, candidateZ)) return null;
		if (Math.hypot(candidateX - currentX, candidateZ - currentZ) < 64.0) return null;
		double candidateY = calculateOptimalVerdantHoverY(level, candidateX, candidateZ, currentY);
		if (getHiveCoverageColumnState(level, Mth.floor(candidateX), Mth.floor(candidateZ), candidateY) != 0) {
			auditHiveRegionSaturation(level, hiveState, regionKey, currentY, true);
			candidateX = hiveState.regionGapX.get(regionKey);
			candidateZ = hiveState.regionGapZ.get(regionKey);
			if (candidateX == null || candidateZ == null) return null;
			candidateY = calculateOptimalVerdantHoverY(level, candidateX, candidateZ, currentY);
		}
		for (VerdantHiveMember otherMember : hiveState.members.values()) {
			if (otherMember.entityUuid.equals(entity.getUUID())) continue;
			if (Math.hypot(candidateX - otherMember.reservedX, candidateZ - otherMember.reservedZ) < 96.0) return null;
		}
		return new double[]{candidateX, candidateY, candidateZ};
	}

	private static boolean isSpawnAlreadyCoveredByHivePeer(ServerLevel level, VerdantHiveState hiveState, Entity entity,
			double spawnX, double spawnZ) {
		if (level == null || hiveState == null || entity == null) return false;
		for (VerdantHiveMember member : hiveState.members.values()) {
			if (member.entityUuid.equals(entity.getUUID())) continue;
			Entity other = level.getEntity(member.entityUuid);
			if (other == null || !other.isAlive()) continue;
			CompoundTag otherNbt = other.getPersistentData();
			double otherRadius = persistentDouble(otherNbt, "verdant_infection_radius", BEAM_RADIUS + 2.0);
			if (otherRadius < 48.0) continue;
			double coverageX = persistentBoolean(otherNbt, "verdant_is_relocating", false) ? member.reservedX : other.getX();
			double coverageZ = persistentBoolean(otherNbt, "verdant_is_relocating", false) ? member.reservedZ : other.getZ();
			if (Math.hypot(spawnX - coverageX, spawnZ - coverageZ) <= otherRadius * 0.80) return true;
		}
		return false;
	}

	private static double[] findBestSpawnExpansionTarget(ServerLevel level, VerdantHiveState hiveState, Entity entity,
			double spawnX, double spawnY, double spawnZ) {
		if (level == null || hiveState == null || entity == null) return null;
		int centerRegionX = hiveRegionX(hiveRegionKey(spawnX, spawnZ));
		int centerRegionZ = hiveRegionZ(hiveRegionKey(spawnX, spawnZ));
		long bestRegion = Long.MIN_VALUE;
		double bestSaturation = Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
		double bestDistance = Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;

		for (int regionOffsetX = -1; regionOffsetX <= 1; regionOffsetX++) {
			for (int regionOffsetZ = -1; regionOffsetZ <= 1; regionOffsetZ++) {
				long candidateRegion = ((long) (centerRegionX + regionOffsetX) << 32)
					^ ((centerRegionZ + regionOffsetZ) & 0xffffffffL);
				if (!isHiveRegionInsideBorder(level, candidateRegion)
						|| hiveState.completedRegions.containsKey(candidateRegion)
						|| isHiveRegionClaimedByOther(hiveState, entity.getUUID(), candidateRegion)) continue;
				double saturation = auditHiveRegionSaturation(level, hiveState, candidateRegion, spawnY, false);
				double[] bounds = getHiveRegionCoverageBounds(level, candidateRegion);
				if (bounds == null) continue;
				double centerX = (bounds[0] + bounds[1]) * 0.5;
				double centerZ = (bounds[2] + bounds[3]) * 0.5;
				double distance = Math.hypot(centerX - spawnX, centerZ - spawnZ);
				if (saturation < bestSaturation - 0.01
						|| (Math.abs(saturation - bestSaturation) <= 0.01 && distance > bestDistance)) {
					bestRegion = candidateRegion;
					bestSaturation = saturation;
					bestDistance = distance;
				}
			}
		}
		if (bestRegion == Long.MIN_VALUE) return null;
		double[] preferredDirection = getHivePreferredDirection(hiveState, entity);
		return findHiveCoverageGapTarget(level, hiveState, entity, bestRegion,
			spawnX, spawnY, spawnZ, preferredDirection[0], preferredDirection[1]);
	}

	private static VerdantHiveState registerVerdantWithHive(ServerLevel level, Entity entity, CompoundTag nbt, double currentX, double currentZ) {
		ResourceLocation dimensionId = level.dimension().location();
		int currentServerIdentity = System.identityHashCode(level.getServer());
		VerdantHiveState hiveState = VERDANT_HIVES.compute(dimensionId, (hiveDimension, existingHive) ->
			existingHive == null || existingHive.serverIdentity != currentServerIdentity ? new VerdantHiveState(hiveDimension, currentServerIdentity) : existingHive);
		long currentTick = level.getGameTime();
		synchronized (hiveState) {
			UUID memberUuid = entity.getUUID();
			VerdantHiveMember hiveMember = hiveState.members.get(memberUuid);
			if (hiveMember == null) {
				hiveMember = new VerdantHiveMember(memberUuid);
				hiveState.members.put(memberUuid, hiveMember);
			}
			if (hiveState.anchorUuid == null || memberUuid.compareTo(hiveState.anchorUuid) < 0) {
				hiveState.anchorUuid = memberUuid;
			}
			if (!hiveState.originEstablished) {
				hiveState.originX = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_hive_origin_x", currentX);
				hiveState.originZ = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_hive_origin_z", currentZ);
				hiveState.originEstablished = true;
			}
			if (Double.isNaN(hiveState.lastBorderMinX)) {
				hiveState.lastBorderMinX = nbt.contains("verdant_hive_border_min_x")
					? nbt.getDouble("verdant_hive_border_min_x") : level.getWorldBorder().getMinX();
				hiveState.lastBorderMaxX = nbt.contains("verdant_hive_border_max_x")
					? nbt.getDouble("verdant_hive_border_max_x") : level.getWorldBorder().getMaxX();
				hiveState.lastBorderMinZ = nbt.contains("verdant_hive_border_min_z")
					? nbt.getDouble("verdant_hive_border_min_z") : level.getWorldBorder().getMinZ();
				hiveState.lastBorderMaxZ = nbt.contains("verdant_hive_border_max_z")
					? nbt.getDouble("verdant_hive_border_max_z") : level.getWorldBorder().getMaxZ();
			}

			boolean memberRelocating = persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_is_relocating", false);
			double memberReservedX = memberRelocating ? persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_target_x", currentX) : currentX;
			double memberReservedZ = memberRelocating ? persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_target_z", currentZ) : currentZ;
			hiveMember.currentX = currentX;
			hiveMember.currentZ = currentZ;
			hiveMember.reservedX = memberReservedX;
			hiveMember.reservedZ = memberReservedZ;
			hiveMember.claimedRegion = hiveRegionKey(memberReservedX, memberReservedZ);
			hiveMember.relocating = memberRelocating;
			hiveMember.lastSeenTick = currentTick;

			if (hiveState.loadedHistories.putIfAbsent(memberUuid, Boolean.TRUE) == null) {
				long[] savedRegions = persistentLongArray(nbt, "verdant_hive_completed_regions");
				for (long savedRegion : savedRegions) {
					hiveState.completedRegions.put(savedRegion, Boolean.TRUE);
				}
			}

			if (persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_hive_dormant", false)) {
				hiveState.dormant = true;
				hiveState.phase = HIVE_PHASE_DORMANT;
				long savedDormantRegions = persistentLong(nbt, "verdant_hive_dormant_world_regions", Long.MAX_VALUE);
				if (savedDormantRegions != Long.MAX_VALUE) {
					hiveState.dormantWorldRegions = Math.min(hiveState.dormantWorldRegions, savedDormantRegions);
				}
			}

			if (hiveState.lastMaintenanceTick == Long.MIN_VALUE || currentTick - hiveState.lastMaintenanceTick >= 40L) {
				for (Map.Entry<UUID, VerdantHiveMember> memberEntry : hiveState.members.entrySet()) {
					VerdantHiveMember existingMember = memberEntry.getValue();
					if (currentTick - existingMember.lastSeenTick > HIVE_MEMBER_TIMEOUT_TICKS) {
						hiveState.members.remove(memberEntry.getKey(), existingMember);
					}
				}
				hiveState.anchorUuid = null;
				for (UUID activeUuid : hiveState.members.keySet()) {
					if (hiveState.anchorUuid == null || activeUuid.compareTo(hiveState.anchorUuid) < 0) {
						hiveState.anchorUuid = activeUuid;
					}
				}
				hiveState.totalWorldRegions = calculateHiveWorldRegionCount(level);
				hiveState.lastMaintenanceTick = currentTick;
			}

			if (!hiveState.dormant) {
				int memberState = persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_state", 0);
				if (memberState >= 1) {
					hiveState.phase = HIVE_PHASE_TERRAFORMING;
				} else if (hiveState.phase < HIVE_PHASE_TERRAFORMING) {
					hiveState.phase = HIVE_PHASE_CLAIMING;
				}
			}
			nbt.putString("verdant_hive_id", dimensionId.toString());
			nbt.putBoolean("verdant_hive_anchor", memberUuid.equals(hiveState.anchorUuid));
			nbt.putDouble("verdant_hive_origin_x", hiveState.originX);
			nbt.putDouble("verdant_hive_origin_z", hiveState.originZ);
			nbt.putInt("verdant_hive_phase", hiveState.phase);
			nbt.putLong("verdant_hive_world_regions", hiveState.totalWorldRegions);
			nbt.putInt("verdant_hive_member_count", hiveState.members.size());
			nbt.putDouble("verdant_hive_border_min_x", hiveState.lastBorderMinX);
			nbt.putDouble("verdant_hive_border_max_x", hiveState.lastBorderMaxX);
			nbt.putDouble("verdant_hive_border_min_z", hiveState.lastBorderMinZ);
			nbt.putDouble("verdant_hive_border_max_z", hiveState.lastBorderMaxZ);
			syncHiveCompletedRegions(nbt, hiveState);
			nbt.putInt("verdant_hive_completed_region_count", countCompletedHiveRegionsInsideBorder(level, hiveState));
			return hiveState;
		}
	}

	private static int getHiveWorkOffset(Entity entity) {
		return Math.floorMod(entity.getUUID().hashCode(), 40);
	}

	private static double[] getHivePreferredDirection(VerdantHiveState hiveState, Entity entity) {
		if (hiveState == null || entity == null) return new double[]{0.0, 0.0};
		synchronized (hiveState) {
			List<UUID> orderedMembers = new ArrayList<>(hiveState.members.keySet());
			orderedMembers.sort(UUID::compareTo);
			int memberIndex = orderedMembers.indexOf(entity.getUUID());
			if (memberIndex < 0) return new double[]{0.0, 0.0};
			long dimensionHash = Integer.toUnsignedLong(hiveState.dimension.hashCode());
			double baseAngle = (dimensionHash / 4294967296.0) * Math.PI * 2.0;
			double sectorAngle = baseAngle + (Math.PI * 2.0 * memberIndex / Math.max(1, orderedMembers.size()));
			return new double[]{Math.cos(sectorAngle), Math.sin(sectorAngle)};
		}
	}

	private static boolean isHiveRegionClaimedByOther(VerdantHiveState hiveState, UUID entityUuid, long candidateRegion) {
		if (hiveState == null) return false;
		for (VerdantHiveMember hiveMember : hiveState.members.values()) {
			if (!hiveMember.entityUuid.equals(entityUuid) && hiveMember.claimedRegion == candidateRegion) return true;
		}
		return false;
	}

	private static double getHiveCandidateFitness(ServerLevel level, Entity entity, double candidateX, double candidateY, double candidateZ) {
		VerdantHiveState hiveState = VERDANT_HIVES.get(level.dimension().location());
		if (hiveState == null) return 0.0;
		if (!isInsideVerdantWorldBorder(level, entity, candidateX, candidateZ)) {
			return -1000000.0;
		}
		long candidateRegion = hiveRegionKey(candidateX, candidateZ);
		if (hiveState.completedRegions.containsKey(candidateRegion)
				|| isHiveRegionClaimedByOther(hiveState, entity.getUUID(), candidateRegion)) {
			return Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
		}
		double hiveFitness = 0.0;
		hiveFitness += 10000.0;
		Double sharedRegionSaturation = hiveState.regionSaturation.get(candidateRegion);
		if (sharedRegionSaturation != null) {
			hiveFitness += (1.0 - sharedRegionSaturation) * 4000.0;
		} else {
			hiveFitness += (1.0 - sampleHiveCandidateGapDensity(level, candidateX, candidateY, candidateZ)) * 2500.0;
		}
		for (VerdantHiveMember otherMember : hiveState.members.values()) {
			if (otherMember.entityUuid.equals(entity.getUUID()) || otherMember.relocating) continue;
			Entity otherEntity = level.getEntity(otherMember.entityUuid);
			if (otherEntity == null || !otherEntity.isAlive() || isTerraformingActive(otherEntity)) continue;
			double idleDistance = Math.hypot(otherEntity.getX() - candidateX, otherEntity.getZ() - candidateZ);
			if (idleDistance < MIN_IDLE_VERDANT_SEPARATION) {
				double overlapRatio = 1.0 - idleDistance / MIN_IDLE_VERDANT_SEPARATION;
				hiveFitness -= 50000.0 + overlapRatio * 50000.0;
			}
		}
		return hiveFitness;
	}

	private static void reserveHiveDestination(ServerLevel level, Entity entity, double targetX, double targetZ) {
		VerdantHiveState hiveState = VERDANT_HIVES.get(level.dimension().location());
		if (hiveState == null) return;
		VerdantHiveMember hiveMember = hiveState.members.get(entity.getUUID());
		if (hiveMember == null) return;
		hiveMember.reservedX = targetX;
		hiveMember.reservedZ = targetZ;
		hiveMember.claimedRegion = hiveRegionKey(targetX, targetZ);
		hiveMember.relocating = true;
		hiveMember.lastSeenTick = level.getGameTime();
	}

	private static void removeVerdantFromHive(ServerLevel level, UUID entityUuid) {
		GRAVITY_FIELD_CACHES.remove(entityUuid);
		VerdantHiveState hiveState = VERDANT_HIVES.get(level.dimension().location());
		if (hiveState == null) return;
		hiveState.members.remove(entityUuid);
		hiveState.loadedHistories.remove(entityUuid);
		if (entityUuid.equals(hiveState.anchorUuid)) hiveState.anchorUuid = null;
	}

	private static void appendHiveCompletedRegion(CompoundTag nbt, long completedRegion) {
		long[] existingRegions = persistentLongArray(nbt, "verdant_hive_completed_regions");
		for (long existingRegion : existingRegions) {
			if (existingRegion == completedRegion) return;
		}
		long[] expandedRegions = java.util.Arrays.copyOf(existingRegions, existingRegions.length + 1);
		expandedRegions[existingRegions.length] = completedRegion;
		nbt.putLongArray("verdant_hive_completed_regions", expandedRegions);
	}

	private static boolean isHiveWorldSufficientlyConverted(ServerLevel level, VerdantHiveState hiveState) {
		long totalWorldRegions = calculateHiveWorldRegionCount(level);
		hiveState.totalWorldRegions = totalWorldRegions;
		if (totalWorldRegions == Long.MAX_VALUE || hiveState.completedRegions.size() < totalWorldRegions) return false;
		long completedInsideBorder = 0L;
		for (long completedRegion : hiveState.completedRegions.keySet()) {
			if (isHiveRegionInsideBorder(level, completedRegion)) completedInsideBorder++;
		}
		return completedInsideBorder >= totalWorldRegions;
	}

	private static void resetHiveFinalSweep(VerdantHiveState hiveState) {
		if (hiveState == null) return;
		hiveState.finalSweepActive = false;
		hiveState.finalSweepCursor = 0;
		hiveState.nextFinalSweepTick = Long.MIN_VALUE;
	}

	private static boolean advanceHiveFinalSweep(ServerLevel level, VerdantHiveState hiveState, long gameTime) {
		if (!isHiveWorldSufficientlyConverted(level, hiveState)) {
			resetHiveFinalSweep(hiveState);
			return false;
		}
		if (!hiveState.finalSweepActive) {
			hiveState.finalSweepActive = true;
			hiveState.finalSweepCursor = 0;
			hiveState.nextFinalSweepTick = gameTime;
		}
		if (gameTime < hiveState.nextFinalSweepTick) return false;

		List<Long> sweepRegions = new ArrayList<>();
		for (long completedRegion : hiveState.completedRegions.keySet()) {
			if (isHiveRegionInsideBorder(level, completedRegion)) sweepRegions.add(completedRegion);
		}
		sweepRegions.sort(Long::compareTo);
		if (hiveState.finalSweepCursor >= sweepRegions.size()) {
			resetHiveFinalSweep(hiveState);
			return true;
		}

		long auditedRegion = sweepRegions.get(hiveState.finalSweepCursor);
		double verifiedSaturation = auditHiveRegionSaturation(level, hiveState, auditedRegion,
			level.getMaxBuildHeight() - 4.0, true);
		if (verifiedSaturation < HIVE_REGION_COMPLETION_THRESHOLD) {
			hiveState.completedRegions.remove(auditedRegion);
			resetHiveFinalSweep(hiveState);
			hiveState.phase = HIVE_PHASE_TERRAFORMING;
			syncHiveCompletedRegionsToMembers(level, hiveState);
			return false;
		}
		hiveState.finalSweepCursor++;
		hiveState.nextFinalSweepTick = gameTime + HIVE_FINAL_SWEEP_INTERVAL_TICKS;
		if (hiveState.finalSweepCursor >= sweepRegions.size()) {
			resetHiveFinalSweep(hiveState);
			return true;
		}
		return false;
	}

	private static boolean markHiveRegionSaturated(ServerLevel level, Entity entity, CompoundTag nbt, double centerX, double centerZ) {
		VerdantHiveState hiveState = VERDANT_HIVES.get(level.dimension().location());
		if (hiveState == null) {
			hiveState = registerVerdantWithHive(level, entity, nbt, entity.getX(), entity.getZ());
		}
		long saturatedRegion = hiveRegionKey(centerX, centerZ);
		double verifiedRegionSaturation = auditHiveRegionSaturation(level, hiveState, saturatedRegion, entity.getY(), true);
		nbt.putDouble("verdant_hive_current_region_saturation", verifiedRegionSaturation);
		if (verifiedRegionSaturation < HIVE_REGION_COMPLETION_THRESHOLD) {
			hiveState.completedRegions.remove(saturatedRegion);
			resetHiveFinalSweep(hiveState);
			hiveState.phase = HIVE_PHASE_TERRAFORMING;
			nbt.putInt("verdant_hive_phase", HIVE_PHASE_TERRAFORMING);
			syncHiveCompletedRegionsToMembers(level, hiveState);
			return false;
		}
		hiveState.completedRegions.put(saturatedRegion, Boolean.TRUE);
		appendHiveCompletedRegion(nbt, saturatedRegion);
		syncHiveCompletedRegionsToMembers(level, hiveState);
		nbt.putInt("verdant_hive_completed_region_count", countCompletedHiveRegionsInsideBorder(level, hiveState));
		hiveState.phase = HIVE_PHASE_SATURATED;
		nbt.putInt("verdant_hive_phase", HIVE_PHASE_SATURATED);
		if (isHiveWorldSufficientlyConverted(level, hiveState)) {
			hiveState.phase = HIVE_PHASE_SATURATED;
			return false;
		}
		hiveState.phase = HIVE_PHASE_TERRAFORMING;
		return false;
	}

	private static void handleHiveDormancy(ServerLevel level, Entity entity, CompoundTag nbt) {
		boolean dormancyWasApplied = persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_hive_dormancy_applied", false);
		if (!dormancyWasApplied) {
			nbt.putDouble("verdant_locked_x", entity.getX());
			nbt.putDouble("verdant_locked_y", entity.getY());
			nbt.putDouble("verdant_locked_z", entity.getZ());
			nbt.putBoolean("verdant_hive_dormant", true);
			nbt.putBoolean("verdant_hive_dormancy_applied", true);
			nbt.putLong("verdant_hive_dormant_world_regions", calculateHiveWorldRegionCount(level));
			nbt.putInt("verdant_hive_phase", HIVE_PHASE_DORMANT);
			nbt.putBoolean("verdant_is_relocating", false);
			nbt.putBoolean("verdant_synced_active", false);
			nbt.remove("verdant_target_x");
			nbt.remove("verdant_target_y");
			nbt.remove("verdant_target_z");
			nbt.remove("verdant_border_relocation_pending");
			nbt.remove("verdant_border_relocation_retry_tick");
			setSyncedIsTerraforming(entity, false);
			setSyncedCurrentlyTerraforming(entity, false);
			setSyncedIsDeath(entity, false);
			ACTIVE_CORES.remove(entity.getUUID());
			SUPERCHARGED_CRATER_WORK.remove(entity.getUUID());
			releaseAllCaughtEntities(level, entity.position(), 256.0);
			try {
				net.mcreator.thebackwoods.EntityTrackerData.untrack(entity);
			} catch (Throwable ignored) {}
		}
		maintainOneShotClosingAnimation(level, entity, nbt, "verdant_hive_closing_animation_end_tick");
		double dormantX = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_locked_x", entity.getX());
		double dormantY = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_locked_y", entity.getY());
		double dormantZ = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_locked_z", entity.getZ());
		double[] safeDormantPosition = clampVerdantInsideWorldBorder(level, entity, dormantX, dormantZ);
		if (Math.abs(safeDormantPosition[0] - dormantX) > 0.001 || Math.abs(safeDormantPosition[1] - dormantZ) > 0.001) {
			dormantX = safeDormantPosition[0];
			dormantZ = safeDormantPosition[1];
			nbt.putDouble("verdant_locked_x", dormantX);
			nbt.putDouble("verdant_locked_z", dormantZ);
		}
		entity.setNoGravity(true);
		entity.fallDistance = 0.0F;
		entity.setDeltaMovement(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
		entity.setPos(dormantX, dormantY, dormantZ);
	}

	private static void wakeVerdantHiveMember(Entity member) {
		if (member == null) return;
		CompoundTag memberNbt = member.getPersistentData();
		memberNbt.putBoolean("verdant_hive_dormant", false);
		memberNbt.putBoolean("verdant_hive_dormancy_applied", false);
		memberNbt.remove("verdant_hive_dormant_world_regions");
		memberNbt.remove("verdant_hive_closing_animation_end_tick");
		memberNbt.putInt("verdant_hive_phase", HIVE_PHASE_CLAIMING);
		memberNbt.putBoolean("verdant_synced_active", false);
		memberNbt.putBoolean("verdant_border_relocation_pending", true);
		memberNbt.remove("verdant_border_relocation_retry_tick");
		setSyncedIsTerraforming(member, false);
		setSyncedCurrentlyTerraforming(member, false);
		setSyncedIsDeath(member, false);
		if (persistentInt(memberNbt, "verdant_environment_state", ENVIRONMENT_STATE_UNCHECKED) == ENVIRONMENT_STATE_DORMANT) {
			setSyncedStopTerra(member, true);
		} else {
			memberNbt.putInt("verdant_state", 1);
			memberNbt.putInt("verdant_charge_ticks", 0);
			setSyncedStopTerra(member, false);
		}
	}

	private static boolean handlePendingBorderRelocation(ServerLevel level, VerdantHiveState hiveState, Entity entity, CompoundTag nbt,
			double currentX, double currentY, double currentZ, long gameTime) {
		if (!persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_border_relocation_pending", false)) return false;
		long retryTick = persistentLong(nbt, "verdant_border_relocation_retry_tick", Long.MIN_VALUE);
		if (retryTick != Long.MIN_VALUE && gameTime < retryTick) {
			entity.setNoGravity(true);
			entity.setDeltaMovement(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
			entity.setPos(currentX, currentY, currentZ);
			setSyncedIsTerraforming(entity, false);
			setSyncedCurrentlyTerraforming(entity, false);
			maintainOneShotClosingAnimation(level, entity, nbt, "verdant_border_closing_animation_end_tick");
			return true;
		}

		double[] preferredDirection = getHivePreferredDirection(hiveState, entity);
		double[] relocationTarget = findBestRelocationTarget(level, entity, nbt, currentX, currentY, currentZ,
			preferredDirection[0], preferredDirection[1]);
		if (relocationTarget != null) {
			long targetRegion = hiveRegionKey(relocationTarget[0], relocationTarget[2]);
			if (hiveState.completedRegions.containsKey(targetRegion)
					|| isHiveRegionClaimedByOther(hiveState, entity.getUUID(), targetRegion)) {
				relocationTarget = null;
			}
		}
		if (relocationTarget == null) {
			nbt.putLong("verdant_border_relocation_retry_tick", gameTime + 40L);
			entity.setNoGravity(true);
			entity.setDeltaMovement(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
			entity.setPos(currentX, currentY, currentZ);
			setSyncedIsTerraforming(entity, false);
			setSyncedCurrentlyTerraforming(entity, false);
			maintainOneShotClosingAnimation(level, entity, nbt, "verdant_border_closing_animation_end_tick");
			return true;
		}

		nbt.putDouble("verdant_target_x", relocationTarget[0]);
		nbt.putDouble("verdant_target_y", relocationTarget[1]);
		nbt.putDouble("verdant_target_z", relocationTarget[2]);
		nbt.putBoolean("verdant_is_relocating", true);
		nbt.remove("verdant_border_relocation_pending");
		nbt.remove("verdant_border_relocation_retry_tick");
		nbt.remove("verdant_border_closing_animation_end_tick");
		nbt.putInt("verdant_relocate_cooldown", 0);
		reserveHiveDestination(level, entity, relocationTarget[0], relocationTarget[2]);
		resetInfectionAndProgress(nbt);
		nbt.putInt("verdant_state", 1);
		nbt.putInt("verdant_charge_ticks", 0);
		nbt.putBoolean("verdant_synced_active", false);
		stopTerraformingFlow(entity, nbt, false);
		setSyncedStopTerra(entity, false);
		return false;
	}

	private static boolean handlePendingProximityRelocation(ServerLevel level, VerdantHiveState hiveState, Entity entity, CompoundTag nbt,
			int state, double currentX, double currentY, double currentZ, long gameTime) {
		if (!persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_proximity_relocation_pending", false)) return false;
		if (persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_is_relocating", false)) {
			nbt.remove("verdant_proximity_relocation_pending");
			nbt.remove("verdant_proximity_relocation_retry_tick");
			nbt.remove("verdant_proximity_relocation_closing");
			nbt.remove("verdant_proximity_relocation_closing_end_tick");
			return false;
		}

		boolean animationSequenceReady = state == 2
			&& getSyncedCurrentlyTerraforming(entity)
			&& persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_start_terra_delay", 0) == 0;
		boolean proximityClosing = persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_proximity_relocation_closing", false);
		if (!animationSequenceReady && !proximityClosing) return false;

		if (proximityClosing) {
			long closingEndTick = persistentLong(nbt, "verdant_proximity_relocation_closing_end_tick", gameTime);
			if (gameTime < closingEndTick) {
				entity.setNoGravity(true);
				entity.setDeltaMovement(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
				entity.setPos(currentX, currentY, currentZ);
				setSyncedIsTerraforming(entity, false);
				setSyncedCurrentlyTerraforming(entity, false);
				setSyncedStopTerra(entity, true);
				return true;
			}

		if (!nbt.contains("verdant_target_x") || !nbt.contains("verdant_target_z")) {
			nbt.remove("verdant_proximity_relocation_closing");
			nbt.remove("verdant_proximity_relocation_closing_end_tick");
			nbt.putLong("verdant_proximity_relocation_retry_tick", gameTime + 20L);
			return false;
		}
		nbt.putBoolean("verdant_is_relocating", true);
		nbt.remove("verdant_proximity_relocation_pending");
		nbt.remove("verdant_proximity_relocation_retry_tick");
		nbt.remove("verdant_proximity_relocation_closing");
		nbt.remove("verdant_proximity_relocation_closing_end_tick");
		nbt.putInt("verdant_relocate_cooldown", 0);
		resetInfectionAndProgress(nbt);
		nbt.putInt("verdant_state", 1);
		nbt.putInt("verdant_charge_ticks", 0);
		nbt.putBoolean("verdant_synced_active", false);
		setSyncedStopTerra(entity, false);
		return false;
		}
		if (queuePendingProximityRelocation(level, hiveState, entity, nbt, currentX, currentY, currentZ, gameTime)) return true;
		return false;
	}

	private static boolean queuePendingProximityRelocation(ServerLevel level, VerdantHiveState hiveState, Entity entity, CompoundTag nbt,
			double currentX, double currentY, double currentZ, long gameTime) {
		long retryTick = persistentLong(nbt, "verdant_proximity_relocation_retry_tick", Long.MIN_VALUE);
		if (retryTick != Long.MIN_VALUE && gameTime < retryTick) return false;
		double[] preferredDirection = getHivePreferredDirection(hiveState, entity);
		double[] relocationTarget = findBestRelocationTarget(level, entity, nbt, currentX, currentY, currentZ,
			preferredDirection[0], preferredDirection[1]);
		if (relocationTarget == null) {
			nbt.putLong("verdant_proximity_relocation_retry_tick", gameTime + 20L);
			return false;
		}
		nbt.putDouble("verdant_target_x", relocationTarget[0]);
		nbt.putDouble("verdant_target_y", relocationTarget[1]);
		nbt.putDouble("verdant_target_z", relocationTarget[2]);
		reserveHiveDestination(level, entity, relocationTarget[0], relocationTarget[2]);
		nbt.remove("verdant_proximity_relocation_retry_tick");
		nbt.putBoolean("verdant_proximity_relocation_closing", true);
		nbt.putLong("verdant_proximity_relocation_closing_end_tick", gameTime + ONE_SHOT_CLOSING_ANIMATION_TICKS);
		nbt.putBoolean("verdant_synced_active", false);
		setSyncedIsTerraforming(entity, false);
		setSyncedCurrentlyTerraforming(entity, false);
		setSyncedStopTerra(entity, true);
		releaseAllCaughtEntities(level, entity.position(), 72.0);
		return true;
	}

	private static boolean reconcileHiveWorldBorder(ServerLevel level, VerdantHiveState hiveState, long gameTime) {
		if (hiveState == null) return false;
		synchronized (hiveState) {
			if (hiveState.lastBorderReconciliationTick != Long.MIN_VALUE
					&& gameTime - hiveState.lastBorderReconciliationTick < 40L) return false;
			hiveState.lastBorderReconciliationTick = gameTime;
			double currentBorderMinX = level.getWorldBorder().getMinX();
			double currentBorderMaxX = level.getWorldBorder().getMaxX();
			double currentBorderMinZ = level.getWorldBorder().getMinZ();
			double currentBorderMaxZ = level.getWorldBorder().getMaxZ();
			boolean borderChanged = Math.abs(currentBorderMinX - hiveState.lastBorderMinX) > 0.5
				|| Math.abs(currentBorderMaxX - hiveState.lastBorderMaxX) > 0.5
				|| Math.abs(currentBorderMinZ - hiveState.lastBorderMinZ) > 0.5
				|| Math.abs(currentBorderMaxZ - hiveState.lastBorderMaxZ) > 0.5;
			if (borderChanged) {
				hiveState.lastBorderMinX = currentBorderMinX;
				hiveState.lastBorderMaxX = currentBorderMaxX;
				hiveState.lastBorderMinZ = currentBorderMinZ;
				hiveState.lastBorderMaxZ = currentBorderMaxZ;
				hiveState.completedRegions.clear();
				hiveState.regionSaturation.clear();
				hiveState.regionAuditTicks.clear();
				hiveState.regionGapX.clear();
				hiveState.regionGapZ.clear();
				resetHiveFinalSweep(hiveState);
				hiveState.dormant = false;
				hiveState.phase = HIVE_PHASE_CLAIMING;
				hiveState.dormantWorldRegions = Long.MAX_VALUE;
				hiveState.totalWorldRegions = calculateHiveWorldRegionCount(level);
				syncHiveCompletedRegionsToMembers(level, hiveState);
				syncHiveBorderSnapshotToMembers(level, hiveState);
				for (UUID memberUuid : hiveState.members.keySet()) wakeVerdantHiveMember(level.getEntity(memberUuid));
				return true;
			}
			long currentWorldRegions = calculateHiveWorldRegionCount(level);
			hiveState.totalWorldRegions = currentWorldRegions;
			boolean allRegionsConverted = isHiveWorldSufficientlyConverted(level, hiveState);
			if (!allRegionsConverted) resetHiveFinalSweep(hiveState);
			boolean sufficientlyConverted = hiveState.dormant
				? allRegionsConverted
				: allRegionsConverted && advanceHiveFinalSweep(level, hiveState, gameTime);

			if (hiveState.dormant) {
				if (sufficientlyConverted) {
					hiveState.dormantWorldRegions = currentWorldRegions;
					for (UUID memberUuid : hiveState.members.keySet()) {
						Entity member = level.getEntity(memberUuid);
						if (member != null) member.getPersistentData().putLong("verdant_hive_dormant_world_regions", currentWorldRegions);
					}
					return false;
				}

				hiveState.dormant = false;
				hiveState.phase = HIVE_PHASE_CLAIMING;
				hiveState.dormantWorldRegions = Long.MAX_VALUE;
				for (UUID memberUuid : hiveState.members.keySet()) {
					wakeVerdantHiveMember(level.getEntity(memberUuid));
				}
				return true;
			}

			if (sufficientlyConverted) {
				hiveState.dormant = true;
				hiveState.phase = HIVE_PHASE_DORMANT;
				hiveState.dormantWorldRegions = currentWorldRegions;
			}
			return false;
		}
	}

	private static int surveyEnvironmentTerrain(ServerLevel level, double centerX, double centerZ) {
		boolean[][] viableGrid = new boolean[ENVIRONMENT_GRID_SIZE][ENVIRONMENT_GRID_SIZE];
		int availableColumns = 0;
		int viableColumns = 0;
		int deepColumns = 0;
		int halfGrid = ENVIRONMENT_GRID_SIZE / 2;
		int minimumY = level.getMinBuildHeight();
		BlockPos.MutableBlockPos surveyPos = new BlockPos.MutableBlockPos();

		for (int gridX = 0; gridX < ENVIRONMENT_GRID_SIZE; gridX++) {
			for (int gridZ = 0; gridZ < ENVIRONMENT_GRID_SIZE; gridZ++) {
				int blockX = Mth.floor(centerX) + (gridX - halfGrid) * ENVIRONMENT_GRID_SPACING;
				int blockZ = Mth.floor(centerZ) + (gridZ - halfGrid) * ENVIRONMENT_GRID_SPACING;
				net.minecraft.world.level.chunk.ChunkAccess surveyChunk = level.getChunkSource().getChunkNow(blockX >> 4, blockZ >> 4);
				if (surveyChunk == null) continue;

				availableColumns++;
				int surfaceY = surveyChunk.getHeight(Heightmap.Types.MOTION_BLOCKING_NO_LEAVES, blockX & 15, blockZ & 15);
				int scanBottom = Math.max(minimumY + 1, surfaceY - ENVIRONMENT_SUPPORT_SCAN_DEPTH);
				int supportBlocks = 0;
				for (int blockY = surfaceY - 1; blockY >= scanBottom; blockY--) {
					surveyPos.set(blockX, blockY, blockZ);
					BlockState supportState = surveyChunk.getBlockState(surveyPos);
					if (!supportState.isAir() && !supportState.getCollisionShape(level, surveyPos).isEmpty()) {
						supportBlocks++;
					}
				}

				if (supportBlocks >= ENVIRONMENT_MIN_SUPPORT_BLOCKS) {
					viableGrid[gridX][gridZ] = true;
					viableColumns++;
				}
				if (supportBlocks >= ENVIRONMENT_DEEP_SUPPORT_BLOCKS) deepColumns++;
			}
		}

		if (availableColumns < ENVIRONMENT_MIN_LOADED_COLUMNS) return ENVIRONMENT_SURVEY_INSUFFICIENT;
		int largestComponent = findLargestViableTerrainComponent(viableGrid);
		double viableRatio = (double) viableColumns / (double) availableColumns;
		boolean hasNormalTerrainMass = viableColumns >= 12 && viableRatio >= 0.22 && largestComponent >= 8;
		boolean hasDeepIslandMass = deepColumns >= 6 && largestComponent >= 5;
		return hasNormalTerrainMass || hasDeepIslandMass ? ENVIRONMENT_SURVEY_VIABLE : ENVIRONMENT_SURVEY_SPARSE;
	}

	private static int findLargestViableTerrainComponent(boolean[][] viableGrid) {
		boolean[][] visitedGrid = new boolean[ENVIRONMENT_GRID_SIZE][ENVIRONMENT_GRID_SIZE];
		int[] queueX = new int[ENVIRONMENT_GRID_SIZE * ENVIRONMENT_GRID_SIZE];
		int[] queueZ = new int[ENVIRONMENT_GRID_SIZE * ENVIRONMENT_GRID_SIZE];
		int largestComponent = 0;
		int[] stepX = {1, -1, 0, 0};
		int[] stepZ = {0, 0, 1, -1};

		for (int startX = 0; startX < ENVIRONMENT_GRID_SIZE; startX++) {
			for (int startZ = 0; startZ < ENVIRONMENT_GRID_SIZE; startZ++) {
				if (!viableGrid[startX][startZ] || visitedGrid[startX][startZ]) continue;
				int queueHead = 0;
				int queueTail = 0;
				int componentSize = 0;
				queueX[queueTail] = startX;
				queueZ[queueTail] = startZ;
				queueTail++;
				visitedGrid[startX][startZ] = true;

				while (queueHead < queueTail) {
					int currentX = queueX[queueHead];
					int currentZ = queueZ[queueHead];
					queueHead++;
					componentSize++;
					for (int directionIndex = 0; directionIndex < 4; directionIndex++) {
						int neighborX = currentX + stepX[directionIndex];
						int neighborZ = currentZ + stepZ[directionIndex];
						if (neighborX < 0 || neighborZ < 0 || neighborX >= ENVIRONMENT_GRID_SIZE || neighborZ >= ENVIRONMENT_GRID_SIZE) continue;
						if (!viableGrid[neighborX][neighborZ] || visitedGrid[neighborX][neighborZ]) continue;
						visitedGrid[neighborX][neighborZ] = true;
						queueX[queueTail] = neighborX;
						queueZ[queueTail] = neighborZ;
						queueTail++;
					}
				}
				largestComponent = Math.max(largestComponent, componentSize);
			}
		}
		return largestComponent;
	}

	private static void holdEnvironmentAssessment(ServerLevel level, Entity entity, CompoundTag nbt, double lockedX, double lockedY, double lockedZ) {
		if (!persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_environment_assessment_applied", false)) {
			nbt.putBoolean("verdant_environment_assessment_applied", true);
			nbt.putBoolean("verdant_synced_active", false);
			nbt.putInt("verdant_start_terra_delay", 0);
			nbt.putInt("verdant_stop_terra_delay", 0);
			nbt.putInt("verdant_clear_stop_terra_ticks", 0);
			setSyncedIsTerraforming(entity, false);
			setSyncedCurrentlyTerraforming(entity, false);

			setSyncedStopTerra(entity, false);
			setSyncedIsDeath(entity, false);
			ACTIVE_CORES.remove(entity.getUUID());
			releaseAllCaughtEntities(level, entity.position(), 256.0);
		}
		entity.setNoGravity(true);
		entity.fallDistance = 0.0F;
		entity.setDeltaMovement(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
		entity.setPos(lockedX, lockedY + Math.sin(level.getGameTime() * 0.04) * 0.20, lockedZ);
	}

	private static void applyEnvironmentDormancy(ServerLevel level, Entity entity, CompoundTag nbt, double lockedX, double lockedY, double lockedZ) {
		if (!persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_environment_dormancy_applied", false)) {
			nbt.putInt("verdant_environment_state", ENVIRONMENT_STATE_DORMANT);
			nbt.putBoolean("verdant_environment_dormancy_applied", true);
			nbt.putBoolean("verdant_environment_assessment_applied", false);
			if (!nbt.contains("verdant_environment_next_survey")) {
				nbt.putLong("verdant_environment_next_survey", level.getGameTime() + ENVIRONMENT_RECHECK_INTERVAL_TICKS);
			}
			nbt.putDouble("verdant_environment_dormant_x", lockedX);
			nbt.putDouble("verdant_environment_dormant_y", lockedY);
			nbt.putDouble("verdant_environment_dormant_z", lockedZ);
			nbt.putBoolean("verdant_is_relocating", false);
			nbt.putBoolean("verdant_synced_active", false);
			nbt.putInt("verdant_start_terra_delay", 0);
			nbt.putInt("verdant_stop_terra_delay", 0);
			nbt.putInt("verdant_clear_stop_terra_ticks", 0);
			nbt.remove("verdant_target_x");
			nbt.remove("verdant_target_y");
			nbt.remove("verdant_target_z");
			nbt.remove("verdant_nav_route_y");
			nbt.remove("verdant_nav_commit_ticks");
			nbt.remove("verdant_nav_blocked_ticks");
			setSyncedIsTerraforming(entity, false);
			setSyncedCurrentlyTerraforming(entity, false);
			setSyncedIsDeath(entity, false);
			ACTIVE_CORES.remove(entity.getUUID());
			releaseAllCaughtEntities(level, entity.position(), 256.0);
			try {
				net.mcreator.thebackwoods.EntityTrackerData.untrack(entity);
			} catch (Throwable ignored) {}
		}
		maintainOneShotClosingAnimation(level, entity, nbt, "verdant_environment_closing_animation_end_tick");

		double dormantX = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_environment_dormant_x", lockedX);
		double dormantY = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_environment_dormant_y", lockedY);
		double dormantZ = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_environment_dormant_z", lockedZ);
		entity.setNoGravity(true);
		entity.fallDistance = 0.0F;
		entity.setDeltaMovement(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
		entity.setPos(dormantX, dormantY, dormantZ);
	}

	private static void resumeFromEnvironmentCheck(Entity entity, CompoundTag nbt) {
		nbt.putInt("verdant_environment_state", ENVIRONMENT_STATE_VIABLE);
		nbt.putBoolean("verdant_environment_dormancy_applied", false);
		nbt.putBoolean("verdant_environment_assessment_applied", false);
		nbt.putInt("verdant_environment_sparse_votes", 0);
		nbt.putInt("verdant_environment_viable_votes", 0);
		nbt.putInt("verdant_environment_wake_votes", 0);
		nbt.remove("verdant_environment_next_survey");
		nbt.remove("verdant_environment_dormant_x");
		nbt.remove("verdant_environment_dormant_y");
		nbt.remove("verdant_environment_dormant_z");
		nbt.remove("verdant_environment_closing_animation_end_tick");
		nbt.putInt("verdant_state", 1);
		nbt.putInt("verdant_charge_ticks", 0);
		nbt.putBoolean("verdant_is_relocating", false);
		nbt.putBoolean("verdant_synced_active", false);
		nbt.putInt("verdant_start_terra_delay", 0);
		nbt.putInt("verdant_stop_terra_delay", 0);
		nbt.putInt("verdant_clear_stop_terra_ticks", 0);
		setSyncedIsTerraforming(entity, false);
		setSyncedCurrentlyTerraforming(entity, false);
		setSyncedStopTerra(entity, false);
		setSyncedIsDeath(entity, false);
	}

	private static boolean handleEnvironmentState(ServerLevel level, Entity entity, CompoundTag nbt, int verdantState, double lockedX, double lockedY, double lockedZ) {
		if (verdantState < 1 || persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_is_relocating", false)) return false;
		long gameTime = level.getGameTime();
		int environmentState = persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_environment_state", ENVIRONMENT_STATE_UNCHECKED);
		if (environmentState == ENVIRONMENT_STATE_VIABLE) return false;

		if (environmentState == ENVIRONMENT_STATE_UNCHECKED) {
			environmentState = ENVIRONMENT_STATE_ASSESSING;
			nbt.putInt("verdant_environment_state", environmentState);
			nbt.putInt("verdant_environment_sparse_votes", 0);
			nbt.putInt("verdant_environment_viable_votes", 0);
			nbt.putInt("verdant_environment_wake_votes", 0);
			nbt.putLong("verdant_environment_next_survey", gameTime + 20L);
		}

		if (environmentState == ENVIRONMENT_STATE_ASSESSING) {
			holdEnvironmentAssessment(level, entity, nbt, lockedX, lockedY, lockedZ);
			long nextSurvey = persistentLong(nbt, "verdant_environment_next_survey", gameTime);
			if (gameTime < nextSurvey) return true;
			int surveyResult = surveyEnvironmentTerrain(level, lockedX, lockedZ);
			if (surveyResult == ENVIRONMENT_SURVEY_INSUFFICIENT) {
				nbt.putLong("verdant_environment_next_survey", gameTime + 40L);
				return true;
			}
			if (surveyResult == ENVIRONMENT_SURVEY_SPARSE) {
				int sparseVotes = persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_environment_sparse_votes", 0) + 1;
				nbt.putInt("verdant_environment_sparse_votes", sparseVotes);
				nbt.putInt("verdant_environment_viable_votes", 0);
				if (sparseVotes >= ENVIRONMENT_SPARSE_CONFIRMATIONS) {
					nbt.putLong("verdant_environment_next_survey", gameTime + ENVIRONMENT_RECHECK_INTERVAL_TICKS);
					applyEnvironmentDormancy(level, entity, nbt, lockedX, lockedY, lockedZ);
					return true;
				}
			} else {
				int viableVotes = persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_environment_viable_votes", 0) + 1;
				nbt.putInt("verdant_environment_viable_votes", viableVotes);
				nbt.putInt("verdant_environment_sparse_votes", 0);
				if (viableVotes >= ENVIRONMENT_VIABLE_CONFIRMATIONS) {
					resumeFromEnvironmentCheck(entity, nbt);
					return true;
				}
			}
			nbt.putLong("verdant_environment_next_survey", gameTime + ENVIRONMENT_ASSESS_INTERVAL_TICKS);
			return true;
		}

		if (environmentState == ENVIRONMENT_STATE_DORMANT) {
			applyEnvironmentDormancy(level, entity, nbt, lockedX, lockedY, lockedZ);
			long nextSurvey = persistentLong(nbt, "verdant_environment_next_survey", gameTime + ENVIRONMENT_RECHECK_INTERVAL_TICKS);
			if (gameTime < nextSurvey) return true;
			int surveyResult = surveyEnvironmentTerrain(level,
				persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_environment_dormant_x", lockedX),
				persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_environment_dormant_z", lockedZ));
			if (surveyResult == ENVIRONMENT_SURVEY_INSUFFICIENT) {
				nbt.putLong("verdant_environment_next_survey", gameTime + ENVIRONMENT_ASSESS_INTERVAL_TICKS);
				return true;
			}
			if (surveyResult == ENVIRONMENT_SURVEY_VIABLE) {
				int wakeVotes = persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_environment_wake_votes", 0) + 1;
				nbt.putInt("verdant_environment_wake_votes", wakeVotes);
				if (wakeVotes >= ENVIRONMENT_WAKE_CONFIRMATIONS) {
					resumeFromEnvironmentCheck(entity, nbt);
					return true;
				}
			} else {
				nbt.putInt("verdant_environment_wake_votes", 0);
			}
			nbt.putLong("verdant_environment_next_survey", gameTime + ENVIRONMENT_RECHECK_INTERVAL_TICKS);
			return true;
		}

		nbt.putInt("verdant_environment_state", ENVIRONMENT_STATE_UNCHECKED);
		nbt.putLong("verdant_environment_next_survey", gameTime + 20L);
		holdEnvironmentAssessment(level, entity, nbt, lockedX, lockedY, lockedZ);
		return true;
	}

	public static void execute() {}
	public static void execute(LevelAccessor world, double x, double y, double z, Entity entity) { execute(null, world, x, y, z, entity); }

	private static void execute(Event event, LevelAccessor world, double x, double y, double z, Entity entity) {
		if (entity == null || !(world instanceof ServerLevel level) || !isVerdantEngine(entity)) return;

		CompoundTag nbt = entity.getPersistentData();
		long gameTime = level.getGameTime();
		if (nbt.getLong("verdant_last_game_tick") == gameTime) return;
		nbt.putLong("verdant_last_game_tick", gameTime);

		cleanseNegativeEffects(entity);

		if (!nbt.getBoolean("verdant_initialized")) {
			setupInitialSpawnData(level, entity, nbt);
		}
		if (!persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_scheduler_v2_migrated", false)) {
			nbt.remove("verdant_radial_work_debt");
			nbt.remove("verdant_burst_work_debt");
			nbt.remove("verdant_biome_work_debt");
			nbt.putBoolean("verdant_scheduler_v2_migrated", true);
		}
		boolean saturationModelMigrationRequired = !persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_saturation_model_v3_migrated", false);
		int saturationMigrationServerIdentity = System.identityHashCode(level.getServer());
		Integer previousSaturationMigrationServer = SATURATION_MODEL_V3_SERVERS.put(level.dimension().location(), saturationMigrationServerIdentity);
		boolean clearHiveSaturationHistory = saturationModelMigrationRequired
			&& (previousSaturationMigrationServer == null || previousSaturationMigrationServer.intValue() != saturationMigrationServerIdentity);
		if (saturationModelMigrationRequired) {
			nbt.remove("verdant_hive_completed_regions");
			nbt.putInt("verdant_hive_completed_region_count", 0);
			nbt.putBoolean("verdant_hive_dormant", false);
			nbt.putBoolean("verdant_hive_dormancy_applied", false);
			nbt.remove("verdant_hive_dormant_world_regions");
			nbt.remove("verdant_hive_closing_animation_ticks");
			nbt.remove("verdant_hive_closing_animation_end_tick");
			nbt.putBoolean("verdant_saturation_model_v3_migrated", true);
		}
		boolean coverageModelMigrationRequired = !persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_hive_coverage_v4_migrated", false);
		int coverageMigrationServerIdentity = System.identityHashCode(level.getServer());
		Integer previousCoverageMigrationServer = SATURATION_MODEL_V4_SERVERS.put(level.dimension().location(), coverageMigrationServerIdentity);
		boolean clearHiveCoverageHistory = coverageModelMigrationRequired
			&& (previousCoverageMigrationServer == null || previousCoverageMigrationServer.intValue() != coverageMigrationServerIdentity);
		if (coverageModelMigrationRequired) {
			nbt.remove("verdant_hive_completed_regions");
			nbt.putInt("verdant_hive_completed_region_count", 0);
			nbt.putBoolean("verdant_hive_dormant", false);
			nbt.putBoolean("verdant_hive_dormancy_applied", false);
			nbt.remove("verdant_hive_dormant_world_regions");
			nbt.remove("verdant_hive_current_region_saturation");
			nbt.remove("verdant_hive_closing_animation_end_tick");
			nbt.putBoolean("verdant_hive_coverage_v4_migrated", true);
		}
		boolean sectorModelMigrationRequired = !persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_hive_sector_v5_migrated", false);
		int sectorMigrationServerIdentity = System.identityHashCode(level.getServer());
		Integer previousSectorMigrationServer = SATURATION_MODEL_V5_SERVERS.put(level.dimension().location(), sectorMigrationServerIdentity);
		boolean clearHiveSectorHistory = sectorModelMigrationRequired
			&& (previousSectorMigrationServer == null || previousSectorMigrationServer.intValue() != sectorMigrationServerIdentity);
		if (sectorModelMigrationRequired) {
			nbt.remove("verdant_hive_completed_regions");
			nbt.putInt("verdant_hive_completed_region_count", 0);
			nbt.putBoolean("verdant_hive_dormant", false);
			nbt.putBoolean("verdant_hive_dormancy_applied", false);
			nbt.remove("verdant_hive_dormant_world_regions");
			nbt.remove("verdant_hive_current_region_saturation");
			nbt.remove("verdant_hive_closing_animation_end_tick");
			nbt.putBoolean("verdant_hive_sector_v5_migrated", true);
		}

		int state = nbt.getInt("verdant_state");
		double tx = nbt.getDouble("verdant_locked_x"), ty = nbt.getDouble("verdant_locked_y"), tz = nbt.getDouble("verdant_locked_z");
		float yaw = nbt.getFloat("verdant_locked_yaw"), pitch = nbt.getFloat("verdant_locked_pitch");

		if (tx == 0.0 && ty == 0.0 && tz == 0.0) {
			tx = entity.getX();
			ty = entity.getY();
			tz = entity.getZ();
		}

		double[] safeLockedPosition = clampVerdantInsideWorldBorder(level, entity, tx, tz);
		if (Math.abs(safeLockedPosition[0] - tx) > 0.001 || Math.abs(safeLockedPosition[1] - tz) > 0.001) {
			tx = safeLockedPosition[0];
			tz = safeLockedPosition[1];
			nbt.putDouble("verdant_locked_x", tx);
			nbt.putDouble("verdant_locked_z", tz);
		}
		double[] safePhysicalPosition = clampVerdantInsideWorldBorder(level, entity, entity.getX(), entity.getZ());
		if (Math.abs(safePhysicalPosition[0] - entity.getX()) > 0.001 || Math.abs(safePhysicalPosition[1] - entity.getZ()) > 0.001) {
			entity.setPos(safePhysicalPosition[0], entity.getY(), safePhysicalPosition[1]);
		}
		if (nbt.contains("verdant_target_x") && nbt.contains("verdant_target_z")) {
			double targetX = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_target_x", tx);
			double targetZ = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_target_z", tz);
			double[] safeTarget = clampVerdantInsideWorldBorder(level, entity, targetX, targetZ);
			if (Math.abs(safeTarget[0] - targetX) > 0.001 || Math.abs(safeTarget[1] - targetZ) > 0.001) {
				nbt.putDouble("verdant_target_x", safeTarget[0]);
				nbt.putDouble("verdant_target_z", safeTarget[1]);
			}
		}

		if (isHomeDimension(level)) {
			double targetHoverY = calculateOptimalVerdantHoverY(level, entity.getX(), entity.getZ(), entity.getY());
			double hoverY = targetHoverY + Math.sin(gameTime * 0.04) * 0.25;
			entity.setNoGravity(true);
			entity.fallDistance = 0.0F;

			double diffY = hoverY - entity.getY();
			double diffX = tx - entity.getX();
			double diffZ = tz - entity.getZ();

			double vy = Mth.clamp(diffY * 0.02, -0.12, 0.12);
			double vx = Mth.clamp(diffX * 0.02, -0.12, 0.12);
			double vz = Mth.clamp(diffZ * 0.02, -0.12, 0.12);
			entity.setDeltaMovement(vx, vy, vz);

			if (persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_terraforming_active", false)) {
				stopTerraformingFlow(entity, nbt, false);
			}
			return;
		}

		boolean isAbsorbingRailgun = persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_railgun_absorbing", false);
		int heatTicks = persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_railgun_heat_ticks", 0);

		if (isAbsorbingRailgun && heatTicks > 0) {
			heatTicks--;
			nbt.putInt("verdant_railgun_heat_ticks", heatTicks);

			if (level instanceof ServerLevel serverLevel) {
				double width = entity.getBbWidth();
				double height = entity.getBbHeight();
				for (int pi = 0; pi < 12; pi++) {
					double px = entity.getX() + (serverLevel.getRandom().nextDouble() - 0.5) * (width * 1.4);
					double py = entity.getY() + (serverLevel.getRandom().nextDouble() * height);
					double pz = entity.getZ() + (serverLevel.getRandom().nextDouble() - 0.5) * (width * 1.4);
					serverLevel.sendParticles(net.minecraft.core.particles.ParticleTypes.FLAME, px, py, pz, 2, 0.05, 0.05, 0.05, 0.02);
					serverLevel.sendParticles(net.minecraft.core.particles.ParticleTypes.LAVA, px, py, pz, 1, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
					serverLevel.sendParticles(net.minecraft.core.particles.ParticleTypes.SMOKE, px, py, pz, 1, 0.02, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01);
				}
				if (heatTicks % 8 == 0) {
					serverLevel.playSound(null, entity.getX(), entity.getY(), entity.getZ(),
						net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvents.FIRE_EXTINGUISH, net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource.HOSTILE,
						1.5F, (float) (0.5 + serverLevel.getRandom().nextDouble() * 0.5));
				}
			}

									if (heatTicks <= 0) {
				nbt.putBoolean("verdant_railgun_absorbing", false);
				nbt.putBoolean("verdant_supercharged_mode", true);

				if (entity instanceof LivingEntity living) {
					try {
						net.mcreator.thebackwoods.AttributeLimitBypass.applyBypass(living, 8000.0D, 450.0D, 80.0D, 1.0D);
						living.setHealth(8000.0F);
					} catch (Throwable ignored) {}
				}

				stopTerraformingFlow(entity, nbt, false);
				setSyncedStopTerra(entity, true);
				nbt.putBoolean("verdant_synced_active", false);
				nbt.putInt("verdant_charge_ticks", 0);
				nbt.putInt("verdant_cycle_ticks", 0);
				releaseAllCaughtEntities(level, entity.position(), 72.0);

				double currentLockedY = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_locked_y", entity.getY());
				double targetHoverY = currentLockedY + 30.0;
				nbt.putDouble("verdant_locked_y", targetHoverY);

				VerdantHiveState localHiveState = VERDANT_HIVES.get(level.dimension().location());
				double[] preferredDir = localHiveState != null ? getHivePreferredDirection(localHiveState, entity) : new double[]{1.0, 0.0};
				double[] relocationTarget = findBestRelocationTarget(level, entity, nbt, entity.getX(), entity.getY(), entity.getZ(),
					preferredDir[0], preferredDir[1]);
				if (relocationTarget == null) {
					relocationTarget = findInitialRelocationTarget(level, entity, nbt, entity.getX(), targetHoverY, entity.getZ(),
						preferredDir[0], preferredDir[1]);
				}

				if (relocationTarget != null) {
					nbt.putDouble("verdant_target_x", relocationTarget[0]);
					nbt.putDouble("verdant_target_y", relocationTarget[1] + 30.0);
					nbt.putDouble("verdant_target_z", relocationTarget[2]);
					nbt.putBoolean("verdant_is_relocating", true);
					nbt.putInt("verdant_state", 1);
					if (localHiveState != null) {
						reserveHiveDestination(level, entity, relocationTarget[0], relocationTarget[2]);
					}
				} else {
					nbt.putInt("verdant_state", 1);
				}

				if (level instanceof ServerLevel serverLevel) {
					serverLevel.playSound(null, entity.getX(), entity.getY(), entity.getZ(),
						net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvents.ENDER_DRAGON_GROWL, net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource.HOSTILE,
						5.0F, 0.5F);
					serverLevel.playSound(null, entity.getX(), entity.getY(), entity.getZ(),
						net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvents.GENERIC_EXPLODE.value(), net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource.HOSTILE,
						5.0F, 0.2F);
					for (int bi = 0; bi < 40; bi++) {
						double bx = entity.getX() + (serverLevel.getRandom().nextDouble() - 0.5) * 10.0;
						double by = entity.getY() + serverLevel.getRandom().nextDouble() * 8.0;
						double bz = entity.getZ() + (serverLevel.getRandom().nextDouble() - 0.5) * 10.0;
						serverLevel.sendParticles(net.minecraft.core.particles.ParticleTypes.EXPLOSION_EMITTER, bx, by, bz, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		boolean isDying = persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_death_sequence_active", false);
		if (isDying) {
			handleDeathSequence(level, entity, nbt, tx, ty, tz, yaw, pitch);
			return;
		}

		VerdantHiveState hiveState = registerVerdantWithHive(level, entity, nbt, tx, tz);
		if (clearHiveSaturationHistory) {
			synchronized (hiveState) {
				hiveState.completedRegions.clear();
				hiveState.dormant = false;
				hiveState.phase = HIVE_PHASE_CLAIMING;
				hiveState.dormantWorldRegions = Long.MAX_VALUE;
			}
			syncHiveCompletedRegions(nbt, hiveState);
			setSyncedStopTerra(entity, false);
		}
		if (clearHiveCoverageHistory || clearHiveSectorHistory) {
			synchronized (hiveState) {
				hiveState.completedRegions.clear();
				hiveState.regionSaturation.clear();
				hiveState.regionAuditTicks.clear();
				hiveState.regionGapX.clear();
				hiveState.regionGapZ.clear();
				resetHiveFinalSweep(hiveState);
				hiveState.dormant = false;
				hiveState.phase = HIVE_PHASE_CLAIMING;
				hiveState.dormantWorldRegions = Long.MAX_VALUE;
			}
			syncHiveCompletedRegionsToMembers(level, hiveState);
			setSyncedStopTerra(entity, false);
		}
		if (reconcileHiveWorldBorder(level, hiveState, gameTime)) return;
		if (hiveState.dormant || persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_hive_dormant", false)) {
			handleHiveDormancy(level, entity, nbt);
			return;
		}

		int environmentState = persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_environment_state", ENVIRONMENT_STATE_UNCHECKED);
		if (state >= 1 && environmentState == ENVIRONMENT_STATE_DORMANT
				&& handleEnvironmentState(level, entity, nbt, state, tx, ty, tz)) {
			return;
		}

		if ((gameTime + entity.getId()) % 40 == 0) {
			ensureLongDistanceTracking(level, entity);
		}

		if (state >= 1 && handleEnvironmentState(level, entity, nbt, state, tx, ty, tz)) return;

		if (handlePendingBorderRelocation(level, hiveState, entity, nbt, tx, ty, tz, gameTime)) return;
		if (handlePendingProximityRelocation(level, hiveState, entity, nbt, state, tx, ty, tz, gameTime)) return;

		if (state >= 1) {
			handleVerdantMusic(level, entity, nbt, tx, ty, tz);

			int stopTerraDelay = persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_stop_terra_delay", 0);
			if (stopTerraDelay > 0) {
				stopTerraDelay--;
				nbt.putInt("verdant_stop_terra_delay", stopTerraDelay);
				if (stopTerraDelay == 0) {
					setSyncedStopTerra(entity, true);
