					nbt.putInt("verdant_clear_stop_terra_ticks", 70);
				}
			}

			int clearStopTerra = persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_clear_stop_terra_ticks", 0);
			if (clearStopTerra > 0) {
				clearStopTerra--;
				nbt.putInt("verdant_clear_stop_terra_ticks", clearStopTerra);
				if (clearStopTerra == 0) {
					setSyncedStopTerra(entity, false);
					setSyncedIsDeath(entity, false);
				}
			}

			int startTerraDelay = persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_start_terra_delay", 0);
			if (startTerraDelay > 0) {
				startTerraDelay--;
				nbt.putInt("verdant_start_terra_delay", startTerraDelay);
				if (startTerraDelay == 0) {
					setSyncedIsTerraforming(entity, false);
					setSyncedCurrentlyTerraforming(entity, true);
					setSyncedStopTerra(entity, false);
					setSyncedIsDeath(entity, false);
				}
			}

			int relocateCooldown = persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_relocate_cooldown", 0);
			if (relocateCooldown > 0) {
				nbt.putInt("verdant_relocate_cooldown", relocateCooldown - 1);
			}

			boolean isRelocating = persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_is_relocating", false);

			double schedulingRadius = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_infection_radius", 0.0);
			double satThresholdForScheduling = getInfectionSaturationThreshold(nbt);
			double lastMeasuredSaturation = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_real_block_saturation", 0.0);
			int saturationCheckInterval = schedulingRadius >= getMaxInfectionRadius(nbt) * 0.85
				|| lastMeasuredSaturation >= satThresholdForScheduling - 0.03 ? 10 : 40;
			boolean runSaturationCheck = (gameTime + getHiveWorkOffset(entity)) % saturationCheckInterval == 0;
			boolean runProximityCheck = (gameTime + entity.getId()) % 10L == 0L;
			boolean voidRelocationPending = persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_void_relocation_pending", false);
			boolean runVoidCheck = !voidRelocationPending && (gameTime + entity.getId()) % 10L == 0L;
			boolean runVoidRelocationRetry = voidRelocationPending && (gameTime + entity.getId()) % 20L == 0L;
			if (!isRelocating && (runSaturationCheck || runProximityCheck || runVoidCheck || runVoidRelocationRetry)) {
				double searchR = 384.0;
				AABB searchBox = new AABB(tx - searchR, level.getMinBuildHeight(), tz - searchR,
					tx + searchR, level.getMaxBuildHeight(), tz + searchR);
				List<Entity> neighbors = level.getEntities(entity, searchBox, e -> isVerdantEngine(e) && e.isAlive() && e != entity);

				boolean shouldRelocate = runVoidRelocationRetry;
				boolean forcedProximityRelocation = false;
				double preferredDx = 0.0, preferredDz = 0.0;
				double[] hiveDirection = getHivePreferredDirection(hiveState, entity);

				if (!neighbors.isEmpty()) {
					double avgDx = 0.0, avgDz = 0.0;
					int count = 0;
					for (Entity other : neighbors) {
						double neighborDx = entity.getX() - other.getX();
						double neighborDz = entity.getZ() - other.getZ();
						double distSq = neighborDx * neighborDx + neighborDz * neighborDz;
						if (distSq < FORCED_PROXIMITY_RELOCATION_DISTANCE * FORCED_PROXIMITY_RELOCATION_DISTANCE
								&& entity.getId() > other.getId()) {
							forcedProximityRelocation = true;
						}
						if (relocateCooldown > 0) continue;
						if (distSq < 224.0 * 224.0) {

							if (entity.getId() < other.getId()) continue;
							if (distSq > 0.01) {
								avgDx += (entity.getX() - other.getX());
								avgDz += (entity.getZ() - other.getZ());
							} else {

								double separationAngle = deterministicSeparationAngle(entity);
								avgDx += Math.cos(separationAngle);
								avgDz += Math.sin(separationAngle);
							}
							count++;
						}
					}
					if (count > 0) {
						double len = Math.sqrt(avgDx * avgDx + avgDz * avgDz);
						if (len < 0.001) { avgDx = 1.0; avgDz = 0.0; len = 1.0; }
						preferredDx = avgDx / len;
						preferredDz = avgDz / len;
					}
				}
				boolean proximityRelocationPending = persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_proximity_relocation_pending", false);
				if (forcedProximityRelocation) {
					nbt.putBoolean("verdant_proximity_relocation_pending", true);
					proximityRelocationPending = true;
				}
				if (preferredDx == 0.0 && preferredDz == 0.0) {
					preferredDx = hiveDirection[0];
					preferredDz = hiveDirection[1];
				}

				if (!shouldRelocate && !proximityRelocationPending && runSaturationCheck) {
					int totalSamples = 0;
					int infectedCount = 0;
					List<BlockPos> failedSaturationColumns = new ArrayList<>();
					double curInfR = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_infection_radius", 0.0);
					double sampleR = Math.max(4.0, curInfR);

					int centerSampleX = Mth.floor(tx);
					int centerSampleZ = Mth.floor(tz);
					if (isHiveSaturationSampleInsideBorder(level, centerSampleX, centerSampleZ)) {
						totalSamples++;
						if (isColumnDeeplyInfected(level, centerSampleX, centerSampleZ, ty)) infectedCount++;
						else failedSaturationColumns.add(new BlockPos(centerSampleX, 0, centerSampleZ));
					}

					double r1 = sampleR * 0.25;
					for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
						double angle = (Math.PI * 2.0 / 10.0) * i;
						int sx = Mth.floor(tx + fastCos(angle) * r1);
						int sz = Mth.floor(tz + fastSin(angle) * r1);
						if (isHiveSaturationSampleInsideBorder(level, sx, sz)) {
							totalSamples++;
							if (isColumnDeeplyInfected(level, sx, sz, ty)) infectedCount++;
							else failedSaturationColumns.add(new BlockPos(sx, 0, sz));
						}
					}

					double r2 = sampleR * 0.50;
					for (int i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
						double angle = (Math.PI * 2.0 / 20.0) * i + 0.2;
						int sx = Mth.floor(tx + fastCos(angle) * r2);
						int sz = Mth.floor(tz + fastSin(angle) * r2);
						if (isHiveSaturationSampleInsideBorder(level, sx, sz)) {
							totalSamples++;
							if (isColumnDeeplyInfected(level, sx, sz, ty)) infectedCount++;
							else failedSaturationColumns.add(new BlockPos(sx, 0, sz));
						}
					}

					double r3 = sampleR * 0.75;
					for (int i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
						double angle = (Math.PI * 2.0 / 30.0) * i;
						int sx = Mth.floor(tx + fastCos(angle) * r3);
						int sz = Mth.floor(tz + fastSin(angle) * r3);
						if (isHiveSaturationSampleInsideBorder(level, sx, sz)) {
							totalSamples++;
							if (isColumnDeeplyInfected(level, sx, sz, ty)) infectedCount++;
							else failedSaturationColumns.add(new BlockPos(sx, 0, sz));
						}
					}

					double r4 = sampleR * 0.95;
					for (int i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
						double angle = (Math.PI * 2.0 / 40.0) * i + 0.35;
						int sx = Mth.floor(tx + fastCos(angle) * r4);
						int sz = Mth.floor(tz + fastSin(angle) * r4);
						if (isHiveSaturationSampleInsideBorder(level, sx, sz)) {
							totalSamples++;
							if (isColumnDeeplyInfected(level, sx, sz, ty)) infectedCount++;
							else failedSaturationColumns.add(new BlockPos(sx, 0, sz));
						}
					}

					if (curInfR >= getMaxInfectionRadius(nbt) * 0.90 && !failedSaturationColumns.isEmpty()) {
						double radiusFraction = Math.max(0.001, Math.min(1.0, curInfR / getMaxInfectionRadius(nbt)));
						double requiredDensity = Math.min(1.0, satThresholdForScheduling / radiusFraction);
						int requiredInfectedSamples = (int) Math.ceil(requiredDensity * totalSamples - 1.0E-9);
						int repairsNeededForTarget = Math.max(0, requiredInfectedSamples - infectedCount);
						int repairCount = Math.min(COMPLETION_REPAIR_COLUMNS_PER_CHECK,
							Math.min(failedSaturationColumns.size(), repairsNeededForTarget));
						int repairOffset = Math.floorMod(entity.getUUID().hashCode() + (int) (gameTime / saturationCheckInterval), failedSaturationColumns.size());
						for (int repairIndex = 0; repairIndex < repairCount; repairIndex++) {
							BlockPos failedColumn = failedSaturationColumns.get((repairOffset + repairIndex) % failedSaturationColumns.size());
							double repairGroundY = findGroundBelow(level, failedColumn.getX(), failedColumn.getZ(), ty);
							double repairScanTop = isCeiledDimension(level)
								? Math.min(level.getMaxBuildHeight() - 4.0, repairGroundY + 15.0)
								: level.getMaxBuildHeight() - 4.0;
							infectRod3DBlock(level, failedColumn.getX(), failedColumn.getZ(), repairScanTop, repairGroundY, curInfR, true, false, false);
							if (isColumnDeeplyInfected(level, failedColumn.getX(), failedColumn.getZ(), ty)) infectedCount++;
						}
						nbt.putInt("verdant_completion_repairs_last", repairCount);
					}

					double density = totalSamples > 0 ? (double) infectedCount / (double) totalSamples : 0.0;
					nbt.putDouble("verdant_last_sample_density", density);
					double saturationRatio = Math.min(1.0, (curInfR / getMaxInfectionRadius(nbt)) * density);
					nbt.putDouble("verdant_real_block_saturation", saturationRatio);
					nbt.putLong("verdant_saturation_probe_tick", gameTime);
					if (curInfR >= getMaxInfectionRadius(nbt) * 0.90 && (gameTime + getHiveWorkOffset(entity)) % HIVE_COVERAGE_CACHE_TICKS == 0L) {
						long currentHiveRegion = hiveRegionKey(tx, tz);
						double currentRegionSaturation = auditHiveRegionSaturation(level, hiveState, currentHiveRegion, ty, false);
						nbt.putDouble("verdant_hive_current_region_saturation", currentRegionSaturation);
					}
					double satThreshold = satThresholdForScheduling;

					if (saturationRatio >= satThreshold) {
						int currentDwell = persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_saturation_dwell_ticks", 0) + saturationCheckInterval;
						nbt.putInt("verdant_saturation_dwell_ticks", currentDwell);
						int requiredDwell = getSaturationRelocateDelayTicks(nbt);
						if (currentDwell >= requiredDwell) {
							if (markHiveRegionSaturated(level, entity, nbt, tx, tz)) {
								handleHiveDormancy(level, entity, nbt);
								return;
							}
							shouldRelocate = true;
						}
					} else {
						nbt.putInt("verdant_saturation_dwell_ticks", 0);
					}

				}

				if (runVoidCheck) {
					double beamGroundY = findGroundBelow(level, tx, tz, level.getMaxBuildHeight() - 10.0);
					boolean isVoidHole = beamGroundY <= level.getMinBuildHeight() + 5.0;
					if (!isVoidHole) {

						double gN = findGroundBelow(level, tx, tz + 4.0, level.getMaxBuildHeight() - 10.0);
						double gS = findGroundBelow(level, tx, tz - 4.0, level.getMaxBuildHeight() - 10.0);
						double gE = findGroundBelow(level, tx + 4.0, tz, level.getMaxBuildHeight() - 10.0);
						double gW = findGroundBelow(level, tx - 4.0, tz, level.getMaxBuildHeight() - 10.0);
						if (gN <= level.getMinBuildHeight() + 5.0 || gS <= level.getMinBuildHeight() + 5.0
								|| gE <= level.getMinBuildHeight() + 5.0 || gW <= level.getMinBuildHeight() + 5.0) {
							isVoidHole = true;
						}
					}
					if (isVoidHole) {
						int voidHoverTicks = persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_void_hover_ticks", 0) + 10;
						nbt.putInt("verdant_void_hover_ticks", voidHoverTicks);
						if (voidHoverTicks >= 30) {
							nbt.putBoolean("verdant_void_relocation_pending", true);
							voidRelocationPending = true;
							shouldRelocate = true;
							nbt.putInt("verdant_void_hover_ticks", 0);
							if (persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_synced_active", false)) {
								stopTerraformingFlow(entity, nbt, true);
								nbt.putBoolean("verdant_synced_active", false);
								playEngineSound(level, tx, ty, tz, "block.beacon.deactivate", 48.0F, 0.85F);
								releaseAllCaughtEntities(level, entity.position(), 72.0);
							}
						}
					} else {
						nbt.putInt("verdant_void_hover_ticks", 0);
					}
				}

				if (shouldRelocate) {
					double[] relocationTarget = findBestRelocationTarget(level, entity, nbt, tx, ty, tz, preferredDx, preferredDz);
					if (relocationTarget != null) {
						boolean wasVoidRelocationPending = persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_void_relocation_pending", false);
						double bestTargetX = relocationTarget[0];
						double bestTargetY = relocationTarget[1];
						double bestTargetZ = relocationTarget[2];

						nbt.putDouble("verdant_target_x", bestTargetX);
						nbt.putDouble("verdant_target_y", bestTargetY);
						nbt.putDouble("verdant_target_z", bestTargetZ);
						nbt.putBoolean("verdant_is_relocating", true);
						nbt.remove("verdant_void_relocation_pending");
						nbt.putInt("verdant_void_hover_ticks", 0);
						reserveHiveDestination(level, entity, bestTargetX, bestTargetZ);
						isRelocating = true;
						resetInfectionAndProgress(nbt);
						nbt.putInt("verdant_state", 1);
						nbt.putInt("verdant_charge_ticks", 0);
						state = 1;

						boolean wasActive = persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_synced_active", false);
						if (wasActive) {
							double moveDist = Math.hypot(bestTargetX - tx, bestTargetZ - tz);
							boolean isFarRelocation = moveDist >= 96.0;
							stopTerraformingFlow(entity, nbt, isFarRelocation);
							nbt.putBoolean("verdant_synced_active", false);
							playEngineSound(level, tx, ty, tz, "block.beacon.deactivate", 48.0F, 0.85F);
						} else if (!wasVoidRelocationPending) {
							stopTerraformingFlow(entity, nbt, false);
							setSyncedStopTerra(entity, false);
						}
						releaseAllCaughtEntities(level, entity.position(), 72.0);
					}
				}
			}

			if (!isRelocating && persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_void_relocation_pending", false)) {
				entity.setNoGravity(true);
				entity.fallDistance = 0.0F;
				entity.setDeltaMovement(0, 0, 0);
				entity.setPos(tx, ty + Math.sin(gameTime * 0.04) * 0.35, tz);
				return;
			}

			if (isRelocating && nbt.contains("verdant_target_x") && nbt.contains("verdant_target_z")) {
				double targetX = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_target_x", tx);
				double targetZ = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_target_z", tz);
				double targetY = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_target_y", ty);
				double[] safeCurrent = clampVerdantInsideWorldBorder(level, entity, tx, tz);
				if (Math.abs(safeCurrent[0] - tx) > 0.001 || Math.abs(safeCurrent[1] - tz) > 0.001) {
					tx = safeCurrent[0];
					tz = safeCurrent[1];
					nbt.putDouble("verdant_locked_x", tx);
					nbt.putDouble("verdant_locked_z", tz);
					entity.setPos(tx, ty, tz);
				}
				double[] safeTarget = clampVerdantInsideWorldBorder(level, entity, targetX, targetZ);
				if (Math.abs(safeTarget[0] - targetX) > 0.001 || Math.abs(safeTarget[1] - targetZ) > 0.001) {
					targetX = safeTarget[0];
					targetZ = safeTarget[1];
					nbt.putDouble("verdant_target_x", targetX);
					nbt.putDouble("verdant_target_z", targetZ);
					reserveHiveDestination(level, entity, targetX, targetZ);
				}
				double dx = targetX - tx;
				double dz = targetZ - tz;
				double dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz);

				if (dist > 0.8) {
					double moveSpeed = Math.min(0.275, Math.max(0.075, dist * 0.025));
					double stepDistance = Math.min(dist, moveSpeed);
					double plannedX = tx + (dx / dist) * stepDistance;
					double plannedZ = tz + (dz / dist) * stepDistance;
					double routeY = chooseRelocationRouteY(level, entity, nbt, tx, ty, tz, targetX, targetY, targetZ);
					boolean routeAvailable = !Double.isNaN(routeY);
					double desiredY = routeAvailable ? routeY : ty;
					double nextY = ty;

					if (Math.abs(desiredY - nextY) > 0.05) {
						boolean isClimbing = desiredY > nextY;
						double maxStep = isClimbing ? NON_TERRAFORMING_ASCEND_SPEED : NON_TERRAFORMING_DESCEND_SPEED;
						double climbStep = Math.min(maxStep, Math.max(0.02, Math.abs(desiredY - nextY) * 0.02));
						nextY += isClimbing ? climbStep : -climbStep;
					}

					double floorProbeY = Math.max(level.getMinBuildHeight() + 2.0, nextY - 0.5);
					double localGroundY = findGroundBelow(level, plannedX, plannedZ, floorProbeY);
					if (nextY < localGroundY + 1.5) nextY = localGroundY + 1.5;

					boolean nextPositionClear = isRelocationPositionClear(level, entity, plannedX, nextY, plannedZ);
					int blockedTicks = persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_nav_blocked_ticks", 0);
					if (!nextPositionClear) {
						blockedTicks++;
						nbt.putInt("verdant_nav_blocked_ticks", blockedTicks);
						if (!routeAvailable) nbt.putInt("verdant_nav_commit_ticks", 0);

						if (!routeAvailable && blockedTicks >= NAVIGATION_BLOCK_CONFIRM_TICKS) {
							int destroyedBlocks = destroySoftNavigationObstacles(level, entity, plannedX, nextY, plannedZ);
							if (destroyedBlocks > 0) {
								nextPositionClear = isRelocationPositionClear(level, entity, plannedX, nextY, plannedZ);
							}
						}

						if (!nextPositionClear) {
							double normDx = dx / dist;
							double normDz = dz / dist;
							double perpLx = -normDz;
							double perpLz = normDx;
							boolean preferredSideLeft = (entity.getId() % 2 == 0);
							for (double tangentStep = 1.0; tangentStep <= 8.0; tangentStep += 1.0) {
								double sideOffset = Math.max(10.0, stepDistance) * tangentStep;
								double candLx = tx + perpLx * sideOffset;
								double candLz = tz + perpLz * sideOffset;
								double candRx = tx - perpLx * sideOffset;
								double candRz = tz - perpLz * sideOffset;
								double firstX = preferredSideLeft ? candLx : candRx;
								double firstZ = preferredSideLeft ? candLz : candRz;
								double secondX = preferredSideLeft ? candRx : candLx;
								double secondZ = preferredSideLeft ? candRz : candLz;

								if (isRelocationPositionClear(level, entity, firstX, nextY, firstZ)) {
									plannedX = firstX;
									plannedZ = firstZ;
									nextPositionClear = true;
									break;
								} else if (isRelocationPositionClear(level, entity, secondX, nextY, secondZ)) {
									plannedX = secondX;
									plannedZ = secondZ;
									nextPositionClear = true;
									break;
								}
							}
						}

						if (!nextPositionClear && isRelocationPositionClear(level, entity, tx, nextY, tz)) {
							ty = nextY;
						}
					}

					if (nextPositionClear) {
						tx = plannedX;
						tz = plannedZ;
						ty = nextY;
						nbt.putInt("verdant_nav_blocked_ticks", 0);
					}

					nbt.putDouble("verdant_locked_x", tx);
					nbt.putDouble("verdant_locked_y", ty);
					nbt.putDouble("verdant_locked_z", tz);

					entity.setDeltaMovement(0, 0, 0);

					entity.setPos(tx, ty + Math.sin(gameTime * 0.04) * 0.35, tz);

					if ((gameTime + entity.getId()) % 20 == 0) {
						try {
							net.mcreator.thebackwoods.EntityTrackerData.updatePosition(entity, net.mcreator.thebackwoods.EntityTrackerData.LOADER_TICKET_RADIUS);
						} catch (Throwable ignored) {}
					}
					return;
				} else {

					nbt.putBoolean("verdant_is_relocating", false);
					nbt.putInt("verdant_relocate_cooldown", 1200);
					double groundY = findHighestGroundInRadius(level, tx, tz, level.getMaxBuildHeight() - 10.0, 5);
					nbt.putDouble("verdant_ground_y", groundY);

					try {
						net.mcreator.thebackwoods.EntityTrackerData.track(entity, net.mcreator.thebackwoods.EntityTrackerData.LOADER_TICKET_RADIUS, "verdant_engine");
					} catch (Throwable ignored) {}

					resetInfectionAndProgress(nbt);

					nbt.putInt("verdant_state", 1);
					nbt.putInt("verdant_charge_ticks", 0);
					state = 1;

					startTerraformingFlow(entity, nbt);
					nbt.putBoolean("verdant_synced_active", true);
					playEngineSound(level, tx, ty, tz, "block.beacon.activate", 48.0F, 1.0F);

					entity.setPos(tx, ty + Math.sin(gameTime * 0.04) * 0.35, tz);
					return;
				}
			}
		}

		Vec3 core = new Vec3(tx, entity.getY() + entity.getBbHeight() * 0.5 + BEAM_START_Y_OFFSET, tz);
		handleBoilingCoreHeatAndWaterBubbles(level, entity, core);

		if (state == 0) {
			handleAscensionPhase(level, entity, nbt, tx, ty, tz, yaw, pitch);
			return;
		}

		double hoverY = ty + Math.sin(gameTime * 0.04) * 0.35;
		entity.setNoGravity(true);
		entity.fallDistance = 0.0F;
		entity.setYRot(yaw);
		entity.setXRot(pitch);
		entity.setYHeadRot(yaw);
		entity.setYBodyRot(yaw);

		double diffY = hoverY - entity.getY();
		double diffX = tx - entity.getX();
		double diffZ = tz - entity.getZ();

		double vy = Mth.clamp(diffY * 0.02, -0.12, 0.12);
		double vx = Mth.clamp(diffX * 0.02, -0.12, 0.12);
		double vz = Mth.clamp(diffZ * 0.02, -0.12, 0.12);
		entity.setDeltaMovement(vx, vy, vz);

		core = new Vec3(tx, entity.getY() + entity.getBbHeight() * 0.5 + BEAM_START_Y_OFFSET, tz);
		double groundY = nbt.getDouble("verdant_ground_y");
		if (groundY <= level.getMinBuildHeight() || gameTime % 20 == 0) {
			groundY = findGroundBelow(level, core.x, core.z, core.y);
			nbt.putDouble("verdant_ground_y", groundY);
		}

		if (state == 1) {
			int charge = nbt.getInt("verdant_charge_ticks") + 1;
			nbt.putInt("verdant_charge_ticks", charge);
			if (!nbt.getBoolean("verdant_synced_active")) {
				startTerraformingFlow(entity, nbt);
				nbt.putBoolean("verdant_synced_active", true);
			}

			double prog = (double) charge / CHARGE_DURATION_TICKS;
			double expandMargin = 0.8 + prog * 14.2;
			if (charge % 4 == 0) {
				carveOpenedBodyEncroachment(level, entity, expandMargin);
			}

			double beamQuality = getBeamParticleQuality(level, core.x, core.z, nbt);
			spawnChargingCorona(level, core, prog, beamQuality);
			if (charge % 15 == 1) {
				playEngineSound(level, core.x, core.y, core.z, "block.beacon.ambient", 48.0F + (float) prog * 32.0F, 0.35F + (float) prog * 0.30F);
			}
			if (charge >= CHARGE_DURATION_TICKS) {
				nbt.putInt("verdant_state", 2);
				nbt.putInt("verdant_cycle_ticks", 0);
				playEngineSound(level, core.x, core.y, core.z, "block.beacon.activate", 40.0F, 0.40F);
				playEngineSound(level, core.x, core.y, core.z, "block.end_portal.spawn", 48.0F, 1.85F);
				playEngineSound(level, core.x, groundY, core.z, "block.end_portal.spawn", 48.0F, 1.85F);
			}
			handleActiveShockwaves(level, nbt);
			return;
		}

		if (!nbt.getBoolean("verdant_synced_active")) {
			startTerraformingFlow(entity, nbt);
			nbt.putBoolean("verdant_synced_active", true);
		}

		boolean isSuperchargedMode = persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_supercharged_mode", false);
		double cycleFraction = isSuperchargedMode ? persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_supercharged_cycle_fraction", 0.0) : 0.0;
		double accumulatedCycleAdvance = cycleFraction + (isSuperchargedMode ? SUPERCHARGED_CYCLE_SPEED : 1.0);
		int wholeCycleAdvance = Math.max(1, (int) Math.floor(accumulatedCycleAdvance));
		if (isSuperchargedMode) nbt.putDouble("verdant_supercharged_cycle_fraction", accumulatedCycleAdvance - wholeCycleAdvance);
		else nbt.remove("verdant_supercharged_cycle_fraction");
		int cycle = nbt.getInt("verdant_cycle_ticks") + wholeCycleAdvance;
		int pounds = nbt.getInt("verdant_pound_count");
		double curPoundInc = isSuperchargedMode ? SUPERCHARGED_POUND_RADIUS_INCREMENT : POUND_RADIUS_INCREMENT;
		double curPoundCap = isSuperchargedMode ? SUPERCHARGED_MAX_POUND_RADIUS_CAP : MAX_POUND_RADIUS_CAP;
		double gravR = Math.min(curPoundCap, BASE_GRAVITY_RADIUS + (pounds * curPoundInc));
		double poundR = Math.min(curPoundCap, BASE_POUND_RADIUS + (pounds * curPoundInc));
		processPendingSuperchargedCraterWork(level, entity);

		double densityFactor = 0.0;
		int decayTicks = persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_pound_decay_ticks", 0);
		if (cycle >= RINGS_START_TICK && cycle < CYCLE_TOTAL_TICKS) {
			double chargeProg = (double) (cycle - RINGS_START_TICK) / (CYCLE_TOTAL_TICKS - RINGS_START_TICK);
			densityFactor = chargeProg * chargeProg * (3.0 - 2.0 * chargeProg);
		} else if (decayTicks > 0) {
			decayTicks--;
			nbt.putInt("verdant_pound_decay_ticks", decayTicks);
			double decayProg = (double) decayTicks / 50.0;
			densityFactor = decayProg * decayProg * (3.0 - 2.0 * decayProg);
		}

		double beamQuality = getBeamParticleQuality(level, core.x, core.z, nbt);
		renderToweringBeam(level, core, groundY, BEAM_RADIUS, densityFactor, beamQuality);
		carveObstructingCanopy(level, core, groundY, BEAM_RADIUS);
		if (level.getGameTime() % 10 == 0) {
			carveOpenedBodyEncroachment(level, entity, 15.0);
		}

		boolean terraform = isDimensionAllowedForTerraforming(level);
		if (terraform) {
			if (isGravityBeamPenetrationEnabled(nbt)) {
				performSlowKineticTerraforming(level, nbt, core.x, groundY, core.z, BEAM_RADIUS, pounds);
			}
			performContinuousBeamTipInfection(level, nbt, core.x, groundY, core.z, cycle, pounds);
			performContinuousSolidRadialWoodInfection(level, entity, nbt, core.x, groundY, core.z, pounds);
			handleBurstInfectionSpill(level, entity, nbt, core.x, groundY, core.z, pounds);
			double curInfR = Math.min(getMaxInfectionRadius(nbt), persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_infection_radius", BEAM_RADIUS + 2.0));
			net.mcreator.thebackwoods.VerdantEngineTerraforming.processBiomeTerraforming(level, nbt, core.x, groundY, core.z, curInfR);
		}

		handleGravityDistortionAndProjectiles(level, entity, core, groundY, gravR, cycle, terraform && isTerraformingActive(entity));

		if (cycle < RINGS_START_TICK) {
			if (cycle % 35 == 0) playEngineSound(level, core.x, groundY + 4.0, core.z, "block.beacon.ambient", 64.0F, 0.40F);
		} else if (cycle < CYCLE_TOTAL_TICKS) {
			double chargeProg = (double) (cycle - RINGS_START_TICK) / (CYCLE_TOTAL_TICKS - RINGS_START_TICK);
			double easeQuint = Math.pow(chargeProg, 3.2);
			double ringSpeed = 0.008 + 0.075 * easeQuint;
			double ringTravel = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_ring_travel", 0.0) + ringSpeed;
			nbt.putDouble("verdant_ring_travel", ringTravel);

			spawnPreSlamOnionRings(level, core, groundY, BEAM_RADIUS, chargeProg, ringTravel, beamQuality);
			if (cycle % 8 == 0) playEngineSound(level, core.x, groundY + 2.0, core.z, "block.beacon.power_select", 64.0F, 0.38F + (float) chargeProg * 0.25F);
		} else {
			triggerPlanetaryPound(level, entity, core.x, groundY, core.z, poundR, pounds, terraform);
			nbt.putInt("verdant_pound_count", pounds + 1);
			nbt.putInt("verdant_pound_decay_ticks", 50);
			nbt.putDouble("verdant_ring_travel", 0.0);
			cycle = 0;
		}

		nbt.putInt("verdant_cycle_ticks", cycle);
		handleActiveShockwaves(level, nbt);
	}

	@SubscribeEvent
	public static void onEntityTick(EntityTickEvent.Pre event) {
		Entity e = event.getEntity();
		if (e != null && isVerdantEngine(e)) {
			if (e instanceof LivingEntity living) {
				boolean isDying = e.getPersistentData().getBoolean("verdant_death_sequence_active");
				if (isDying) {
					living.hurtTime = 0;
					living.hurtDuration = 0;
					living.deathTime = 0;
					living.setHealth(1.0F);
				}
			}
			execute(event, e.level(), e.getX(), e.getY(), e.getZ(), e);
		}
	}

	private static boolean isShootingStarDamage(DamageSource ds) {
		if (ds == null) return false;
		try {
			if (ds.typeHolder() != null && ds.typeHolder().unwrapKey().isPresent()) {
				String typeId = ds.typeHolder().unwrapKey().get().location().toString().toLowerCase();
				if (typeId.equals("shooting_star_demo:shooting_star") || typeId.equals("shooting_star_demo:shooting_star_hit")) {
					return true;
				}
			}
		} catch (Throwable ignored) {}
		String msgId = ds.getMsgId();
		if (msgId != null) {
			String lower = msgId.toLowerCase();
			return lower.equals("shooting_star_demo:shooting_star") || lower.equals("shooting_star_demo:shooting_star_hit")
				|| lower.equals("shooting_star_demo.shooting_star") || lower.equals("shooting_star_demo.shooting_star_hit")
				|| lower.equals("shooting_star") || lower.equals("shooting_star_hit");
		}
		return false;
	}

	@SubscribeEvent(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST)
	public static void onLivingDamage(LivingDamageEvent.Pre event) {
		Entity entity = event.getEntity();
		if (entity != null && isVerdantEngine(entity) && entity instanceof LivingEntity living) {
			DamageSource ds = event.getSource();
			boolean isKillCmd = ds != null && ds.is(net.minecraft.world.damagesource.DamageTypes.GENERIC_KILL);
			boolean isAlreadyDying = persistentBoolean(entity.getPersistentData(), "verdant_death_sequence_active", false);

			if (isAlreadyDying) {
				event.setNewDamage(0.0F);
				living.setHealth(1.0F);
				living.deathTime = 0;
				living.hurtDuration = 0;
				living.hurtTime = 0;
				living.setInvulnerable(true);
				return;
			}

			if (isKillCmd) {
				event.setNewDamage(0.0F);
				living.setHealth(1.0F);
				living.deathTime = 0;
				living.hurtDuration = 0;
				living.hurtTime = 0;
				living.setInvulnerable(true);
				startDeathSequence(entity.level() instanceof ServerLevel sl ? sl : null, entity, entity.getPersistentData());
				return;
			}

			boolean isSupercharged = persistentBoolean(entity.getPersistentData(), "verdant_supercharged_mode", false);
			boolean isAbsorbing = persistentBoolean(entity.getPersistentData(), "verdant_railgun_absorbing", false);
			boolean isRailgun = isShootingStarDamage(ds);

			if (!isSupercharged && (isRailgun || isAbsorbing)) {
				event.setNewDamage(0.0F);
				living.setHealth(living.getMaxHealth());
				living.deathTime = 0;
				living.hurtDuration = 0;
				living.hurtTime = 0;
				if (!isAbsorbing) {
					entity.getPersistentData().putBoolean("verdant_railgun_absorbing", true);
					entity.getPersistentData().putInt("verdant_railgun_heat_ticks", 160);
				}
				return;
			}

			if (isAbsorbing) {
				event.setNewDamage(0.0F);
				living.setHealth(living.getMaxHealth());
				living.deathTime = 0;
				living.hurtDuration = 0;
				living.hurtTime = 0;
				return;
			}
		}
	}

	@SubscribeEvent(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST)
	public static void onLivingIncomingDamage(LivingIncomingDamageEvent event) {
		Entity entity = event.getEntity();
		if (entity != null && isVerdantEngine(entity) && entity instanceof LivingEntity living) {
			DamageSource ds = event.getSource();
			boolean isKillCmd = ds != null && ds.is(net.minecraft.world.damagesource.DamageTypes.GENERIC_KILL);
			boolean isAlreadyDying = persistentBoolean(entity.getPersistentData(), "verdant_death_sequence_active", false);

			if (isAlreadyDying) {
				event.setAmount(0.0F);
				event.setCanceled(true);
				living.setHealth(1.0F);
				living.deathTime = 0;
				living.hurtDuration = 0;
				living.hurtTime = 0;
				living.setInvulnerable(true);
				return;
			}

			if (isKillCmd) {
				event.setAmount(0.0F);
				event.setCanceled(true);
				living.setHealth(1.0F);
				living.deathTime = 0;
				living.hurtDuration = 0;
				living.hurtTime = 0;
				living.setInvulnerable(true);
				startDeathSequence(entity.level() instanceof ServerLevel sl ? sl : null, entity, entity.getPersistentData());
				return;
			}

			boolean isSupercharged = persistentBoolean(entity.getPersistentData(), "verdant_supercharged_mode", false);
			boolean isAbsorbing = persistentBoolean(entity.getPersistentData(), "verdant_railgun_absorbing", false);
			boolean isRailgun = isShootingStarDamage(ds);

			if (isSupercharged && isRailgun) {
				event.setAmount(0.0F);
				event.setCanceled(true);
				long gameTime = entity.level().getGameTime();
				long lastBoost = entity.getPersistentData().getLong("verdant_last_railgun_hp_boost_time");
				if (gameTime - lastBoost > 15L) {
					entity.getPersistentData().putLong("verdant_last_railgun_hp_boost_time", gameTime);
					var attr = living.getAttribute(net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.Attributes.MAX_HEALTH);
					if (attr != null) {
						attr.setBaseValue(attr.getBaseValue() * 1.80);
					}
				}
				living.setHealth(living.getMaxHealth());
				living.deathTime = 0;
				living.hurtDuration = 0;
				living.hurtTime = 0;
				living.setInvulnerable(true);
				return;
			}

			if (!isSupercharged && (isRailgun || isAbsorbing)) {
				event.setAmount(0.0F);
				event.setCanceled(true);
				living.setHealth(living.getMaxHealth());
				living.deathTime = 0;
				living.hurtDuration = 0;
				living.hurtTime = 0;
				if (!isAbsorbing) {
					entity.getPersistentData().putBoolean("verdant_railgun_absorbing", true);
					entity.getPersistentData().putInt("verdant_railgun_heat_ticks", 160);
				}
				return;
			}

			if (isAbsorbing) {
				event.setAmount(0.0F);
				event.setCanceled(true);
				living.setHealth(living.getMaxHealth());
				living.deathTime = 0;
				living.hurtDuration = 0;
				living.hurtTime = 0;
				return;
			}
		}
	}

	@SubscribeEvent(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST)
	public static void onLivingDeath(LivingDeathEvent event) {
		Entity entity = event.getEntity();
		if (entity != null && isVerdantEngine(entity) && entity instanceof LivingEntity living) {
			DamageSource ds = event.getSource();
			boolean isKillCmd = ds != null && ds.is(net.minecraft.world.damagesource.DamageTypes.GENERIC_KILL);
			boolean isAlreadyDying = persistentBoolean(entity.getPersistentData(), "verdant_death_sequence_active", false);

			if (isAlreadyDying) {
				event.setCanceled(true);
				living.setHealth(1.0F);
				living.deathTime = 0;
				living.hurtDuration = 0;
				living.hurtTime = 0;
				living.setInvulnerable(true);
				return;
			}

			boolean isSupercharged = persistentBoolean(entity.getPersistentData(), "verdant_supercharged_mode", false);
			boolean isAbsorbing = persistentBoolean(entity.getPersistentData(), "verdant_railgun_absorbing", false);
			boolean isRailgun = isShootingStarDamage(ds);

			if (isSupercharged && isRailgun) {
				event.setCanceled(true);
				long gameTime = entity.level().getGameTime();
				long lastBoost = entity.getPersistentData().getLong("verdant_last_railgun_hp_boost_time");
				if (gameTime - lastBoost > 15L) {
					entity.getPersistentData().putLong("verdant_last_railgun_hp_boost_time", gameTime);
					var attr = living.getAttribute(net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.Attributes.MAX_HEALTH);
					if (attr != null) {
						attr.setBaseValue(attr.getBaseValue() * 1.80);
					}
				}
				living.setHealth(living.getMaxHealth());
				living.deathTime = 0;
				living.hurtDuration = 0;
				living.hurtTime = 0;
				living.setInvulnerable(true);
				return;
			}

			if (!isSupercharged && (isRailgun || isAbsorbing)) {
				event.setCanceled(true);
				living.setHealth(living.getMaxHealth());
				living.deathTime = 0;
				living.hurtDuration = 0;
				living.hurtTime = 0;
				if (!isAbsorbing) {
					entity.getPersistentData().putBoolean("verdant_railgun_absorbing", true);
					entity.getPersistentData().putInt("verdant_railgun_heat_ticks", 160);
				}
				return;
			}

			if (isAbsorbing) {
				event.setCanceled(true);
				living.setHealth(living.getMaxHealth());
				living.deathTime = 0;
				living.hurtDuration = 0;
				living.hurtTime = 0;
				return;
			}

			event.setCanceled(true);
			living.setHealth(1.0F);
			living.deathTime = 0;
			living.hurtDuration = 0;
			living.hurtTime = 0;
			living.setInvulnerable(true);
			startDeathSequence(entity.level() instanceof ServerLevel sl ? sl : null, entity, entity.getPersistentData());
		}
	}

	@SubscribeEvent
	public static void onEntityLeaveLevel(EntityLeaveLevelEvent event) {
		Entity entity = event.getEntity();
		if (entity != null && isVerdantEngine(entity) && event.getLevel() instanceof ServerLevel level) {
			SUPERCHARGED_CRATER_WORK.remove(entity.getUUID());
			releaseAllCaughtEntities(level, entity.position(), 256.0);
			if (entity.isRemoved() && (entity.getRemovalReason() == Entity.RemovalReason.KILLED || entity.getRemovalReason() == Entity.RemovalReason.DISCARDED)) {
				removeVerdantFromHive(level, entity.getUUID());
				boolean isDying = persistentBoolean(entity.getPersistentData(), "verdant_death_sequence_active", false);
				if (isDying || getSyncedIsDeath(entity)) {
					ACTIVE_CORES.remove(entity.getUUID());
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public static class ActiveVerdantCore {
		public final java.util.UUID entityUuid;
		public final ResourceLocation dimension;
		public double coreX;
		public double coreZ;
		public double groundY;
		public double scanTop;
		public double infectionRadius;
		public int pounds;
		public long lastUpdateTick;

		public ActiveVerdantCore(java.util.UUID uuid, ResourceLocation dim, double cx, double cz, double gy, double scanTop, double r, int pounds, long tick) {
			this.entityUuid = uuid;
			this.dimension = dim;
			this.coreX = cx;
			this.coreZ = cz;
			this.groundY = gy;
			this.scanTop = scanTop;
			this.infectionRadius = r;
			this.pounds = pounds;
			this.lastUpdateTick = tick;
		}
	}

	private static final java.util.Map<java.util.UUID, ActiveVerdantCore> ACTIVE_CORES = new java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap<>();

	private static final class SuperchargedCraterWork {
		private final double centerX, groundY, centerZ, radius, penetrationSpeed;
		private final int poundCount, radialSteps, totalCandidates;
		private int cursor;
		private final java.util.Set<Long> visitedColumns = new java.util.HashSet<>();

		private SuperchargedCraterWork(double centerX, double groundY, double centerZ, double radius, int poundCount, double penetrationSpeed) {
			this.centerX = centerX;
			this.groundY = groundY;
			this.centerZ = centerZ;
			this.radius = radius;
			this.poundCount = poundCount;
			this.penetrationSpeed = penetrationSpeed;
			this.radialSteps = Math.max(1, (int) Math.floor(radius / 1.2) + 1);
			this.totalCandidates = 64 * radialSteps;
		}
	}

	private static final java.util.Map<UUID, java.util.ArrayDeque<SuperchargedCraterWork>> SUPERCHARGED_CRATER_WORK = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

	private static final class TerraformChunkLease {
		private UUID owner;
		private long expiresAt;

		private TerraformChunkLease(UUID owner, long expiresAt) {
			this.owner = owner;
			this.expiresAt = expiresAt;
		}
	}

	private static final class TerraformWorkCoordinator {
		private final Map<Long, TerraformChunkLease> chunkLeases = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
		private final Map<Long, Long> recentlySampledColumns = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
		private final Map<Long, Long> completedColumns = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
		private long lastCleanupTick = Long.MIN_VALUE;
		private long lastColumnCleanupTick = Long.MIN_VALUE;
	}

	private static final class GravityFieldCache {
		private ResourceLocation dimension;
		private long lastQueryTick = Long.MIN_VALUE;
		private final List<UUID> entityUuids = new ArrayList<>();
	}

	private static final Map<ServerLevel, TerraformWorkCoordinator> TERRAFORM_COORDINATORS =
		java.util.Collections.synchronizedMap(new java.util.WeakHashMap<>());
	private static final Map<UUID, GravityFieldCache> GRAVITY_FIELD_CACHES = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

	private static TerraformWorkCoordinator getTerraformCoordinator(ServerLevel level) {
		synchronized (TERRAFORM_COORDINATORS) {
			return TERRAFORM_COORDINATORS.computeIfAbsent(level, ignored -> new TerraformWorkCoordinator());
		}
	}

	private static boolean claimTerraformColumn(TerraformWorkCoordinator coordinator, long gameTime, ServerLevel level, Entity entity,
			int blockX, int blockZ, int activeCount) {
		if (level == null || entity == null) return false;
		if (coordinator.completedColumns.isEmpty() && activeCount <= 1) return true;
		if (coordinator.lastColumnCleanupTick == Long.MIN_VALUE || gameTime - coordinator.lastColumnCleanupTick >= 100L) {
			coordinator.recentlySampledColumns.entrySet().removeIf(entry -> gameTime - entry.getValue() >= TERRAFORM_COLUMN_COOLDOWN_TICKS);
			coordinator.completedColumns.entrySet().removeIf(entry -> gameTime - entry.getValue() >= TERRAFORM_COMPLETED_COLUMN_COOLDOWN_TICKS);
			coordinator.lastColumnCleanupTick = gameTime;
		}
		long columnKey = (((long) blockX) << 32) | (blockZ & 0xFFFFFFFFL);
		Long completedTick = coordinator.completedColumns.get(columnKey);
		if (completedTick != null && gameTime - completedTick < TERRAFORM_COMPLETED_COLUMN_COOLDOWN_TICKS) return false;
		if (activeCount <= 1) return true;
		Long previousTick = coordinator.recentlySampledColumns.putIfAbsent(columnKey, gameTime);
		if (previousTick != null) {
			if (gameTime - previousTick < TERRAFORM_COLUMN_COOLDOWN_TICKS) return false;
			coordinator.recentlySampledColumns.replace(columnKey, previousTick, gameTime);
		}
		if (coordinator.recentlySampledColumns.size() > TERRAFORM_RECENT_COLUMN_LIMIT) {
			coordinator.recentlySampledColumns.entrySet().removeIf(entry -> gameTime - entry.getValue() >= 20L);
		}
		return true;
	}

	private static void markTerraformColumnComplete(TerraformWorkCoordinator coordinator, long gameTime, ServerLevel level, int blockX, int blockZ) {
		if (level == null) return;
		long columnKey = (((long) blockX) << 32) | (blockZ & 0xFFFFFFFFL);
		coordinator.completedColumns.put(columnKey, gameTime);
		if (coordinator.completedColumns.size() > TERRAFORM_RECENT_COLUMN_LIMIT) {
			long cutoff = gameTime - 40L;
			coordinator.completedColumns.entrySet().removeIf(entry -> entry.getValue() < cutoff);
		}
	}

	private static List<Entity> getCachedGravityFieldEntities(ServerLevel level, Entity engine, AABB queryBox) {
		if (level == null || engine == null) return java.util.Collections.emptyList();
		long gameTime = level.getGameTime();
		GravityFieldCache cache = GRAVITY_FIELD_CACHES.computeIfAbsent(engine.getUUID(), ignored -> new GravityFieldCache());
		boolean dimensionChanged = cache.dimension == null || !cache.dimension.equals(level.dimension().location());
		long elapsed = gameTime - cache.lastQueryTick;
		boolean scheduledRefresh = elapsed >= GRAVITY_FIELD_ENTITY_QUERY_INTERVAL_TICKS
			&& Math.floorMod((int) gameTime + engine.getUUID().hashCode(), (int) GRAVITY_FIELD_ENTITY_QUERY_INTERVAL_TICKS) == 0;
		if (dimensionChanged || cache.lastQueryTick == Long.MIN_VALUE || scheduledRefresh || elapsed >= GRAVITY_FIELD_ENTITY_QUERY_FAILSAFE_TICKS) {
			cache.dimension = level.dimension().location();
			cache.lastQueryTick = gameTime;
			cache.entityUuids.clear();
			for (Entity nearby : level.getEntitiesOfClass(Entity.class, queryBox, e -> e != engine && e.isAlive())) {
				cache.entityUuids.add(nearby.getUUID());
			}
		}
		List<Entity> cachedEntities = new ArrayList<>(cache.entityUuids.size());
		for (UUID entityUuid : cache.entityUuids) {
			Entity cachedEntity = level.getEntity(entityUuid);
			if (cachedEntity != null && cachedEntity.isAlive() && cachedEntity != engine) cachedEntities.add(cachedEntity);
		}
		return cachedEntities;
	}

	private static int getActiveTerraformingEngineCount(ServerLevel level) {
		if (level == null) return 1;
		long gameTime = level.getGameTime();
		ResourceLocation dimension = level.dimension().location();
		int activeCount = 0;
		for (Map.Entry<UUID, ActiveVerdantCore> entry : ACTIVE_CORES.entrySet()) {
			ActiveVerdantCore core = entry.getValue();
			long age = gameTime - core.lastUpdateTick;
			if (age < 0L || age > HIVE_MEMBER_TIMEOUT_TICKS) {
				ACTIVE_CORES.remove(entry.getKey(), core);
				continue;
			}
			Entity activeEntity = level.getEntity(entry.getKey());
			if (age <= 40L && core.dimension.equals(dimension) && activeEntity != null && activeEntity.isAlive()) activeCount++;
		}
		return Math.max(1, activeCount);
	}

	private static int claimTerraformingSampleBudget(CompoundTag nbt, String debtKey,
			int requestedSamples, int globalBudget, int activeCount) {
		if (requestedSamples < 0) requestedSamples = 0;
		nbt.putInt("verdant_active_terraformer_count", activeCount);
		if (activeCount <= 1) {
			int existingDebt = persistentInt(nbt, debtKey, 0);
			if (requestedSamples == 0 && existingDebt > 0) {
				int grantedDebt = Math.min(existingDebt, globalBudget);
				int remainingDebt = existingDebt - grantedDebt;
				if (remainingDebt > 0) nbt.putInt(debtKey, remainingDebt);
				else nbt.remove(debtKey);
				return grantedDebt;
			}
			nbt.remove(debtKey);
			return requestedSamples;
		}
		int maximumDebt = globalBudget * 16;
		int queuedWork = Math.min(maximumDebt, persistentInt(nbt, debtKey, 0) + requestedSamples);
		int fairShare = Math.max(1, (globalBudget + activeCount - 1) / activeCount);
		int grantedWork = Math.min(queuedWork, fairShare);
		int remainingWork = queuedWork - grantedWork;
		if (remainingWork > 0) nbt.putInt(debtKey, remainingWork);
		else nbt.remove(debtKey);
		return grantedWork;
	}

	private static boolean claimTerraformChunk(TerraformWorkCoordinator coordinator, long gameTime, ServerLevel level, Entity entity,
			int bx, int bz, int activeCount) {
		if (level == null || entity == null || activeCount <= 1) return true;
		if (coordinator.lastCleanupTick == Long.MIN_VALUE || gameTime - coordinator.lastCleanupTick >= 200L) {
			coordinator.chunkLeases.entrySet().removeIf(entry -> entry.getValue().expiresAt < gameTime);
			coordinator.lastCleanupTick = gameTime;
		}
		int chunkX = bx >> 4;
		int chunkZ = bz >> 4;
		long chunkKey = (((long) chunkX) << 32) | (chunkZ & 0xFFFFFFFFL);
		TerraformChunkLease lease = coordinator.chunkLeases.get(chunkKey);
		UUID claimantUuid = entity.getUUID();
		if (lease == null) {
			coordinator.chunkLeases.put(chunkKey, new TerraformChunkLease(claimantUuid, gameTime + TERRAFORM_CHUNK_LEASE_TICKS));
			return true;
		}
		if (lease.expiresAt < gameTime || lease.owner.equals(claimantUuid)) {
			lease.owner = claimantUuid;
			lease.expiresAt = gameTime + TERRAFORM_CHUNK_LEASE_TICKS;
			return true;
		}

		ActiveVerdantCore ownerCore = ACTIVE_CORES.get(lease.owner);
		if (ownerCore == null || gameTime - ownerCore.lastUpdateTick > 40L || !ownerCore.dimension.equals(level.dimension().location())) {
			lease.owner = claimantUuid;
			lease.expiresAt = gameTime + TERRAFORM_CHUNK_LEASE_TICKS;
			return true;
		}
		double chunkCenterX = (chunkX << 4) + 8.0;
		double chunkCenterZ = (chunkZ << 4) + 8.0;
		double claimantDistanceSq = entity.distanceToSqr(chunkCenterX, entity.getY(), chunkCenterZ);
		double ownerDx = ownerCore.coreX - chunkCenterX;
		double ownerDz = ownerCore.coreZ - chunkCenterZ;
		double ownerDistanceSq = ownerDx * ownerDx + ownerDz * ownerDz;
		if (claimantDistanceSq + 64.0 < ownerDistanceSq) {
			lease.owner = claimantUuid;
			lease.expiresAt = gameTime + TERRAFORM_CHUNK_LEASE_TICKS;
			return true;
		}
		return false;
	}

	@SubscribeEvent
	public static void onChunkLoad(ChunkEvent.Load event) {

	}

	private static void catchUpInfectChunk(ServerLevel level, LevelChunk chunk, ActiveVerdantCore core) {
		int chunkX = chunk.getPos().x;
		int chunkZ = chunk.getPos().z;
		int minWorldY = (int) level.getMinBuildHeight() + 5;
		int topY = (int) core.scanTop;
		if (topY <= minWorldY) return;

		BlockPos.MutableBlockPos pos = new BlockPos.MutableBlockPos();
		Block petrified = getPetrifiedOakBlock();
		Block splintered = getSplinteredOakBlock();
		Block lignumCaro = getLignumCaroBlock();
		Block falseOak = getFalseOakPlanksBlock();
		long seed = level.getSeed();

		double solidR = Math.max(0.0, core.infectionRadius - 3.0);

		for (int lx = 0; lx < 16; lx++) {
			int bx = (chunkX << 4) + lx;
			for (int lz = 0; lz < 16; lz++) {
				int bz = (chunkZ << 4) + lz;

				double dist = Math.hypot(bx - core.coreX, bz - core.coreZ);
				if (dist > core.infectionRadius) continue;

				boolean isSolidCore = dist <= solidR;
				if (!isSolidCore) {
					double edgeDist = core.infectionRadius - dist;
					double prob = edgeDist / 3.0;
					double dither = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(bx * 0.45 + bz * 0.45 + seed);
					if (Math.random() > prob * (0.65 + 0.35 * dither)) continue;
				}

				boolean isCell = isCellularMembrane(bx, bz, seed);
				int worldTop = chunk.getHeight(Heightmap.Types.WORLD_SURFACE, lx, lz);
				int oceanFloor = chunk.getHeight(Heightmap.Types.OCEAN_FLOOR, lx, lz);
				boolean hasCeiling = isCeiledDimension(level);
				int startY = hasCeiling ? Math.min(topY, (int) core.groundY + 15) : Math.min(topY, worldTop);
				int crustEndY = hasCeiling
					? Math.max(minWorldY, (int) core.groundY - 10)
					: Math.max(minWorldY, Math.min(oceanFloor, startY) - 32);

				if (worldTop > oceanFloor) {
					for (int y = worldTop; y >= oceanFloor; y--) {
						pos.set(bx, y, bz);
						BlockState st = chunk.getBlockState(pos);
						if (st.isAir()) continue;
						replaceBlockWithOakPlague(level, pos, st, false, isCell);
					}
				}

				int currentSecY = -999;
				net.minecraft.world.level.chunk.LevelChunkSection curSection = null;
				for (int y = startY; y >= crustEndY; y--) {
					int secY = y >> 4;
					if (secY != currentSecY) {
						currentSecY = secY;
						int secIndex = chunk.getSectionIndex(y);
						curSection = (secIndex >= 0 && secIndex < chunk.getSections().length) ? chunk.getSections()[secIndex] : null;
					}
					if (curSection == null || curSection.hasOnlyAir()) {
						y = (secY << 4);
						continue;
					}
					pos.set(bx, y, bz);
					BlockState st = curSection.getBlockState(lx, y & 15, lz);
					if (st.isAir()) continue;

					Block b = st.getBlock();
					if (b == Blocks.ANCIENT_DEBRIS || isAncientDebris(st, b)) continue;

					if (isIndestructibleBlock(st, level, pos) && !canInfectIndestructible(null)) continue;

					boolean isAlreadyInfected = isAlreadyInfectedBlock(b);
					if (isAlreadyInfected) continue;

					if (isFoliageOrDebris(st, b)) {
						level.setBlock(pos, Blocks.AIR.defaultBlockState(), INFECTION_BLOCK_FLAGS);
						continue;
					}

					replaceBlockWithOakPlague(level, pos, st, false, isCell);
				}

				if (crustEndY > minWorldY + 8) {
					int caveSamples = isSolidCore ? 8 : 4;
					for (int cs = 0; cs < caveSamples; cs++) {
						int cy = minWorldY + (int) (Math.random() * (crustEndY - minWorldY));
						pos.set(bx, cy, bz);
						BlockState st = chunk.getBlockState(pos);
						Block b = st.getBlock();
						if (b == Blocks.BEDROCK || b == Blocks.BARRIER) continue;

						if (st.isAir()) {
							pos.set(bx, cy - 1, bz);
							st = chunk.getBlockState(pos);
							b = st.getBlock();
							if (st.isAir() || b == Blocks.BEDROCK || b == Blocks.BARRIER) continue;
						}

						boolean isAlreadyInfected = isAlreadyInfectedBlock(b);
						if (isAlreadyInfected) continue;

						if (isFoliageOrDebris(st, b)) {
							level.setBlock(pos, Blocks.AIR.defaultBlockState(), INFECTION_BLOCK_FLAGS);
							continue;
						}

						replaceBlockWithOakPlague(level, pos, st, false, isCell);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		try {
			net.mcreator.thebackwoods.VerdantEngineTerraforming.processBiomeTerraforming(level, null, core.coreX, core.groundY, core.coreZ, core.infectionRadius);
		} catch (Throwable ignored) {}
	}

	private static boolean isDimensionAllowedForTerraforming(ServerLevel level) {
		if (level == null) return false;
		String id = level.dimension().location().toString().toLowerCase();
		return !id.contains("the_backwoods") && !id.contains("backwood");
	}

	private static double findHighestGroundInRadius(ServerLevel level, double cx, double cz, double fromY, int radius) {
		double maxGround = level.getMinBuildHeight() + 4.0;
		for (int dx = -radius; dx <= radius; dx += Math.max(1, radius / 2)) {
			for (int dz = -radius; dz <= radius; dz += Math.max(1, radius / 2)) {
				double gy = findGroundBelow(level, cx + dx, cz + dz, fromY);
				if (gy > maxGround) {
					maxGround = gy;
				}
			}
		}
		return maxGround;
	}

	private static double findCeilingAbove(ServerLevel level, double x, double z, double fromY) {
		if (!level.dimensionType().hasCeiling()) {
			return level.getMaxBuildHeight() + 2.0;
		}
		int floorX = Mth.floor(x);
		int floorZ = Mth.floor(z);
		int startY = Math.max((int) level.getMinBuildHeight() + 2, Mth.floor(fromY));
		int maxY = (int) level.getMaxBuildHeight() - 2;
		double lowestCeiling = maxY + 2.0;
		BlockPos.MutableBlockPos pos = new BlockPos.MutableBlockPos();

		for (int dx = -5; dx <= 5; dx += 2) {
			for (int dz = -5; dz <= 5; dz += 2) {
				for (int y = startY; y < maxY; y++) {
					pos.set(floorX + dx, y, floorZ + dz);
					if (!level.hasChunkAt(pos)) continue;
					BlockState st = level.getBlockState(pos);
					if (st.isAir() || !st.getFluidState().isEmpty() || st.is(BlockTags.LEAVES) ||
						st.is(BlockTags.FLOWERS) || st.is(BlockTags.SAPLINGS) || st.is(BlockTags.CROPS) ||
						st.getBlock() == Blocks.SNOW || st.getBlock() == Blocks.MOSS_CARPET || st.getBlock() == Blocks.VINE) {
						continue;
					}
					if (y < lowestCeiling) {
						lowestCeiling = y;
					}
					break;
				}
			}
		}
		return lowestCeiling;
	}

	private static double calculateOptimalVerdantHoverY(ServerLevel level, double x, double z, double currentY) {
		return calculateOptimalVerdantHoverY(level, x, z, currentY, null);
	}

	private static double calculateOptimalVerdantHoverY(ServerLevel level, double x, double z, double currentY, CompoundTag nbt) {
		double scanStart = Math.min(level.getMaxBuildHeight() - 5.0, Math.max(currentY + 60.0, 180.0));
		double highestGroundY = findHighestGroundInRadius(level, x, z, scanStart, 5);
		double targetY = highestGroundY + getHoverTargetAltitude(nbt);

		if (level.dimensionType().hasCeiling()) {
			double ceilingY = findCeilingAbove(level, x, z, highestGroundY + 3.0);
			if (ceilingY < level.getMaxBuildHeight()) {
				double headroom = 28.0;
				double maxAllowedUnderCeiling = ceilingY - headroom;
				double minAllowedAboveGround = highestGroundY + 12.0;
				if (maxAllowedUnderCeiling > minAllowedAboveGround) {
					targetY = Math.min(targetY, maxAllowedUnderCeiling);
					targetY = Math.max(targetY, minAllowedAboveGround);
				} else {
					targetY = highestGroundY + Math.max(6.0, (ceilingY - highestGroundY) * 0.40);
				}
			}
		}
		return Math.min(level.getMaxBuildHeight() - 12.0, Math.max(level.getMinBuildHeight() + 15.0, targetY));
	}

	private static double deterministicSeparationAngle(Entity entity) {
		long unsignedHash = Integer.toUnsignedLong(entity.getUUID().hashCode());
		return (unsignedHash / 4294967296.0) * Math.PI * 2.0;
	}

	private static double distanceToReservedVerdantPosition(Entity other, double candidateX, double candidateZ) {
		CompoundTag otherNbt = other.getPersistentData();
		double occupiedX = other.getX();
		double occupiedZ = other.getZ();
		if (persistentBoolean(otherNbt, "verdant_is_relocating", false)) {
			occupiedX = persistentDouble(otherNbt, "verdant_target_x", occupiedX);
			occupiedZ = persistentDouble(otherNbt, "verdant_target_z", occupiedZ);
		}
		return Math.hypot(occupiedX - candidateX, occupiedZ - candidateZ);
	}

	private static AABB getRelocationClearanceBox(Entity entity, double testX, double testY, double testZ) {
		return entity.getBoundingBox()
			.move(testX - entity.getX(), testY - entity.getY(), testZ - entity.getZ())
			.inflate(NAVIGATION_HORIZONTAL_MARGIN, NAVIGATION_VERTICAL_MARGIN, NAVIGATION_HORIZONTAL_MARGIN);
	}

	private static boolean isRelocationPositionClear(ServerLevel level, Entity entity, double testX, double testY, double testZ) {
		if (!isInsideVerdantWorldBorder(level, entity, testX, testZ)) return false;
		BlockPos testPos = BlockPos.containing(testX, testY, testZ);
		if (!level.hasChunkAt(testPos)) return false;
		return level.noCollision(entity, getRelocationClearanceBox(entity, testX, testY, testZ));
	}

	private static boolean isRelocationVerticalTransitionClear(ServerLevel level, Entity entity, double currentX, double currentY, double currentZ, double candidateY) {
		double verticalDistance = Math.abs(candidateY - currentY);
		int verticalSamples = Math.max(1, (int) Math.ceil(verticalDistance / 1.5));
		for (int sampleIndex = 1; sampleIndex <= verticalSamples; sampleIndex++) {
			double sampleProgress = (double) sampleIndex / (double) verticalSamples;
			double sampleY = currentY + (candidateY - currentY) * sampleProgress;
			if (!isRelocationPositionClear(level, entity, currentX, sampleY, currentZ)) return false;
		}
		return true;
	}

	private static boolean isRelocationCorridorClear(ServerLevel level, Entity entity, double currentX, double currentZ, double endX, double endZ, double candidateY) {
		double corridorDx = endX - currentX;
		double corridorDz = endZ - currentZ;
		double corridorDistance = Math.hypot(corridorDx, corridorDz);
		int corridorSamples = Math.max(1, (int) Math.ceil(corridorDistance / 1.5));
		for (int sampleIndex = 1; sampleIndex <= corridorSamples; sampleIndex++) {
			double sampleProgress = (double) sampleIndex / (double) corridorSamples;
			double sampleX = currentX + corridorDx * sampleProgress;
			double sampleZ = currentZ + corridorDz * sampleProgress;
			if (!isRelocationPositionClear(level, entity, sampleX, candidateY, sampleZ)) return false;
		}
		return true;
	}

	private static double scoreRelocationRouteCandidate(ServerLevel level, Entity entity, double currentX, double currentY, double currentZ,
			double lookaheadX, double lookaheadZ, double candidateY, double normalHoverY, double targetY, double remainingDistance) {
		double minimumRouteY = level.getMinBuildHeight() + 2.0;
		double maximumRouteY = level.getMaxBuildHeight() - entity.getBbHeight() - 2.0;
		if (candidateY < minimumRouteY || candidateY > maximumRouteY) return Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
		if (!isRelocationVerticalTransitionClear(level, entity, currentX, currentY, currentZ, candidateY)) return Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
		if (isRelocationCorridorClear(level, entity, currentX, currentZ, lookaheadX, lookaheadZ, candidateY)) {
			double routeScore = Math.abs(candidateY - currentY) * 0.65 + Math.abs(candidateY - normalHoverY);
			if (candidateY > currentY) routeScore += 0.35;
			if (remainingDistance < 20.0) {
				double arrivalWeight = (20.0 - remainingDistance) / 20.0;
				routeScore += Math.abs(candidateY - targetY) * arrivalWeight;
			}
			return routeScore;
		}
		double dx = lookaheadX - currentX;
		double dz = lookaheadZ - currentZ;
		double len = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
		if (len > 0.001) {
			double perpLx = -dz / len;
			double perpLz = dx / len;
			for (double sideOffset = 8.0; sideOffset <= 48.0; sideOffset += 8.0) {
				if (isRelocationCorridorClear(level, entity, currentX, currentZ, lookaheadX + perpLx * sideOffset, lookaheadZ + perpLz * sideOffset, candidateY)
						|| isRelocationCorridorClear(level, entity, currentX, currentZ, lookaheadX - perpLx * sideOffset, lookaheadZ - perpLz * sideOffset, candidateY)) {
					double routeScore = Math.abs(candidateY - currentY) * 0.65 + Math.abs(candidateY - normalHoverY) + sideOffset * 0.1;
					if (candidateY > currentY) routeScore += 0.35;
					return routeScore;
				}
			}
		}
		return Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
	}

	private static double chooseRelocationRouteY(ServerLevel level, Entity entity, CompoundTag nbt, double currentX, double currentY, double currentZ,
			double targetX, double targetY, double targetZ) {
		double routeDx = targetX - currentX;
		double routeDz = targetZ - currentZ;
		double remainingDistance = Math.hypot(routeDx, routeDz);
		if (remainingDistance < 0.001) return targetY;

		double lookaheadDistance = Math.min(NAVIGATION_LOOKAHEAD_DISTANCE, remainingDistance);
		double lookaheadX = currentX + routeDx / remainingDistance * lookaheadDistance;
		double lookaheadZ = currentZ + routeDz / remainingDistance * lookaheadDistance;
		int committedTicks = persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_nav_commit_ticks", 0);
		if (committedTicks > 0 && nbt.contains("verdant_nav_route_y")) {
			double committedY = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_nav_route_y", currentY);
			nbt.putInt("verdant_nav_commit_ticks", committedTicks - 1);
			return committedY;
		}

		double groundProbeY = Math.max(level.getMinBuildHeight() + 2.0, Math.min(level.getMaxBuildHeight() - 2.0, currentY - 0.5));
		double terrainGroundY = findGroundBelow(level, lookaheadX, lookaheadZ, groundProbeY);
		double minimumRouteY = level.getMinBuildHeight() + 2.0;
		double maximumRouteY = level.getMaxBuildHeight() - entity.getBbHeight() - 2.0;
		double normalHoverY = Mth.clamp(terrainGroundY + getHoverTargetAltitude(nbt), minimumRouteY, maximumRouteY);
		double bestRouteY = Double.NaN;
		double bestRouteScore = Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY;

		double candidateScore = scoreRelocationRouteCandidate(level, entity, currentX, currentY, currentZ, lookaheadX, lookaheadZ,
			normalHoverY, normalHoverY, targetY, remainingDistance);
		if (candidateScore < bestRouteScore) {
			bestRouteScore = candidateScore;
			bestRouteY = normalHoverY;
		}

		candidateScore = scoreRelocationRouteCandidate(level, entity, currentX, currentY, currentZ, lookaheadX, lookaheadZ,
			currentY, normalHoverY, targetY, remainingDistance);
		if (candidateScore < bestRouteScore) {
			bestRouteScore = candidateScore;
			bestRouteY = currentY;
		}

		candidateScore = scoreRelocationRouteCandidate(level, entity, currentX, currentY, currentZ, lookaheadX, lookaheadZ,
			targetY, normalHoverY, targetY, remainingDistance);
		if (candidateScore < bestRouteScore) {
			bestRouteScore = candidateScore;
			bestRouteY = targetY;
		}

		for (double routeOffset = 4.0; routeOffset <= 96.0; routeOffset += 4.0) {
			double lowerCandidateY = currentY - routeOffset;
			if (lowerCandidateY >= minimumRouteY) {
				candidateScore = scoreRelocationRouteCandidate(level, entity, currentX, currentY, currentZ, lookaheadX, lookaheadZ,
					lowerCandidateY, normalHoverY, targetY, remainingDistance);
				if (candidateScore < bestRouteScore) {
					bestRouteScore = candidateScore;
					bestRouteY = lowerCandidateY;
				}
			}

			double upperCandidateY = currentY + routeOffset;
			if (upperCandidateY <= maximumRouteY) {
				candidateScore = scoreRelocationRouteCandidate(level, entity, currentX, currentY, currentZ, lookaheadX, lookaheadZ,
					upperCandidateY, normalHoverY, targetY, remainingDistance);
				if (candidateScore < bestRouteScore) {
					bestRouteScore = candidateScore;
					bestRouteY = upperCandidateY;
				}
			}
		}

		if (!Double.isNaN(bestRouteY)) {
			nbt.putDouble("verdant_nav_route_y", bestRouteY);
			nbt.putInt("verdant_nav_commit_ticks", NAVIGATION_ROUTE_COMMIT_TICKS);
		}
		return bestRouteY;
	}

	private static int destroySoftNavigationObstacles(ServerLevel level, Entity entity, double testX, double testY, double testZ) {
		AABB clearanceBox = getRelocationClearanceBox(entity, testX, testY, testZ);
		int minimumX = Mth.floor(clearanceBox.minX);
		int maximumX = Mth.floor(clearanceBox.maxX);
		int minimumY = Math.max(level.getMinBuildHeight(), Mth.floor(clearanceBox.minY));
		int maximumY = Math.min(level.getMaxBuildHeight() - 1, Mth.floor(clearanceBox.maxY));
		int minimumZ = Mth.floor(clearanceBox.minZ);
		int maximumZ = Mth.floor(clearanceBox.maxZ);
		int destroyedBlocks = 0;
		BlockPos.MutableBlockPos obstaclePos = new BlockPos.MutableBlockPos();

		for (int obstacleY = minimumY; obstacleY <= maximumY && destroyedBlocks < NAVIGATION_MAX_BLOCKS_BROKEN_PER_TICK; obstacleY++) {
			for (int obstacleX = minimumX; obstacleX <= maximumX && destroyedBlocks < NAVIGATION_MAX_BLOCKS_BROKEN_PER_TICK; obstacleX++) {
				for (int obstacleZ = minimumZ; obstacleZ <= maximumZ && destroyedBlocks < NAVIGATION_MAX_BLOCKS_BROKEN_PER_TICK; obstacleZ++) {
					obstaclePos.set(obstacleX, obstacleY, obstacleZ);
					if (!level.hasChunkAt(obstaclePos)) continue;
					BlockState obstacleState = level.getBlockState(obstaclePos);
					if (obstacleState.isAir() || obstacleState.getCollisionShape(level, obstaclePos).isEmpty()) continue;
					if (isIndestructibleBlock(obstacleState, level, obstaclePos)) continue;
					float obstacleHardness = obstacleState.getDestroySpeed(level, obstaclePos);
					if (obstacleHardness < 0.0F || obstacleHardness >= NAVIGATION_MAX_BREAK_HARDNESS) continue;
					if (level.destroyBlock(obstaclePos, false)) destroyedBlocks++;
				}
			}
		}
		return destroyedBlocks;
	}

	private static double[] findBestRelocationTarget(ServerLevel level, Entity entity, CompoundTag nbt, double currentX, double currentY, double currentZ, double preferredDx, double preferredDz) {
		boolean isVoidEmergency = findGroundBelow(level, currentX, currentZ, level.getMaxBuildHeight() - 10.0) <= level.getMinBuildHeight() + 5.0
			|| persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_void_relocation_pending", false);
		VerdantHiveState hiveState = VERDANT_HIVES.get(level.dimension().location());
		if (!isVoidEmergency && hiveState != null) {
			long currentRegion = hiveRegionKey(currentX, currentZ);
			if (!hiveState.completedRegions.containsKey(currentRegion)) {
				double currentRegionSaturation = auditHiveRegionSaturation(level, hiveState, currentRegion, currentY, false);
				nbt.putDouble("verdant_hive_current_region_saturation", currentRegionSaturation);
				if (currentRegionSaturation < HIVE_REGION_COMPLETION_THRESHOLD) {
					double[] gapTarget = findHiveCoverageGapTarget(level, hiveState, entity, currentRegion,
						currentX, currentY, currentZ, preferredDx, preferredDz);
					if (gapTarget != null) return gapTarget;
				}
			}
		}
		for (int validationAttempt = 0; validationAttempt < RELOCATION_SATURATION_VALIDATION_ATTEMPTS; validationAttempt++) {
			double[] candidate = findBestRelocationTargetUnchecked(level, entity, nbt, currentX, currentY, currentZ, preferredDx, preferredDz);
			if (candidate == null) break;
			if (isVoidEmergency) return candidate;
			double candidateSaturation = sampleRelocationCandidateSaturation(level, candidate[0], candidate[1], candidate[2]);
			nbt.putDouble("verdant_last_candidate_saturation", candidateSaturation);
			if (candidateSaturation < RELOCATION_SATURATION_REJECTION_THRESHOLD) return candidate;
			if (markHiveRegionSaturated(level, entity, nbt, candidate[0], candidate[2])) return null;
			if (hiveState != null) {
				long candidateRegion = hiveRegionKey(candidate[0], candidate[2]);
				if (!hiveState.completedRegions.containsKey(candidateRegion)) {
					double[] gapTarget = findHiveCoverageGapTarget(level, hiveState, entity, candidateRegion,
						currentX, currentY, currentZ, preferredDx, preferredDz);
					if (gapTarget != null) return gapTarget;
				}
			}
		}
		double[] fallbackCandidate = findBestRelocationTargetUnchecked(level, entity, nbt, currentX, currentY, currentZ, preferredDx, preferredDz);
		if (fallbackCandidate != null) return fallbackCandidate;
		return null;
	}

	private static double[] findBestRelocationTargetUnchecked(ServerLevel level, Entity entity, CompoundTag nbt, double currentX, double currentY, double currentZ, double preferredDx, double preferredDz) {
		if (level.dimension() == net.minecraft.world.level.Level.OVERWORLD) {
			return findOverworldRelocationTarget(level, entity, nbt, currentX, currentY, currentZ, preferredDx, preferredDz, true);
		} else if (level.dimension() == net.minecraft.world.level.Level.NETHER) {
			return findNetherRelocationTarget(level, entity, nbt, currentX, currentY, currentZ, preferredDx, preferredDz);
		} else if (level.dimension() == net.minecraft.world.level.Level.END) {
			return findEndRelocationTarget(level, entity, nbt, currentX, currentY, currentZ, preferredDx, preferredDz);
		} else {
			return findGenericDimensionRelocationTarget(level, entity, nbt, currentX, currentY, currentZ, preferredDx, preferredDz);
		}
	}

	private static double[] findInitialRelocationTarget(ServerLevel level, Entity entity, CompoundTag nbt, double currentX, double currentY, double currentZ, double preferredDx, double preferredDz) {
		if (level.dimension() == net.minecraft.world.level.Level.OVERWORLD) {
			return findOverworldRelocationTarget(level, entity, nbt, currentX, currentY, currentZ, preferredDx, preferredDz, false);
		}
		return findBestRelocationTarget(level, entity, nbt, currentX, currentY, currentZ, preferredDx, preferredDz);
	}

	private static double[] getBorderAwareRelocationDistances(ServerLevel level, double[] standardDistances) {
		double borderWidth = level.getWorldBorder().getMaxX() - level.getWorldBorder().getMinX();
		double borderDepth = level.getWorldBorder().getMaxZ() - level.getWorldBorder().getMinZ();
		if (Math.min(borderWidth, borderDepth) >= 768.0) return standardDistances;
		double[] compactDistances = new double[]{64.0, 96.0, 128.0, 160.0, 224.0};
		double[] combinedDistances = java.util.Arrays.copyOf(compactDistances, compactDistances.length + standardDistances.length);
		System.arraycopy(standardDistances, 0, combinedDistances, compactDistances.length, standardDistances.length);
		return combinedDistances;
	}

	private static double getVerdantWorldBorderInset(Entity entity) {
		return WORLD_BORDER_CLEARANCE + entity.getBbWidth() * 0.5 + NAVIGATION_HORIZONTAL_MARGIN;
	}

	private static double[] clampVerdantInsideWorldBorder(ServerLevel level, Entity entity, double targetX, double targetZ) {
		double inset = getVerdantWorldBorderInset(entity);
		double minimumX = level.getWorldBorder().getMinX() + inset;
		double maximumX = level.getWorldBorder().getMaxX() - inset;
		double minimumZ = level.getWorldBorder().getMinZ() + inset;
		double maximumZ = level.getWorldBorder().getMaxZ() - inset;
		if (minimumX > maximumX) minimumX = maximumX = (level.getWorldBorder().getMinX() + level.getWorldBorder().getMaxX()) * 0.5;
		if (minimumZ > maximumZ) minimumZ = maximumZ = (level.getWorldBorder().getMinZ() + level.getWorldBorder().getMaxZ()) * 0.5;
		return new double[]{Math.max(minimumX, Math.min(maximumX, targetX)), Math.max(minimumZ, Math.min(maximumZ, targetZ))};
	}

	private static boolean isInsideVerdantWorldBorder(ServerLevel level, Entity entity, double targetX, double targetZ) {
		double inset = getVerdantWorldBorderInset(entity);
		return targetX >= level.getWorldBorder().getMinX() + inset && targetX <= level.getWorldBorder().getMaxX() - inset
			&& targetZ >= level.getWorldBorder().getMinZ() + inset && targetZ <= level.getWorldBorder().getMaxZ() - inset;
	}

	private static double[] findOverworldRelocationTarget(ServerLevel level, Entity entity, CompoundTag nbt, double currentX, double currentY, double currentZ, double preferredDx, double preferredDz, boolean allowChunkLoads) {
		double bestTargetX = currentX;
		double bestTargetY = currentY;
		double bestTargetZ = currentZ;
		double highestFitness = -999999.0;

		boolean isVoidEmergency = findGroundBelow(level, currentX, currentZ, level.getMaxBuildHeight() - 10.0) <= level.getMinBuildHeight() + 5.0
			|| persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_void_relocation_pending", false);
		boolean supercharged = persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_supercharged_mode", false);
		double[] baseDistances = isVoidEmergency ? new double[]{256.0, 320.0, 384.0, 512.0} : (supercharged ? new double[]{512.0, 640.0, 768.0, 1024.0} : new double[]{384.0, 448.0, 512.0, 640.0});
		double[] testDistances = getBorderAwareRelocationDistances(level, baseDistances);
		int angleSteps = 16;

		List<Entity> existingEngines = getNearbyVerdantEngines(level, entity, currentX, currentZ, 600.0);
		double rawGround = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_ground_y", 64.0);
		double defaultGroundY = (rawGround <= level.getMinBuildHeight() + 10.0) ? 64.0 : Math.max(level.getMinBuildHeight() + 20.0, rawGround);

		for (double dist : testDistances) {
			for (int dir = 0; dir < angleSteps; dir++) {
				double angle = (Math.PI * 2.0 / angleSteps) * dir + (Math.random() * 0.2 - 0.1);
				double candX = currentX + Math.cos(angle) * dist;
				double candZ = currentZ + Math.sin(angle) * dist;
				if (!isInsideVerdantWorldBorder(level, entity, candX, candZ)) continue;

				int candChunkX = Mth.floor(candX) >> 4;
				int candChunkZ = Mth.floor(candZ) >> 4;

				net.minecraft.world.level.chunk.ChunkAccess actualChunk = level.getChunkSource().getChunkNow(candChunkX, candChunkZ);
				if (actualChunk == null && allowChunkLoads) {
					try {
						actualChunk = level.getChunk(candChunkX, candChunkZ);
					} catch (Throwable ignored) {}
				}

				double candGroundY = actualChunk != null ? actualChunk.getHeight(Heightmap.Types.MOTION_BLOCKING_NO_LEAVES, Mth.floor(candX) & 15, Mth.floor(candZ) & 15) : defaultGroundY;

				if (candGroundY <= level.getMinBuildHeight() + 8.0 || candGroundY >= level.getMaxBuildHeight() - 25.0) {
					continue;
				}

				if (actualChunk != null) {
					double centerProbe = findGroundBelow(level, candX, candZ, candGroundY + 4.0);
					if (centerProbe <= level.getMinBuildHeight() + 10.0) continue;
					double cN = findGroundBelow(level, candX, candZ + 8.0, candGroundY + 4.0);
					double cS = findGroundBelow(level, candX, candZ - 8.0, candGroundY + 4.0);
					double cE = findGroundBelow(level, candX + 8.0, candZ, candGroundY + 4.0);
					double cW = findGroundBelow(level, candX - 8.0, candZ, candGroundY + 4.0);
					if (cN <= level.getMinBuildHeight() + 10.0 || cS <= level.getMinBuildHeight() + 10.0
							|| cE <= level.getMinBuildHeight() + 10.0 || cW <= level.getMinBuildHeight() + 10.0) {
						continue;
					}
					if (Math.abs(centerProbe - cN) > 35.0 || Math.abs(centerProbe - cS) > 35.0
							|| Math.abs(centerProbe - cE) > 35.0 || Math.abs(centerProbe - cW) > 35.0) {
						continue;
					}
					candGroundY = centerProbe;
				}

				double candHoverY = Math.min(level.getMaxBuildHeight() - 12.0, Math.max(level.getMinBuildHeight() + 15.0, candGroundY + HOVER_TARGET_ALTITUDE));
				double fitness = 100.0 + getHiveCandidateFitness(level, entity, candX, candHoverY, candZ);

				if (preferredDx != 0.0 || preferredDz != 0.0) {
					double cosSim = (Math.cos(angle) * preferredDx + Math.sin(angle) * preferredDz);
					fitness += cosSim * 80.0;
				}

				for (Entity other : existingEngines) {
					double d = distanceToReservedVerdantPosition(other, candX, candZ);
					if (d < 384.0) {
						fitness -= (384.0 - d) * 15.0;
					}
				}

				if (actualChunk != null) {
					if (isColumnInfected(level, Mth.floor(candX), Mth.floor(candZ), candHoverY)) {
						fitness -= 70.0;
					} else {
						fitness += 50.0;
					}

					int worldTopY = actualChunk.getHeight(Heightmap.Types.WORLD_SURFACE, Mth.floor(candX) & 15, Mth.floor(candZ) & 15);
					BlockPos surfacePos = new BlockPos(Mth.floor(candX), worldTopY - 1, Mth.floor(candZ));
					BlockState surfaceState = level.getBlockState(surfacePos);
					boolean isTree = surfaceState.is(BlockTags.LEAVES) || surfaceState.is(BlockTags.LOGS);
					if (!isTree) {
						String blockId = BuiltInRegistries.BLOCK.getKey(surfaceState.getBlock()).getPath();
						if (blockId.contains("stem") || blockId.contains("hyphae") || blockId.contains("leaves") || blockId.contains("log")) {
							isTree = true;
						}
					}
					if (isTree) {
						fitness -= 150.0;
					}

					int lowerNeighbors = 0;
					int[][] orthoOffsets = {{ -1, 0 }, { 1, 0 }, { 0, -1 }, { 0, 1 }};
					for (int[] off : orthoOffsets) {
						int nx = Mth.floor(candX) + off[0];
						int nz = Mth.floor(candZ) + off[1];
						net.minecraft.world.level.chunk.ChunkAccess nChunk = level.getChunkSource().getChunkNow(nx >> 4, nz >> 4);
						double nGroundY = nChunk != null ? nChunk.getHeight(Heightmap.Types.MOTION_BLOCKING_NO_LEAVES, nx & 15, nz & 15) : candGroundY;
						if (candGroundY - nGroundY > 4.0) {
							lowerNeighbors++;
						}
					}
					if (lowerNeighbors >= 3) {
						fitness -= 120.0;
					}
				} else if (allowChunkLoads) {
					BlockPos candPos = new BlockPos(Mth.floor(candX), Mth.floor(candHoverY), Mth.floor(candZ));
					if (isInfectedBiome(level, candPos)) {
						fitness -= 70.0;
					} else {
						fitness += 50.0;
					}
				}

				if (fitness > highestFitness) {
					highestFitness = fitness;
					bestTargetX = candX;
					bestTargetY = candHoverY;
					bestTargetZ = candZ;
				}
			}
		}

		if (highestFitness > -900000.0 && (Math.hypot(bestTargetX - currentX, bestTargetZ - currentZ) >= 64.0)) {
			return new double[]{bestTargetX, bestTargetY, bestTargetZ};
		}
		return null;
	}

	private static double[] findNetherRelocationTarget(ServerLevel level, Entity entity, CompoundTag nbt, double currentX, double currentY, double currentZ, double preferredDx, double preferredDz) {
		double bestTargetX = currentX;
		double bestTargetY = currentY;
		double bestTargetZ = currentZ;
		double highestFitness = -999999.0;

		double[] testDistances = getBorderAwareRelocationDistances(level, new double[]{384.0, 448.0, 512.0});
		int angleSteps = 12;

		List<Entity> existingEngines = getNearbyVerdantEngines(level, entity, currentX, currentZ, 600.0);

		for (double dist : testDistances) {
			for (int dir = 0; dir < angleSteps; dir++) {
				double angle = (Math.PI * 2.0 / angleSteps) * dir + (Math.random() * 0.2 - 0.1);
				double candX = currentX + Math.cos(angle) * dist;
				double candZ = currentZ + Math.sin(angle) * dist;
				if (!isInsideVerdantWorldBorder(level, entity, candX, candZ)) continue;

				double candHoverY = calculateOptimalVerdantHoverY(level, candX, candZ, currentY);
				double candGroundY = findHighestGroundInRadius(level, candX, candZ, candHoverY - 5.0, 3);

				if (candGroundY <= level.getMinBuildHeight() + 4.0 || candGroundY >= level.getMaxBuildHeight() - 15.0) {
					continue;
				}

				double fitness = 100.0 + getHiveCandidateFitness(level, entity, candX, candHoverY, candZ);
				if (preferredDx != 0.0 || preferredDz != 0.0) {
					double cosSim = (Math.cos(angle) * preferredDx + Math.sin(angle) * preferredDz);
					fitness += cosSim * 80.0;
				}

				for (Entity other : existingEngines) {
					double d = distanceToReservedVerdantPosition(other, candX, candZ);
					if (d < 384.0) {
						fitness -= (384.0 - d) * 15.0;
					}
				}

				if (isColumnInfected(level, Mth.floor(candX), Mth.floor(candZ), candHoverY)) {
					fitness -= 70.0;
				} else {
					fitness += 50.0;
				}

				if (fitness > highestFitness) {
					highestFitness = fitness;
					bestTargetX = candX;
					bestTargetY = candHoverY;
					bestTargetZ = candZ;
				}
			}
		}

		if (highestFitness > -900000.0 && (Math.hypot(bestTargetX - currentX, bestTargetZ - currentZ) >= 64.0)) {
			return new double[]{bestTargetX, bestTargetY, bestTargetZ};
		}
		return null;
	}

	private static double[] findEndRelocationTarget(ServerLevel level, Entity entity, CompoundTag nbt, double currentX, double currentY, double currentZ, double preferredDx, double preferredDz) {
		double bestTargetX = currentX;
		double bestTargetY = currentY;
		double bestTargetZ = currentZ;
		double highestFitness = -999999.0;

		double[] testDistances = getBorderAwareRelocationDistances(level, new double[]{384.0, 512.0, 768.0, 1056.0});
		int angleSteps = 12;

		AABB engineBox = new AABB(currentX - 1200.0, currentY - 256.0, currentZ - 1200.0, currentX + 1200.0, currentY + 256.0, currentZ + 1200.0);
		List<Entity> existingEngines = level.getEntities(entity, engineBox, e -> isVerdantEngine(e) && e.isAlive() && e != entity);
		double defaultGroundY = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_ground_y", Math.max(level.getMinBuildHeight() + 20.0, currentY - getHoverTargetAltitude(nbt)));

		for (double dist : testDistances) {
			for (int dir = 0; dir < angleSteps; dir++) {
				double angle = (Math.PI * 2.0 / angleSteps) * dir + (Math.random() * 0.2 - 0.1);
				double candX = currentX + Math.cos(angle) * dist;
				double candZ = currentZ + Math.sin(angle) * dist;
				if (!isInsideVerdantWorldBorder(level, entity, candX, candZ)) continue;

				int candChunkX = Mth.floor(candX) >> 4;
				int candChunkZ = Mth.floor(candZ) >> 4;

				net.minecraft.world.level.chunk.ChunkAccess actualChunk = level.getChunkSource().getChunkNow(candChunkX, candChunkZ);
				if (actualChunk == null) {
					try {
						actualChunk = level.getChunk(candChunkX, candChunkZ);
					} catch (Exception ignored) {}
				}

				double candGroundY = actualChunk != null ? actualChunk.getHeight(Heightmap.Types.MOTION_BLOCKING_NO_LEAVES, Mth.floor(candX) & 15, Mth.floor(candZ) & 15) : defaultGroundY;
				if (candGroundY <= level.getMinBuildHeight() + 4.0 || candGroundY >= level.getMaxBuildHeight() - 25.0) {
					continue;
				}

				double candHoverY = Math.min(level.getMaxBuildHeight() - 12.0, Math.max(level.getMinBuildHeight() + 15.0, candGroundY + getHoverTargetAltitude(nbt)));
				double fitness = 100.0 + getHiveCandidateFitness(level, entity, candX, candHoverY, candZ);

				if (preferredDx != 0.0 || preferredDz != 0.0) {
					double cosSim = (Math.cos(angle) * preferredDx + Math.sin(angle) * preferredDz);
					fitness += cosSim * 80.0;
				}

				for (Entity other : existingEngines) {
					double d = distanceToReservedVerdantPosition(other, candX, candZ);
					if (d < 384.0) {
						fitness -= (384.0 - d) * 15.0;
					}
				}

				if (actualChunk != null) {
					if (isColumnInfected(level, Mth.floor(candX), Mth.floor(candZ), candHoverY)) {
						fitness -= 70.0;
					} else {
						fitness += 50.0;
					}
				}

				boolean tooCloseToVoid = false;
				int solidCount48 = 0;
				int solidCount64 = 0;

				for (int a = 0; a < 8; a++) {
					double checkAngle = (Math.PI * 2.0 / 8.0) * a;
					double cosA = fastCos(checkAngle);
					double sinA = fastSin(checkAngle);

					int cx32 = Mth.floor(candX + cosA * 32.0);
					int cz32 = Mth.floor(candZ + sinA * 32.0);
					int chkX32 = cx32 >> 4;
					int chkZ32 = cz32 >> 4;
