		if (level.hasChunkAt(impactHeadPos) && !level.getBlockState(impactHeadPos).isAir() && level.getBlockState(impactHeadPos).isRedstoneConductor(level, impactHeadPos)) {
			nbt.putDouble("verdant_shockwave_stage", 0);
		} else {
			nbt.putDouble("verdant_shockwave_stage", 1.0);
		}

		nbt.putDouble("verdant_shockwave_ox", ix);
		nbt.putDouble("verdant_shockwave_oy", gy);
		nbt.putDouble("verdant_shockwave_oz", iz);
		nbt.putDouble("verdant_shockwave_max_r", Math.min(getShockwaveParticleRadius(nbt), poundR));
		nbt.putFloat("verdant_shockwave_dmg", slamDmg);

		long[] hitIdArray = new long[hitIds.size()];
		for (int i = 0; i < hitIds.size(); i++) {
			hitIdArray[i] = hitIds.get(i);
		}
		nbt.putLongArray("verdant_shockwave_hit_ids", hitIdArray);

		double curDepth = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_crater_depth", 0.0);
		double penSpeed = getGravityBeamPenetrationSpeed(nbt);
		nbt.putDouble("verdant_crater_depth", Math.min(128.0, curDepth + (1.8 + (poundCount * 0.3)) * penSpeed));

		if (terraform) {
			double maxCarveCap = isSuperchargedPound ? SUPERCHARGED_CRATER_MAX_RADIUS_CAP : 64.0;
			double burstCarveRadius = Math.min(maxCarveCap, Math.min(poundR * 0.95, (isSuperchargedPound ? SUPERCHARGED_CRATER_BASE_RADIUS : 12.0) + (poundCount * (isSuperchargedPound ? SUPERCHARGED_CRATER_RADIUS_PER_POUND : 4.5))));
			if (isSuperchargedPound) {
				queueSuperchargedCraterWork(engine, ix, gy, iz, burstCarveRadius, poundCount, penSpeed);
			} else {
			BlockPos.MutableBlockPos cPos = new BlockPos.MutableBlockPos();
			java.util.Set<Long> visitedCarveCols = new java.util.HashSet<>();
			for (int i = 0; i < 64; i++) {
				double a = (2 * Math.PI / 64) * i;
				for (double dist = 0.0; dist <= burstCarveRadius; dist += 1.2) {
					int bx = Mth.floor(ix + Math.cos(a) * dist);
					int bz = Mth.floor(iz + Math.sin(a) * dist);
					long colKey = (((long) bx) << 32) | (bz & 0xFFFFFFFFL);
					if (!visitedCarveCols.add(colKey)) continue;
					double dNorm = dist / Math.max(1.0, burstCarveRadius);
					int carveDown = (int) Math.round((1.0 - dNorm * (isSuperchargedPound ? 0.55 : 0.60)) * ((isSuperchargedPound ? 7.0 : 3.5) + Math.min(isSuperchargedPound ? 64.0 : 32.0, poundCount * (isSuperchargedPound ? 3.0 : 1.5))) * penSpeed);
					for (int dy = 2; dy >= -carveDown; dy--) {
						cPos.set(bx, Mth.floor(gy + dy), bz);
						if (!level.hasChunkAt(cPos)) continue;
						BlockState st = level.getBlockState(cPos);
						boolean isCurBedrock = st.getBlock() == Blocks.BEDROCK;
						boolean isCurIndestructible = isCurBedrock || isIndestructibleBlock(st, level, cPos);
						if (isCurIndestructible) {
							recordMaterialEncounter(nbt, st.getBlock());
						}
						boolean shatterBedrock = shouldShatterBedrock(nbt, dist, poundCount, st.getBlock());

						if (isCurIndestructible) {
							if (shatterBedrock) {
								level.setBlock(cPos, Blocks.AIR.defaultBlockState(), INFECTION_BLOCK_FLAGS);
							}
							continue;
						}

						if (!st.getFluidState().isEmpty() || st.getBlock() == Blocks.WATER || st.getBlock() == Blocks.LAVA) {
							replaceBlockWithOakPlague(level, cPos, st, false);
							continue;
						}
						if (dy > -carveDown) {
							if (!st.isAir()) {
								level.setBlock(cPos, Blocks.AIR.defaultBlockState(), INFECTION_BLOCK_FLAGS);
							}
						} else {
							replaceBlockWithOakPlague(level, cPos, st, false);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			}
		}

		if (terraform) {
			boolean hasCeiling = isCeiledDimension(level);
			boolean epicenterBlocked = hasCeiling && isEpicenterBlockedByIndestructibleBarrier(level, ix, gy, iz, lockedY);

			if (!epicenterBlocked) {
				nbt.putDouble("verdant_burst_target_radius", Math.min(getMaxInfectionRadius(nbt), persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_infection_radius", BEAM_RADIUS + 0.5) + 32.0 + (poundCount * 14.0)));
				nbt.putInt("verdant_burst_spread_ticks", 40);
				nbt.putInt("verdant_pound_burst_ticks", 40);
			}
		}
	}

	private static void handleActiveShockwaves(ServerLevel level, CompoundTag nbt) {
		double stage = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_shockwave_stage", 0.0);
		if (stage <= 0.0) return;

		long gameTime = level.getGameTime();
		if (persistentLong(nbt, "verdant_shockwave_last_tick", -1L) == gameTime) return;
		nbt.putLong("verdant_shockwave_last_tick", gameTime);

		double ox = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_shockwave_ox", 0.0);
		double oy = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_shockwave_oy", 0.0);
		double oz = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_shockwave_oz", 0.0);

		if (!isPlayerInParticleRange3D(level, ox, oy, oz, 128.0 * 128.0)) {
			clearActiveShockwave(nbt);
			return;
		}

		double maxR = Math.min(getShockwaveParticleRadius(nbt), persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_shockwave_max_r", 48.0));
		int pounds = persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_pound_count", 0);
		double expandSpeed = 2.0 + (pounds * 0.35);
		double radius = BEAM_RADIUS + (stage * expandSpeed);

		if (radius >= maxR || stage > 50.0) {
			clearActiveShockwave(nbt);
			return;
		}

		double quality = getShockwaveParticleQuality(nbt);
		if (quality > 0.0) {
			int count = Math.min(48, Math.max((int) (10 * quality), (int) (2.0 * Math.PI * radius * 0.45 * quality)));

			int lastChunkX = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
			int lastChunkZ = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
			LevelChunk chunk = null;

			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
				double a = (2.0 * Math.PI / count) * i;

				double wave1 = fastSin(a * 5.0 + stage * 0.3) * 0.85;
				double wave2 = fastCos(a * 11.0 - stage * 0.2) * 0.55;
				double organicRadialOffset = wave1 + wave2 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.5;

				double effRadius = Math.max(0.5, radius + organicRadialOffset);
				double c = Math.cos(a);
				double s = Math.sin(a);

				double px = ox + c * effRadius + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;
				double pz = oz + s * effRadius + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;
				int ipx = Mth.floor(px);
				int ipz = Mth.floor(pz);
				int cx = ipx >> 4;
				int cz = ipz >> 4;

				if (cx != lastChunkX || cz != lastChunkZ) {
					lastChunkX = cx;
					lastChunkZ = cz;
					chunk = level.getChunkSource().getChunkNow(cx, cz);
				}
				if (chunk == null) continue;

				int groundY = chunk.getHeight(Heightmap.Types.MOTION_BLOCKING, ipx & 15, ipz & 15);
				if (groundY < level.getMinBuildHeight() + 1 || Math.abs(groundY - oy) > 24.0) continue;

				double surfaceY = groundY + 1.5;

				double outwardSpeed = 0.08 + Math.random() * 0.14;
				double upwardSpeed = 0.03 + Math.random() * 0.05;

				sendFarParticles(level, getBigSmokeParticle(), px, surfaceY, pz, 1, c * outwardSpeed, upwardSpeed, s * outwardSpeed, 0.03);

				if (i % 3 == 0) {
					double curlRadius = Math.max(0.5, effRadius - 0.4 - Math.random() * 0.5);
					double curlY = surfaceY + 0.6 + Math.random() * 0.6;
					double inwardRollSpeed = 0.04 + Math.random() * 0.06;
					double upwardRollSpeed = 0.10 + Math.random() * 0.08;
					sendFarParticles(level, getBigSmokeParticle(), ox + c * curlRadius, curlY, oz + s * curlRadius, 1, -c * inwardRollSpeed, upwardRollSpeed, -s * inwardRollSpeed, 0.02);
				}
			}
		}

		float slamDmg = persistentFloat(nbt, "verdant_shockwave_dmg", 195.0F);
		double innerR = Math.max(0.0, radius - expandSpeed - 2.0);
		double outerR = radius + expandSpeed + 2.0;
		double innerRSq = innerR * innerR;
		double outerRSq = outerR * outerR;
		double maxRSq = maxR * maxR;
		AABB waveBox = new AABB(ox - outerR, oy - 12.0, oz - outerR, ox + outerR, oy + 35.0, oz + outerR);

		long[] hitIds = nbt.contains("verdant_shockwave_hit_ids") ? nbt.getLongArray("verdant_shockwave_hit_ids") : new long[0];
		int initialHitCount = hitIds.length;
		long[] newHitsBuffer = null;
		int newHitCount = 0;

		for (Entity victim : level.getEntitiesOfClass(Entity.class, waveBox, e -> e != null && e.isAlive())) {
			if (victim instanceof Player p && (p.isCreative() || p.isSpectator())) continue;
			if (isWoodbound(victim) || isVerdantEngine(victim)) continue;
			long id = victim.getId();
			boolean alreadyHit = false;
			for (int i = 0; i < initialHitCount; i++) {
				if (hitIds[i] == id) {
					alreadyHit = true;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (alreadyHit) continue;
			if (newHitsBuffer != null) {
				for (int i = 0; i < newHitCount; i++) {
					if (newHitsBuffer[i] == id) {
						alreadyHit = true;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			if (alreadyHit) continue;

			double dx = victim.getX() - ox;
			double dz = victim.getZ() - oz;
			double distSq = dx * dx + dz * dz;

			if (distSq >= innerRSq && distSq <= outerRSq && distSq <= maxRSq) {
				double dist = Math.sqrt(distSq);
				if (newHitsBuffer == null) {
					newHitsBuffer = new long[16];
				} else if (newHitCount >= newHitsBuffer.length) {
					long[] expanded = new long[newHitsBuffer.length * 2];
					System.arraycopy(newHitsBuffer, 0, expanded, 0, newHitCount);
					newHitsBuffer = expanded;
				}
				newHitsBuffer[newHitCount++] = id;
				float waveDmg = Math.max(SHOCKWAVE_MIN_DAMAGE, slamDmg * (float) (1.0 - (dist / (maxR * 1.2))));

				victim.setNoGravity(false);
				victim.getPersistentData().putInt("verdant_slammed_ticks", 30);

				double len = dist > 0.001 ? dist : 1.0;
				double pushX = (dx / len) * 1.8 * (1.0 - dist / (maxR * 1.5));
				double pushZ = (dz / len) * 1.8 * (1.0 - dist / (maxR * 1.5));
				double pushY = 0.6 + 0.4 * (1.0 - dist / (maxR * 1.5));

				victim.setDeltaMovement(pushX, pushY, pushZ);
				victim.hurtMarked = true;
				victim.hasImpulse = true;

				if (victim instanceof LivingEntity living) {
					applyTruePoundDamage(level, living, null, waveDmg);
					living.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(MobEffects.MOVEMENT_SLOWDOWN, 120, 2, false, false, true));
					if (living instanceof ServerPlayer sp) sp.connection.send(new ClientboundSetEntityMotionPacket(sp));
				} else if (victim instanceof net.minecraft.world.entity.item.ItemEntity itemEnt) {
					damageItemEntitySeverely(level, itemEnt, waveDmg);
				}
			}
		}

		if (newHitCount > 0) {
			long[] updatedHits = new long[initialHitCount + newHitCount];
			if (initialHitCount > 0) {
				System.arraycopy(hitIds, 0, updatedHits, 0, initialHitCount);
			}
			System.arraycopy(newHitsBuffer, 0, updatedHits, initialHitCount, newHitCount);
			nbt.putLongArray("verdant_shockwave_hit_ids", updatedHits);
		}

		if ((int) stage % 3 == 1) playEngineSound(level, ox, oy, oz, "block.beacon.power_select", 24.0F, 0.20F - ((float) stage * 0.005F));
		processShockwaveDominoEffects(level, ox, oy, oz, radius, expandSpeed, oy + 85.0);

		nbt.putDouble("verdant_shockwave_stage", stage + 1.0);
	}

	private static void clearActiveShockwave(CompoundTag nbt) {
		if (nbt == null) return;
		nbt.putDouble("verdant_shockwave_stage", 0.0);
		nbt.remove("verdant_shockwave_hit_ids");
		nbt.remove("verdant_shockwave_last_tick");
	}

	private static void performSlowKineticTerraforming(ServerLevel level, CompoundTag nbt, double cx, double gy, double cz, double r, int pounds) {
		double depth = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_crater_depth", 0.0);
		if (depth < 64.0) {
			depth += 0.0012;
			nbt.putDouble("verdant_crater_depth", depth);
		}

		BlockPos.MutableBlockPos pos = new BlockPos.MutableBlockPos();
		for (int s = 0; s < 2; s++) {
			double a = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2, d = Math.sqrt(Math.random()) * r * 1.6;
			double dx = Math.cos(a) * d, dz = Math.sin(a) * d, norm = d / r;
			if (norm > 1.65) continue;

			double rough = 0.5 + 0.22 * Math.sin(dx * 0.75 + dz * 0.6) + 0.15 * Math.cos(dx * 1.2 - dz * 0.8);
			int targetD = (int) Math.round(depth * Math.max(0.0, 1.0 - (norm * 0.65)) * (0.8 + 0.4 * rough));

			for (int dy = 2; dy >= -targetD - 1; dy--) {
				pos.set(Mth.floor(cx + dx), Mth.floor(gy + dy), Mth.floor(cz + dz));
				if (!level.hasChunkAt(pos)) continue;
				BlockState st = level.getBlockState(pos);
				boolean isCurBedrock = st.getBlock() == Blocks.BEDROCK;
				boolean isCurIndestructible = isCurBedrock || isIndestructibleBlock(st, level, pos);
				if (isCurIndestructible) {
					recordMaterialEncounter(nbt, st.getBlock());
				}
				boolean shatterBedrock = shouldShatterBedrock(nbt, d, pounds, st.getBlock());

				if (isCurIndestructible) {
					if (shatterBedrock) {
						level.setBlock(pos, Blocks.AIR.defaultBlockState(), INFECTION_BLOCK_FLAGS);
					}
					continue;
				}

				if (!st.getFluidState().isEmpty() || st.getBlock() == Blocks.WATER || st.getBlock() == Blocks.LAVA) {
					if (ENABLE_FLUID_EVAPORATION) level.setBlock(pos, Blocks.AIR.defaultBlockState(), INFECTION_BLOCK_FLAGS);
					continue;
				}
				if (dy > -targetD) {
					if (!st.isAir()) {
						level.setBlock(pos, Blocks.AIR.defaultBlockState(), INFECTION_BLOCK_FLAGS);
					}
				} else {
					replaceBlockWithOakPlague(level, pos, st, false);
					break;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private static final long[] DEEP_INFECTION_CACHE_KEYS = new long[4096];
	private static final long[] DEEP_INFECTION_CACHE_TICKS = new long[4096];

	private static long deepInfectionCacheKey(ServerLevel level, int bx, int bz) {
		long packedColumn = (((long) bx) << 32) | (bz & 0xFFFFFFFFL);
		long dimensionSalt = Integer.toUnsignedLong(level.dimension().location().hashCode());
		long levelSalt = Integer.toUnsignedLong(System.identityHashCode(level));
		return packedColumn ^ Long.rotateLeft(dimensionSalt, 17) ^ (levelSalt << 32);
	}

	private static int deepInfectionCacheSlot(long key) {
		return (int) (key ^ (key >>> 16) ^ (key >>> 32)) & 4095;
	}

	private static boolean isColumnDeeplyInfected(ServerLevel level, int bx, int bz, double hoverY) {
		long cacheKey = deepInfectionCacheKey(level, bx, bz);
		int cacheSlot = deepInfectionCacheSlot(cacheKey);
		long gameTime = level.getGameTime();
		if (gameTime > 0L && DEEP_INFECTION_CACHE_TICKS[cacheSlot] == gameTime && DEEP_INFECTION_CACHE_KEYS[cacheSlot] == cacheKey) return true;

		int chunkX = bx >> 4;
		int chunkZ = bz >> 4;
		net.minecraft.world.level.chunk.ChunkAccess chunk = level.getChunkSource().getChunkNow(chunkX, chunkZ);
		if (chunk == null) return false;

		int minWorldY = (int) level.getMinBuildHeight() + 5;
		int localX = bx & 15;
		int localZ = bz & 15;
		int surfaceY = chunk.getHeight(Heightmap.Types.MOTION_BLOCKING_NO_LEAVES, localX, localZ);

		if (surfaceY <= minWorldY) {
			BlockPos.MutableBlockPos checkPos = new BlockPos.MutableBlockPos(bx, minWorldY, bz);
			for (int y = Math.min((int) level.getMaxBuildHeight() - 1, (int) hoverY + 10); y >= minWorldY - 5; y--) {
				checkPos.setY(y);
				BlockState st = chunk.getBlockState(checkPos);
				if (!st.isAir() && isAlreadyInfectedBlock(st.getBlock())) {
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		BlockPos.MutableBlockPos pos = new BlockPos.MutableBlockPos();

		boolean surfInfected = isSampleLevelInfected1211(chunk, pos, bx, localX, localZ, Math.max(minWorldY, surfaceY - 1), minWorldY);
		if (!surfInfected) return false;

		int yMid = Math.max(minWorldY + 10, surfaceY - 25);
		boolean midInfected = isSampleLevelInfected1211(chunk, pos, bx, localX, localZ, yMid, minWorldY);
		if (!midInfected) return false;

		int yDeep = Math.max(minWorldY + 5, Math.min(yMid - 10, minWorldY + 20));
		boolean deepInfected = isSampleLevelInfected1211(chunk, pos, bx, localX, localZ, yDeep, minWorldY);

		if (deepInfected && gameTime > 0L) {
			DEEP_INFECTION_CACHE_KEYS[cacheSlot] = cacheKey;
			DEEP_INFECTION_CACHE_TICKS[cacheSlot] = gameTime;
		}
		return deepInfected;
	}

	private static boolean isSampleLevelInfected1211(net.minecraft.world.level.chunk.ChunkAccess chunk, BlockPos.MutableBlockPos pos, int bx, int localX, int localZ, int sampleY, int minWorldY) {
		pos.set(bx, sampleY, localZ);
		BlockState st = chunk.getBlockState(pos);
		while (pos.getY() > minWorldY && (!st.getFluidState().isEmpty() || st.getBlock() == Blocks.WATER || st.getBlock() == Blocks.LAVA)) {
			pos.move(Direction.DOWN);
			st = chunk.getBlockState(pos);
		}
		while (pos.getY() > minWorldY && st.isAir()) {
			pos.move(Direction.DOWN);
			st = chunk.getBlockState(pos);
		}
		if (st.isAir()) return false;
		Block b = st.getBlock();
		return isAlreadyInfectedBlock(b) || b == Blocks.BEDROCK || b == Blocks.BARRIER;
	}

	private static double sampleRelocationCandidateSaturation(ServerLevel level, double candidateX, double candidateY, double candidateZ) {
		int totalSamples = 0;
		int infectedSamples = 0;
		int centerX = Mth.floor(candidateX);
		int centerZ = Mth.floor(candidateZ);
		if (isHiveSaturationSampleInsideBorder(level, centerX, centerZ)) {
			totalSamples++;
			if (isColumnDeeplyInfected(level, centerX, centerZ, candidateY)) infectedSamples++;
		}
		for (int ring = 0; ring < 4; ring++) {
			double radius = ring == 0 ? 48.0 : ring == 1 ? 96.0 : ring == 2 ? 160.0 : 224.0;
			int sampleCount = (ring + 1) * 10;
			for (int sample = 0; sample < sampleCount; sample++) {
				double angle = Math.PI * 2.0 * sample / sampleCount + (ring == 0 ? 0.0 : 0.2);
				int sampleX = Mth.floor(candidateX + fastCos(angle) * radius);
				int sampleZ = Mth.floor(candidateZ + fastSin(angle) * radius);
				if (!isHiveSaturationSampleInsideBorder(level, sampleX, sampleZ)) continue;
				totalSamples++;
				if (isColumnDeeplyInfected(level, sampleX, sampleZ, candidateY)) infectedSamples++;
			}
		}
		return totalSamples > 0 ? (double) infectedSamples / (double) totalSamples : 0.0;
	}

	private static final long[] PROCESSED_COLUMNS = new long[16384];
	private static final int[] PROCESSED_COLUMNS_TAG = new int[16384];

	private static boolean markColumnVisitedThisTick(ServerLevel level, int bx, int bz, int tickEpoch) {
		long packedColumn = (((long) bx) << 32) | (bz & 0xFFFFFFFFL);
		long dimensionSalt = Integer.toUnsignedLong(level.dimension().location().hashCode());
		long levelSalt = Integer.toUnsignedLong(System.identityHashCode(level));
		long key = packedColumn ^ Long.rotateLeft(dimensionSalt, 17) ^ (levelSalt << 32);
		int mask = 16383;
		int idx = (int) (key ^ (key >>> 16) ^ (key >>> 32)) & mask;
		for (int i = 0; i < 16; i++) {
			int slot = (idx + i) & mask;
			if (PROCESSED_COLUMNS_TAG[slot] == tickEpoch) {
				if (PROCESSED_COLUMNS[slot] == key) return false;
			} else {
				PROCESSED_COLUMNS_TAG[slot] = tickEpoch;
				PROCESSED_COLUMNS[slot] = key;
				return true;
			}
		}
		return true;
	}

	private static int infectRod3DBlock(ServerLevel level, int bx, int bz, double scanTop, double gy, double curR, boolean isSolidCore, boolean isBurst, boolean spawnBurstParticles) {
		return infectRod3DBlock(level, bx, bz, scanTop, gy, curR, isSolidCore, isBurst, spawnBurstParticles, new BlockPos.MutableBlockPos());
	}

	private static int infectRod3DBlock(ServerLevel level, int bx, int bz, double scanTop, double gy, double curR, boolean isSolidCore, boolean isBurst,
			boolean spawnBurstParticles, BlockPos.MutableBlockPos pos) {
		int chunkX = bx >> 4;
		int chunkZ = bz >> 4;
		LevelChunk chunk = level.getChunkSource().getChunkNow(chunkX, chunkZ);
		if (chunk == null) return -1;

		int minWorldY = (int) level.getMinBuildHeight() + 5;
		int colHeight = chunk.getHeight(Heightmap.Types.WORLD_SURFACE, bx & 15, bz & 15);
		int startY = Math.min((int) scanTop, colHeight);
		if (startY <= minWorldY) return 0;

		boolean isCell = false;
		boolean isCellResolved = false;
		boolean canBreakIndestructible = canInfectIndestructible(null);
		int converted = 0;
		int changed = 0;
		int maxTarget = isBurst ? 96 : (isSolidCore ? 64 : 32);
		int localX = bx & 15;
		int localZ = bz & 15;
		LevelChunkSection[] sections = chunk.getSections();

		int y = startY;
		while (y >= minWorldY) {
			int sectionIndex = chunk.getSectionIndex(y);
			if (sectionIndex < 0 || sectionIndex >= sections.length) {
				y--;
				continue;
			}

			int sectionBottomY = chunk.getSectionYFromSectionIndex(sectionIndex) << 4;
			int sectionEndY = Math.max(minWorldY, sectionBottomY);
			LevelChunkSection section = sections[sectionIndex];
			if (section == null || section.hasOnlyAir()) {
				y = sectionBottomY - 1;
				continue;
			}

			for (; y >= sectionEndY; y--) {
				BlockState st = section.getBlockState(localX, y & 15, localZ);
				if (st.isAir()) continue;

				Block b = st.getBlock();
				if (isAlreadyInfectedBlock(b)) continue;
				if (b == Blocks.ANCIENT_DEBRIS || isAncientDebris(st, b)) continue;

				pos.set(bx, y, bz);
				if (isIndestructibleBlock(st, level, pos) && !canBreakIndestructible) continue;

				boolean isFoliage = isFoliageOrDebris(st, b);

				if (isFoliage) {
					level.setBlock(pos, Blocks.AIR.defaultBlockState(), INFECTION_BLOCK_FLAGS);
					changed++;
					continue;
				}

				if (!isCellResolved) {
					isCell = isCellularMembrane(bx, bz, level.getSeed());
					isCellResolved = true;
				}
				if (replaceBlockWithOakPlague(level, pos, st, false, isCell)) {
					converted++;
					changed++;
					if (spawnBurstParticles && Math.random() < 0.10) {
						sendFarParticles(level, getOakPlanksParticle(), pos.getX() + 0.5, pos.getY() + 1.05, pos.getZ() + 0.5, 2, 0.2, 0.2, 0.2, 0.05);
					}
					if (converted >= maxTarget) return -(changed + 1);
				}
			}
		}
		return changed;
	}

	private static void performContinuousBeamTipInfection(ServerLevel level, CompoundTag nbt, double cx, double gy, double cz, int cycle, int pounds) {
		boolean hasCeiling = isCeiledDimension(level);
		double lockedY = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_locked_y", gy + getHoverTargetAltitude(nbt));
		if (hasCeiling && isEpicenterBlockedByIndestructibleBarrier(level, cx, gy, cz, lockedY)) {
			return;
		}

		boolean supercharged = persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_supercharged_mode", false);
		double speedMult = getInfectionSpeedMultiplier(nbt) * (supercharged ? 3.0 : 1.0);
		double cycleProg = (double) Math.min(cycle, CYCLE_TOTAL_TICKS) / (double) CYCLE_TOTAL_TICKS;

		double tipRadius = BEAM_RADIUS + cycleProg * (9.0 + Math.min(6.0, pounds * 0.5));

		int sampleCount = (int) Math.max(12, Math.min(36, (12 + tipRadius * 2.0) * speedMult));
		BlockPos.MutableBlockPos pos = new BlockPos.MutableBlockPos();
		boolean canInfectIndestructible = canInfectIndestructible(nbt);
		boolean isCell = isCellularMembrane(Mth.floor(cx), Mth.floor(cz), level.getSeed());

		for (int i = 0; i < sampleCount; i++) {
			double angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2.0;
			double dist = Math.sqrt(Math.random()) * tipRadius;
			int bx = Mth.floor(cx + Math.cos(angle) * dist);
			int bz = Mth.floor(cz + Math.sin(angle) * dist);

			int startY = (int) Math.min(level.getMaxBuildHeight() - 2.0, gy + 5.0);
			int endY = (int) Math.max(level.getMinBuildHeight() + 2.0, gy - 12.0);

			for (int y = startY; y >= endY; y--) {
				pos.set(bx, y, bz);
				if (!level.hasChunkAt(pos)) continue;
				BlockState st = level.getBlockState(pos);
				if (st.isAir()) continue;

				if (isIndestructibleBlock(st, level, pos)) {
					if (!canInfectIndestructible) {
						break;
					}
				}

				if (isFoliageOrDebris(st, st.getBlock())) {
					level.setBlock(pos, Blocks.AIR.defaultBlockState(), INFECTION_BLOCK_FLAGS);
					continue;
				}

				for (int dy = 0; dy < 3; dy++) {
					int subY = y - dy;
					if (subY < endY) break;
					pos.set(bx, subY, bz);
					BlockState subSt = level.getBlockState(pos);
					if (subSt.isAir()) continue;
					if (isIndestructibleBlock(subSt, level, pos)) {
						if (!canInfectIndestructible) break;
					}
					if (isFoliageOrDebris(subSt, subSt.getBlock())) {
						level.setBlock(pos, Blocks.AIR.defaultBlockState(), INFECTION_BLOCK_FLAGS);
						continue;
					}
					replaceBlockWithOakPlague(level, pos, subSt, false, isCell);
				}
				break;
			}
		}
	}

	private static void performContinuousSolidRadialWoodInfection(ServerLevel level, Entity entity, CompoundTag nbt, double cx, double gy, double cz, int pounds) {
		boolean hasCeiling = isCeiledDimension(level);
		double lockedY = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_locked_y", gy + getHoverTargetAltitude(nbt));
		boolean epicenterBlocked = hasCeiling && isEpicenterBlockedByIndestructibleBarrier(level, cx, gy, cz, lockedY);
		if (epicenterBlocked) {
			nbt.putDouble("verdant_infection_radius", BEAM_RADIUS + 0.5);
			nbt.putDouble("verdant_burst_target_radius", 0.0);
			nbt.putInt("verdant_burst_spread_ticks", 0);
			nbt.putInt("verdant_pound_burst_ticks", 0);
			return;
		}

		boolean superchargedRadial = persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_supercharged_mode", false);
		double speedMult = getInfectionSpeedMultiplier(nbt) * (superchargedRadial ? 3.0 : 1.0);
		double maxInfR = superchargedRadial ? SUPERCHARGED_MAX_INFECTION_RADIUS : MAX_INFECTION_RADIUS;
		double curR = Math.min(maxInfR, persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_infection_radius", BEAM_RADIUS + 0.5) + (0.08 * speedMult));
		nbt.putDouble("verdant_infection_radius", curR);

		double density = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_last_sample_density", 1.0);
		double estimatedSatRatio = Math.min(1.0, (curR / maxInfR) * density);
		nbt.putDouble("verdant_estimated_block_saturation", estimatedSatRatio);
		double realSatRatio = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_real_block_saturation", 0.0);
		double scanTop = hasCeiling
			? Math.min(level.getMaxBuildHeight() - 4.0, gy + 15.0)
			: (level.getMaxBuildHeight() - 4.0);
		int tickEpoch = (int) level.getGameTime();

		if (entity != null) {
			UUID coreUuid = entity.getUUID();
			ResourceLocation coreDimension = level.dimension().location();
			ActiveVerdantCore activeCore = ACTIVE_CORES.get(coreUuid);
			if (activeCore == null || !activeCore.dimension.equals(coreDimension)) {
				ACTIVE_CORES.put(coreUuid, new ActiveVerdantCore(coreUuid, coreDimension, cx, cz, gy, scanTop, curR, pounds, level.getGameTime()));
			} else {
				activeCore.coreX = cx;
				activeCore.coreZ = cz;
				activeCore.groundY = gy;
				activeCore.scanTop = scanTop;
				activeCore.infectionRadius = curR;
				activeCore.pounds = pounds;
				activeCore.lastUpdateTick = level.getGameTime();
			}
		}

		if (ENABLE_INFECTION_CHAT_LOGGING && level != null && level.getGameTime() % 20 == 0) {
			double satPct = realSatRatio * 100.0;
			double radPct = Math.min(100.0, (curR / maxInfR) * 100.0);
			double satThreshold = getInfectionSaturationThreshold(nbt) * 100.0;
			int currentIntPct = (int) Math.round(satPct * 100.0);
			int lastLogged = persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_last_logged_sat_pct", -1);

			if (currentIntPct != lastLogged || level.getGameTime() % 60 == 0) {
				nbt.putInt("verdant_last_logged_sat_pct", currentIntPct);
				int engineIdx = getVerdantEngineIndex(level, entity);
				long worldRegions = persistentLong(nbt, "verdant_hive_world_regions", Long.MAX_VALUE);
				String worldRegionText = worldRegions == Long.MAX_VALUE ? "?" : Long.toString(worldRegions);
				String msg = String.format("§a[Verdant %d] §fMeasured Saturation: §e%.2f%% §f/ §c%.2f%% §f(Radius: §b%.1fm §f[%.0f%%] | Pounds: §d%d§f) §8| §fHive: §a%d§7/§a%s §fRegion: §6%.1f%% §fPhase: §b%d §fEnv: §b%d §fReloc: §b%d §fBorderQ: §b%d §fActive: §b%d §fDebt: §b%d/%d/%d §fSamples: §e%d§7/§e%d§7/§e%d §fTime: §c%dµs",
					engineIdx, satPct, satThreshold, curR, radPct, pounds,
					persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_hive_completed_region_count", 0), worldRegionText,
					persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_hive_current_region_saturation", 0.0) * 100.0,
					persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_hive_phase", HIVE_PHASE_ARRIVAL),
					persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_environment_state", ENVIRONMENT_STATE_UNCHECKED),
					persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_is_relocating", false) ? 1 : 0,
					persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_border_relocation_pending", false) ? 1 : 0,
					getActiveTerraformingEngineCount(level),
					persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_radial_work_debt", 0), persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_burst_work_debt", 0),
					persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_biome_work_debt", 0),
					persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_radial_requested_samples", 0), persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_radial_granted_samples", 0),
					persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_radial_executed_samples", 0), persistentLong(nbt, "verdant_radial_elapsed_us", 0L));
				Component comp = Component.literal(msg);
				double broadcastRange = Math.max(curR + 64.0, 256.0);
				double broadcastRangeSq = broadcastRange * broadcastRange;
				for (ServerPlayer player : level.players()) {
					if (player.distanceToSqr(cx, gy, cz) <= broadcastRangeSq) {
						player.sendSystemMessage(comp);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		boolean fullRadiusReached = curR >= maxInfR - 0.001;
		double radiusSampleMultiplier = fullRadiusReached ? FULL_RADIUS_SAMPLE_MULTIPLIER : 1.0;
		long fullRadiusPoundSamplesLong = fullRadiusReached ? (long) Math.max(0, pounds) * FULL_RADIUS_SAMPLES_PER_POUND : 0L;
		int fullRadiusPoundSamples = (int) Math.min(FULL_RADIUS_SAMPLE_CAP, fullRadiusPoundSamplesLong);
		long requestedSamplesLong = (long) Math.max(40.0,
			(2.0 * Math.PI * curR * 1.2 + (pounds * 12.0)) * speedMult * radiusSampleMultiplier) + fullRadiusPoundSamples;
		int requestedSamples = (int) Math.min(fullRadiusReached ? FULL_RADIUS_SAMPLE_CAP : 200L, requestedSamplesLong);

		int poundBurst = persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_pound_burst_ticks", 0);
		if (poundBurst > 0) {
			requestedSamples += (int) ((80 + (Math.random() * 40)) * speedMult);
			nbt.putInt("verdant_pound_burst_ticks", poundBurst - 1);
		}
		if (fullRadiusReached) requestedSamples = Math.min(FULL_RADIUS_SAMPLE_CAP, requestedSamples);
		int activeTerraformers = getActiveTerraformingEngineCount(level);
		nbt.putInt("verdant_active_terraformer_count", activeTerraformers);
		int totalSamples;
		if (superchargedRadial && activeTerraformers <= 1) {
			long combinedWork = (long) Math.max(0, persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_radial_work_debt", 0)) + requestedSamples;
			totalSamples = (int) Math.min(SUPERCHARGED_RADIAL_DEBT_CAP, combinedWork);
			nbt.remove("verdant_radial_work_debt");
		} else {
			totalSamples = claimTerraformingSampleBudget(nbt, "verdant_radial_work_debt",
				requestedSamples, fullRadiusReached
					? Math.min(FULL_RADIUS_SAMPLE_CAP, FULL_RADIUS_RADIAL_COLUMN_BUDGET + fullRadiusPoundSamples)
					: MULTI_ENGINE_RADIAL_COLUMN_BUDGET, activeTerraformers);
			activeTerraformers = persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_active_terraformer_count", activeTerraformers);
		}
		nbt.putInt("verdant_radial_requested_samples", requestedSamples);
		nbt.putInt("verdant_radial_granted_samples", totalSamples);

		int solidSamples = (int) (totalSamples * 0.90);
		double solidR = Math.max(0.0, curR - 2.5);
		long radialCursor = persistentLong(nbt, "verdant_radial_sample_cursor", 0L);
		double angleOffset = entity == null ? 0.0 : Math.floorMod(entity.getUUID().hashCode(), 3600) * (Math.PI / 1800.0);
		long radialWorkStartNanos = System.nanoTime();
		long reservedSuperchargedWork = superchargedRadial
			? SUPERCHARGED_BURST_TIME_BUDGET_NANOS + SUPERCHARGED_CRATER_TIME_BUDGET_NANOS
			: 0L;
		long radialBudgetPool = Math.max(1_000_000L, FULL_RADIUS_SERVER_TIME_BUDGET_NANOS - reservedSuperchargedWork);
		long radialTimeSliceNanos = Math.max(1_000_000L, radialBudgetPool / Math.max(1, activeTerraformers));
		boolean deterministicRadial = fullRadiusReached || superchargedRadial;
		int samplesExamined = 0;
		int samplesExecuted = 0;
		BlockPos.MutableBlockPos infectionPos = new BlockPos.MutableBlockPos();
		TerraformWorkCoordinator terraformCoordinator = getTerraformCoordinator(level);
		long terraformGameTime = level.getGameTime();
		double edgeWidth = Math.max(0.1, curR - solidR);

		for (int i = 0; i < totalSamples; i++) {
			if (deterministicRadial && System.nanoTime() - radialWorkStartNanos >= radialTimeSliceNanos) break;
			samplesExamined++;
			long sequenceIndex = Math.floorMod(radialCursor + i, 1_000_000_007L);
			double a = deterministicRadial ? sequenceIndex * RADIAL_GOLDEN_ANGLE + angleOffset : Math.random() * Math.PI * 2.0;
			double sequenceValue = (sequenceIndex + 1L) * 0.7548776662466927;
			double radialFraction = deterministicRadial ? sequenceValue - Math.floor(sequenceValue) : Math.random();
			boolean isSolidCore = deterministicRadial ? Math.floorMod(sequenceIndex, 10L) != 0L : i < solidSamples;
			double d = isSolidCore
				? (Math.sqrt(radialFraction) * solidR)
				: (solidR + (radialFraction * edgeWidth));

			int trigIndex = fastTrigIndex(a);
			int bx = Mth.floor(cx + COS_LOOKUP[trigIndex] * d);
			int bz = Mth.floor(cz + SIN_LOOKUP[trigIndex] * d);

			if (!claimTerraformChunk(terraformCoordinator, terraformGameTime, level, entity, bx, bz, activeTerraformers)) continue;
			if (!claimTerraformColumn(terraformCoordinator, terraformGameTime, level, entity, bx, bz, activeTerraformers)) continue;
			if (!markColumnVisitedThisTick(level, bx, bz, tickEpoch)) continue;
			samplesExecuted++;
			int infectionResult = infectRod3DBlock(level, bx, bz, scanTop, gy, curR, isSolidCore, false, false, infectionPos);
			if (infectionResult == 0) markTerraformColumnComplete(terraformCoordinator, terraformGameTime, level, bx, bz);
		}
		if (deterministicRadial) nbt.putLong("verdant_radial_sample_cursor", radialCursor + samplesExamined);
		if (superchargedRadial && samplesExamined < totalSamples) {
			long totalDebt = (long) Math.max(0, persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_radial_work_debt", 0)) + (totalSamples - samplesExamined);
			nbt.putInt("verdant_radial_work_debt", (int) Math.min(SUPERCHARGED_RADIAL_DEBT_CAP, totalDebt));
		}
		nbt.putInt("verdant_radial_executed_samples", samplesExecuted);
		nbt.putLong("verdant_radial_elapsed_us", Math.max(0L, (System.nanoTime() - radialWorkStartNanos) / 1000L));
	}

	private static void handleBurstInfectionSpill(ServerLevel level, Entity entity, CompoundTag nbt, double cx, double gy, double cz, int pounds) {
		boolean hasCeiling = isCeiledDimension(level);
		double lockedY = persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_locked_y", gy + getHoverTargetAltitude(nbt));
		boolean epicenterBlocked = hasCeiling && isEpicenterBlockedByIndestructibleBarrier(level, cx, gy, cz, lockedY);
		if (epicenterBlocked) return;

		boolean supercharged = persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_supercharged_mode", false);
		double speedMult = getInfectionSpeedMultiplier(nbt) * (supercharged ? 3.0 : 1.0);
		int burstTicks = persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_burst_spread_ticks", 0);
		int queuedBurstWork = persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_burst_work_debt", 0);
		if (burstTicks <= 0 && queuedBurstWork <= 0) return;
		boolean activeBurst = burstTicks > 0;
		if (activeBurst) {
			burstTicks--;
			nbt.putInt("verdant_burst_spread_ticks", burstTicks);
		}

		double maxInfR = persistentBoolean(nbt, "verdant_supercharged_mode", false) ? (SUPERCHARGED_MAX_INFECTION_RADIUS) : MAX_INFECTION_RADIUS;
		double targetR = Math.min(maxInfR, persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_burst_target_radius", 0.0));
		double curR = Math.min(maxInfR, persistentDouble(nbt, "verdant_infection_radius", 0.0));
		double stepR = activeBurst
			? Math.min(maxInfR, curR + (((targetR - curR) * (1.0 / (burstTicks + 1))) * speedMult))
			: curR;
		nbt.putDouble("verdant_infection_radius", stepR);

		double scanTop = hasCeiling
			? Math.min(level.getMaxBuildHeight() - 4.0, gy + 15.0)
			: (level.getMaxBuildHeight() - 4.0);
		int tickEpoch = (int) level.getGameTime();

		int requestedBurstSamples = activeBurst
			? (int) Math.min(600, Math.max(150, (2.0 * Math.PI * stepR * 1.8) * speedMult))
			: 0;
		int activeTerraformers = getActiveTerraformingEngineCount(level);
		nbt.putInt("verdant_active_terraformer_count", activeTerraformers);
		int burstSamples;
		if (supercharged && activeTerraformers <= 1) {
			long combinedWork = (long) Math.max(0, queuedBurstWork) + requestedBurstSamples;
			burstSamples = (int) Math.min(SUPERCHARGED_BURST_DEBT_CAP, combinedWork);
			nbt.remove("verdant_burst_work_debt");
		} else {
			burstSamples = claimTerraformingSampleBudget(nbt, "verdant_burst_work_debt",
				requestedBurstSamples, MULTI_ENGINE_BURST_COLUMN_BUDGET, activeTerraformers);
		}
		if (burstSamples <= 0) return;
		int solidSamples = (int) (burstSamples * 0.90);
		double solidR = Math.max(0.0, stepR - 2.5);
		long burstCursor = persistentLong(nbt, "verdant_supercharged_burst_cursor", 0L);
		long burstDeadline = supercharged ? System.nanoTime() + SUPERCHARGED_BURST_TIME_BUDGET_NANOS : Long.MAX_VALUE;
		int samplesExamined = 0;
		BlockPos.MutableBlockPos infectionPos = new BlockPos.MutableBlockPos();
		TerraformWorkCoordinator terraformCoordinator = getTerraformCoordinator(level);
		long terraformGameTime = level.getGameTime();
		double edgeWidth = Math.max(0.1, stepR - solidR);

		for (int i = 0; i < burstSamples; i++) {
			if (supercharged && System.nanoTime() >= burstDeadline) break;
			samplesExamined++;
			long sequenceIndex = Math.floorMod(burstCursor + i, 1_000_000_007L);
			double a = supercharged ? sequenceIndex * RADIAL_GOLDEN_ANGLE : Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
			double sequenceValue = (sequenceIndex + 1L) * 0.7548776662466927;
			double radialFraction = supercharged ? sequenceValue - Math.floor(sequenceValue) : Math.random();
			boolean isSolidCore = i < solidSamples;

			double d = isSolidCore
				? (Math.sqrt(radialFraction) * solidR)
				: (solidR + (radialFraction * edgeWidth));

			int trigIndex = fastTrigIndex(a);
			int bx = Mth.floor(cx + COS_LOOKUP[trigIndex] * d);
			int bz = Mth.floor(cz + SIN_LOOKUP[trigIndex] * d);

			if (!claimTerraformChunk(terraformCoordinator, terraformGameTime, level, entity, bx, bz, activeTerraformers)) continue;
			if (!claimTerraformColumn(terraformCoordinator, terraformGameTime, level, entity, bx, bz, activeTerraformers)) continue;
			if (!markColumnVisitedThisTick(level, bx, bz, tickEpoch)) continue;
			int infectionResult = infectRod3DBlock(level, bx, bz, scanTop, gy, stepR, isSolidCore, true, activeBurst, infectionPos);
			if (infectionResult == 0) markTerraformColumnComplete(terraformCoordinator, terraformGameTime, level, bx, bz);
		}
		if (supercharged) {
			nbt.putLong("verdant_supercharged_burst_cursor", burstCursor + samplesExamined);
			int unfinishedSamples = burstSamples - samplesExamined;
			if (unfinishedSamples > 0) {
				long totalDebt = (long) Math.max(0, persistentInt(nbt, "verdant_burst_work_debt", 0)) + unfinishedSamples;
				nbt.putInt("verdant_burst_work_debt", (int) Math.min(SUPERCHARGED_BURST_DEBT_CAP, totalDebt));
			}
		}
	}

	private static Block CACHED_PETRIFIED_OAK = null;
	private static Block CACHED_LIGNUM_CARO = null;
	private static Block CACHED_FALSE_OAK_PLANKS = null;

	private static BlockState CACHED_PETRIFIED_OAK_STATE = null;
	private static BlockState CACHED_LIGNUM_CARO_STATE = null;
	private static BlockState CACHED_FALSE_OAK_PLANKS_STATE = null;
	private static BlockState CACHED_OAK_PLANKS_STATE = null;
	private static BlockParticleOption CACHED_OAK_PLANKS_PARTICLE = null;

	private static final java.util.Map<Block, Boolean> IS_LEAF_CACHE = new java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap<>();
	private static final java.util.Map<Block, Boolean> IS_ORE_CACHE = new java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap<>();
	private static final java.util.Map<Block, Boolean> IS_FOLIAGE_CACHE = new java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap<>();
	private static final java.util.Map<Block, Boolean> IS_SAND_CACHE = new java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap<>();
	private static final java.util.Map<Block, Boolean> IS_FIRE_CACHE = new java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap<>();

	private static final int INFECTION_BLOCK_FLAGS = 18;

	private static BlockState getPetrifiedOakState() {
		if (CACHED_PETRIFIED_OAK_STATE == null) {
			CACHED_PETRIFIED_OAK_STATE = getPetrifiedOakBlock().defaultBlockState();
		}
		return CACHED_PETRIFIED_OAK_STATE;
	}

	private static BlockState getLignumCaroState() {
		if (CACHED_LIGNUM_CARO_STATE == null) {
			CACHED_LIGNUM_CARO_STATE = getLignumCaroBlock().defaultBlockState();
		}
		return CACHED_LIGNUM_CARO_STATE;
	}

	private static BlockState getFalseOakPlanksState() {
		if (CACHED_FALSE_OAK_PLANKS_STATE == null) {
			CACHED_FALSE_OAK_PLANKS_STATE = getFalseOakPlanksBlock().defaultBlockState();
		}
		return CACHED_FALSE_OAK_PLANKS_STATE;
	}

	private static BlockState getOakPlanksState() {
		if (CACHED_OAK_PLANKS_STATE == null) {
			CACHED_OAK_PLANKS_STATE = Blocks.OAK_PLANKS.defaultBlockState();
		}
		return CACHED_OAK_PLANKS_STATE;
	}

	private static BlockParticleOption getOakPlanksParticle() {
		if (CACHED_OAK_PLANKS_PARTICLE == null) {
			CACHED_OAK_PLANKS_PARTICLE = new BlockParticleOption(ParticleTypes.BLOCK, getOakPlanksState());
		}
		return CACHED_OAK_PLANKS_PARTICLE;
	}

	private static ParticleOptions CACHED_BIG_SMOKE_PARTICLE = null;

	private static ParticleOptions getBigSmokeParticle() {
		if (CACHED_BIG_SMOKE_PARTICLE == null) {
			try {
				ResourceLocation loc = ResourceLocation.tryParse("the_backwoods:big_smoke");
				if (loc != null && BuiltInRegistries.PARTICLE_TYPE.containsKey(loc)) {
					Object p = BuiltInRegistries.PARTICLE_TYPE.get(loc);
					if (p instanceof ParticleOptions po) {
						CACHED_BIG_SMOKE_PARTICLE = po;
					}
				}
			} catch (Throwable ignored) {}
			if (CACHED_BIG_SMOKE_PARTICLE == null) {
				CACHED_BIG_SMOKE_PARTICLE = ParticleTypes.LARGE_SMOKE;
			}
		}
		return CACHED_BIG_SMOKE_PARTICLE;
	}

	private static final java.util.Map<Block, Boolean> IS_CACTUS_CACHE = new java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap<>();
	private static final java.util.Map<Block, Boolean> IS_SANDSTONE_CACHE = new java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap<>();

	private static boolean isLeafBlock(Block block, BlockState state) {
		if (block == null || state == null) return false;
		Boolean cached = IS_LEAF_CACHE.get(block);
		if (cached != null) return cached;
		boolean isLeaf = state.is(BlockTags.LEAVES)
			|| block instanceof net.minecraft.world.level.block.LeavesBlock
			|| block == Blocks.CHERRY_LEAVES
			|| block == Blocks.OAK_LEAVES
			|| block == Blocks.SPRUCE_LEAVES
			|| block == Blocks.BIRCH_LEAVES
			|| block == Blocks.JUNGLE_LEAVES
			|| block == Blocks.ACACIA_LEAVES
			|| block == Blocks.DARK_OAK_LEAVES
			|| block == Blocks.MANGROVE_LEAVES
			|| block == Blocks.AZALEA_LEAVES
			|| block == Blocks.FLOWERING_AZALEA_LEAVES
			|| state.is(BlockTags.WART_BLOCKS)
			|| block == Blocks.NETHER_WART_BLOCK
			|| block == Blocks.WARPED_WART_BLOCK
			|| block == Blocks.SHROOMLIGHT;
		if (!isLeaf) {
			String path = BuiltInRegistries.BLOCK.getKey(block).getPath().toLowerCase();
			String simpleName = block.getClass().getSimpleName().toLowerCase();
			isLeaf = path.contains("leaves") || path.contains("leaf") || path.contains("foliage") || path.contains("wart_block")
				|| path.contains("shroomlight") || simpleName.contains("leaves") || simpleName.contains("leaf");
		}
		IS_LEAF_CACHE.put(block, isLeaf);
		return isLeaf;
	}

	private static final java.util.Map<Block, Boolean> IS_ANCIENT_DEBRIS_CACHE = new java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap<>();

	private static boolean isAncientDebris(BlockState state, Block block) {
		if (block == Blocks.ANCIENT_DEBRIS) return true;
		Boolean cached = IS_ANCIENT_DEBRIS_CACHE.get(block);
		if (cached != null) return cached;
		String path = BuiltInRegistries.BLOCK.getKey(block).getPath().toLowerCase();
		boolean result = path.contains("ancient_debris");
		IS_ANCIENT_DEBRIS_CACHE.put(block, result);
		return result;
	}

	private static boolean isOreBlock(Block block, BlockState state) {
		if (isAncientDebris(state, block)) return false;
		Boolean cached = IS_ORE_CACHE.get(block);
		if (cached != null) return cached;
		boolean isOre = state.is(BlockTags.COAL_ORES) || state.is(BlockTags.IRON_ORES) || state.is(BlockTags.COPPER_ORES)
			|| state.is(BlockTags.GOLD_ORES) || state.is(BlockTags.REDSTONE_ORES) || state.is(BlockTags.LAPIS_ORES)
			|| state.is(BlockTags.DIAMOND_ORES) || state.is(BlockTags.EMERALD_ORES);
		if (!isOre) {
			String path = BuiltInRegistries.BLOCK.getKey(block).getPath();
			isOre = path.endsWith("_ore") || path.contains("ore_");
		}
		IS_ORE_CACHE.put(block, isOre);
		return isOre;
	}

	private static final java.util.Map<Block, Boolean> IS_ALREADY_INFECTED_CACHE = new java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap<>(256);

	private static boolean isAlreadyInfectedBlock(Block block) {
		if (block == null) return false;
		if (isGungnirBlock(block)) return false;
		if (block == Blocks.OAK_PLANKS || block == Blocks.OAK_LOG || block == Blocks.OAK_WOOD
				|| block == getPetrifiedOakBlock() || block == getSplinteredOakBlock()
				|| block == getLignumCaroBlock() || block == getFalseOakPlanksBlock()) return true;
		Boolean cached = IS_ALREADY_INFECTED_CACHE.get(block);
		if (cached != null) return cached;
		boolean result = (block == Blocks.OAK_PLANKS || block == Blocks.OAK_LOG || block == Blocks.OAK_WOOD ||
			block == Blocks.OAK_FENCE || block == Blocks.OAK_SLAB || block == Blocks.OAK_STAIRS || block == Blocks.OAK_TRAPDOOR ||
			block == Blocks.SPRUCE_PLANKS || block == Blocks.SPRUCE_LOG || block == Blocks.SPRUCE_WOOD ||
			block == Blocks.SPRUCE_SLAB || block == Blocks.SPRUCE_STAIRS || block == Blocks.SPRUCE_TRAPDOOR ||
			block == Blocks.SPRUCE_FENCE || block == Blocks.PETRIFIED_OAK_SLAB ||
			block == getPetrifiedOakBlock() || block == getSplinteredOakBlock() ||
			block == getLignumCaroBlock() || block == getFalseOakPlanksBlock());
		if (!result) {
			ResourceLocation loc = BuiltInRegistries.BLOCK.getKey(block);
			if (loc != null) {
				String ns = loc.getNamespace().toLowerCase();
				String path = loc.getPath().toLowerCase();
				result = ns.contains("backwood") || path.contains("backwood") || path.contains("petrified") ||
					   path.contains("lignum_caro") || path.contains("splintered") || path.contains("false_oak") ||
					   path.contains("scandere_lignum");
			}
		}
		IS_ALREADY_INFECTED_CACHE.put(block, result);
		return result;
	}

	private static boolean isFoliageOrDebris(BlockState state, Block block) {
		if (block == getFalseOakPlanksBlock()) return true;
		ResourceLocation reg = BuiltInRegistries.BLOCK.getKey(block);
		if (reg != null && reg.getPath().contains("false_oak")) return true;
		if (isLeafBlock(block, state)) return false;
		Boolean cached = IS_FOLIAGE_CACHE.get(block);
		if (cached != null) return cached;

		boolean result = false;
		if (state.is(BlockTags.FLOWERS) || state.is(BlockTags.CROPS) || state.is(BlockTags.SAPLINGS)
			|| block == Blocks.SHORT_GRASS || block == Blocks.TALL_GRASS || block == Blocks.FERN || block == Blocks.LARGE_FERN
			|| block == Blocks.DEAD_BUSH || block == Blocks.SWEET_BERRY_BUSH || block == Blocks.AZALEA || block == Blocks.FLOWERING_AZALEA || block == Blocks.PITCHER_PLANT || block == Blocks.PITCHER_CROP || block == Blocks.TORCHFLOWER || block == Blocks.TORCHFLOWER_CROP || block == Blocks.PINK_PETALS || block == Blocks.LILY_PAD || block == Blocks.SMALL_DRIPLEAF || block == Blocks.BIG_DRIPLEAF || block == Blocks.BIG_DRIPLEAF_STEM || block == Blocks.BROWN_MUSHROOM || block == Blocks.RED_MUSHROOM || block == Blocks.BROWN_MUSHROOM_BLOCK || block == Blocks.RED_MUSHROOM_BLOCK || block == Blocks.MOSS_BLOCK || block == Blocks.MOSS_CARPET || block == Blocks.VINE || block == Blocks.HANGING_ROOTS || block == Blocks.SNOW
			|| block == Blocks.CRIMSON_ROOTS || block == Blocks.WARPED_ROOTS
			|| block == Blocks.NETHER_SPROUTS || block == Blocks.WEEPING_VINES || block == Blocks.WEEPING_VINES_PLANT || block == Blocks.TWISTING_VINES
			|| block == Blocks.TWISTING_VINES_PLANT || block == Blocks.CRIMSON_FUNGUS || block == Blocks.WARPED_FUNGUS || block == Blocks.SUGAR_CANE
			|| block == Blocks.BAMBOO || block == Blocks.BAMBOO_SAPLING) {
			result = true;
		} else {
			String path = BuiltInRegistries.BLOCK.getKey(block).getPath().toLowerCase();
			result = (path.contains("leaf") || path.contains("bush") || path.contains("plant") || path.contains("flower")
				|| path.contains("dry_grass") || path.contains("short_dry_grass") || path.contains("shrub") || path.contains("berry") || path.contains("crop")
				|| path.contains("herb") || path.contains("sprout") || path.contains("root") || path.contains("vine")
				|| path.contains("weed") || path.contains("petal") || path.contains("moss") || path.contains("foliage")
				|| path.contains("flora") || path.contains("fern") || path.contains("firefly")) && !path.contains("cactus") && !path.contains("cacti") && !path.contains("chorus");
		}
		IS_FOLIAGE_CACHE.put(block, result);
		return result;
	}

	private static boolean isCactus(BlockState state, Block block) {
		if (block == Blocks.CACTUS) return true;
		Boolean cached = IS_CACTUS_CACHE.get(block);
		if (cached != null) return cached;
		String path = BuiltInRegistries.BLOCK.getKey(block).getPath().toLowerCase();
		boolean result = path.contains("cactus") || path.contains("cacti");
		IS_CACTUS_CACHE.put(block, result);
		return result;
	}

	private static boolean isSandstone(BlockState state, Block block) {
		if (block == Blocks.SANDSTONE || block == Blocks.RED_SANDSTONE || block == Blocks.SMOOTH_SANDSTONE
			|| block == Blocks.CUT_SANDSTONE || block == Blocks.CHISELED_SANDSTONE || block == Blocks.SMOOTH_RED_SANDSTONE
			|| block == Blocks.CUT_RED_SANDSTONE || block == Blocks.CHISELED_RED_SANDSTONE) {
			return true;
		}
		Boolean cached = IS_SANDSTONE_CACHE.get(block);
		if (cached != null) return cached;
		String path = BuiltInRegistries.BLOCK.getKey(block).getPath().toLowerCase();
		boolean result = path.contains("sandstone") || path.contains("red_sandstone");
		IS_SANDSTONE_CACHE.put(block, result);
		return result;
	}

	private static boolean isAnySand(BlockState state, Block block) {
		Boolean cached = IS_SAND_CACHE.get(block);
		if (cached != null) return cached;

		boolean result = false;
		if (state.is(BlockTags.SAND) || block == Blocks.SAND || block == Blocks.RED_SAND || block == Blocks.SUSPICIOUS_SAND || block == Blocks.SOUL_SAND) {
			result = true;
		} else {
			String path = BuiltInRegistries.BLOCK.getKey(block).getPath().toLowerCase();
			result = path.contains("sand") && !path.contains("sandstone");
		}
		IS_SAND_CACHE.put(block, result);
		return result;
	}

	private static Block getPetrifiedOakBlock() {
		if (CACHED_PETRIFIED_OAK == null) {
			CACHED_PETRIFIED_OAK = getRegisteredBlock("the_backwoods:petrified_oak_planks", null);
			if (CACHED_PETRIFIED_OAK == null) {
				CACHED_PETRIFIED_OAK = getRegisteredBlock("thebackwoods:petrified_oak_planks", Blocks.OAK_PLANKS);
			}
		}
		return CACHED_PETRIFIED_OAK;
	}

	private static volatile Block CACHED_SPLINTERED_OAK = null;
	private static volatile BlockState CACHED_SPLINTERED_OAK_STATE = null;

	private static Block getSplinteredOakBlock() {
		if (CACHED_SPLINTERED_OAK == null) {
			CACHED_SPLINTERED_OAK = getRegisteredBlock("the_backwoods:splintered_oak_planks", null);
			if (CACHED_SPLINTERED_OAK == null) {
				CACHED_SPLINTERED_OAK = getRegisteredBlock("thebackwoods:splintered_oak_planks", Blocks.OAK_PLANKS);
			}
		}
		return CACHED_SPLINTERED_OAK;
	}

	private static BlockState getSplinteredOakState() {
		if (CACHED_SPLINTERED_OAK_STATE == null) {
			CACHED_SPLINTERED_OAK_STATE = getSplinteredOakBlock().defaultBlockState();
		}
		return CACHED_SPLINTERED_OAK_STATE;
	}

	private static Block getLignumCaroBlock() {
		if (CACHED_LIGNUM_CARO == null) {
			CACHED_LIGNUM_CARO = getRegisteredBlock("the_backwoods:lignum_caro", null);
			if (CACHED_LIGNUM_CARO == null) {
				CACHED_LIGNUM_CARO = getRegisteredBlock("thebackwoods:lignum_caro", Blocks.OAK_PLANKS);
			}
		}
		return CACHED_LIGNUM_CARO;
	}

	private static Block getFalseOakPlanksBlock() {
		if (CACHED_FALSE_OAK_PLANKS == null) {
			CACHED_FALSE_OAK_PLANKS = getRegisteredBlock("the_backwoods:false_oak_planks", null);
			if (CACHED_FALSE_OAK_PLANKS == null) {
				CACHED_FALSE_OAK_PLANKS = getRegisteredBlock("thebackwoods:false_oak_planks", Blocks.OAK_PLANKS);
			}
		}
		return CACHED_FALSE_OAK_PLANKS;
	}

	private static final long[] CELLULAR_CACHE_KEYS = new long[2048];
	private static final byte[] CELLULAR_CACHE_VALS = new byte[2048];

	private static boolean isCellularMembrane(double x, double z, long seed) {
		int bx = Mth.floor(x);
		int bz = Mth.floor(z);
		long key = (((long) bx) << 32) | (bz & 0xFFFFFFFFL);
		int mask = 2047;
		int idx = (int) (key ^ (key >>> 16) ^ (key >>> 32)) & mask;
		if (CELLULAR_CACHE_KEYS[idx] == key) {
			byte val = CELLULAR_CACHE_VALS[idx];
			if (val == 2) return true;
			if (val == 1) return false;
		}

		boolean result = computeCellularMembrane(x, z, seed);
		CELLULAR_CACHE_KEYS[idx] = key;
		CELLULAR_CACHE_VALS[idx] = (byte) (result ? 2 : 1);
		return result;
	}

	private static boolean computeCellularMembrane(double x, double z, long seed) {
		double baseScale = 0.026;
		double thickness = 0.045;
		double macroNoise = Math.sin(x * (baseScale * 0.1) + seed) * Math.cos(z * (baseScale * 0.1) - seed);
		double activeScale = baseScale + (macroNoise * (baseScale * 0.3));

		double scaledX = x * activeScale;
		double scaledZ = z * activeScale;

		int cellX = (int) Math.floor(scaledX);
		int cellZ = (int) Math.floor(scaledZ);

		double minDistance1 = Double.MAX_VALUE;
		double minDistance2 = Double.MAX_VALUE;

		for (int i = -1; i <= 1; i++) {
			for (int j = -1; j <= 1; j++) {
				int targetGridX = cellX + i;
				int targetGridZ = cellZ + j;

				long hash = hashCoords(targetGridX, targetGridZ, seed);
				double offsetX = ((hash & 0xFFF) / 4095.0);
				double offsetZ = (((hash >> 12) & 0xFFF) / 4095.0);

				double warpX = Math.sin(targetGridZ * 2.0 + seed) * 0.35;
				double warpZ = Math.cos(targetGridX * 2.0 - seed) * 0.35;

				double featurePointX = targetGridX + offsetX + warpX;
				double featurePointZ = targetGridZ + offsetZ + warpZ;

				double diffX = scaledX - featurePointX;
				double diffZ = scaledZ - featurePointZ;
				double distSq = diffX * diffX + diffZ * diffZ;

				if (distSq < minDistance1) {
					minDistance2 = minDistance1;
					minDistance1 = distSq;
				} else if (distSq < minDistance2) {
					minDistance2 = distSq;
				}
			}
		}

		double boundaryField = Math.sqrt(minDistance2) - Math.sqrt(minDistance1);
		double localWobble = Math.sin(x * 0.1) * Math.cos(z * 0.1);
		double thicknessThreshold = thickness + (localWobble * (thickness * 0.3));

		return boundaryField < thicknessThreshold;
	}

	private static long hashCoords(int x, int z, long seed) {
		long h = seed + x * 3129841L + z * 116129781L;
		h = (h ^ (h >>> 25)) * 268435459L;
		return h;
	}

	private static boolean isCopperBulb(BlockState state, Block block) {
		return block == Blocks.COPPER_BULB || block == Blocks.EXPOSED_COPPER_BULB ||
			block == Blocks.WEATHERED_COPPER_BULB || block == Blocks.OXIDIZED_COPPER_BULB ||
			block == Blocks.WAXED_COPPER_BULB || block == Blocks.WAXED_EXPOSED_COPPER_BULB ||
			block == Blocks.WAXED_WEATHERED_COPPER_BULB || block == Blocks.WAXED_OXIDIZED_COPPER_BULB ||
			BuiltInRegistries.BLOCK.getKey(block).getPath().contains("copper_bulb");
	}

	private static boolean isCopperBlock(BlockState state, Block block) {
		if (isCopperBulb(state, block)) return false;
		return block instanceof net.minecraft.world.level.block.WeatheringCopper ||
			BuiltInRegistries.BLOCK.getKey(block).getPath().contains("copper");
	}

	private static final java.util.Map<Block, Boolean> IS_STONE_CACHE = new java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap<>();
	private static final java.util.Map<Block, Boolean> IS_STORAGE_CACHE = new java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap<>();

	private static boolean isStoneOrBrickOrMineral(BlockState state, Block block) {
		Boolean cached = IS_STONE_CACHE.get(block);
		if (cached != null) return cached;
		boolean result = false;
		if (state.is(BlockTags.BASE_STONE_OVERWORLD) || state.is(BlockTags.BASE_STONE_NETHER) ||
			state.is(BlockTags.STONE_BRICKS) || state.is(BlockTags.TERRACOTTA) || state.is(BlockTags.WALLS)) {
			result = true;
		} else {
			String path = BuiltInRegistries.BLOCK.getKey(block).getPath();
			result = path.contains("tuff") || path.contains("brick") || path.contains("deepslate") ||
				path.contains("stone") || path.contains("prismarine") || path.contains("basalt") ||
				path.contains("blackstone") || path.contains("granite") || path.contains("diorite") ||
				path.contains("andesite") || path.contains("calcite") || path.contains("dripstone") ||
				path.contains("amethyst") || path.contains("obsidian") || path.contains("concrete") ||
				path.contains("terracotta") || path.contains("quartz") || path.contains("purpur") ||
				path.contains("sandstone") || path.contains("cobble") || path.contains("mud") ||
				path.contains("sculk") || path.contains("sponge") || path.contains("coral") ||
				path.contains("glowstone") || path.contains("shroomlight");
		}
		IS_STONE_CACHE.put(block, result);
		return result;
	}

	private static boolean isStorageOrSpawner(Block block) {
		if (block == Blocks.SPAWNER) return true;
		Boolean cached = IS_STORAGE_CACHE.get(block);
		if (cached != null) return cached;
		boolean result = false;
		try {
			ResourceLocation loc = BuiltInRegistries.BLOCK.getKey(block);
			if (loc != null) {
				String path = loc.getPath().toLowerCase();
				if (path.contains("spawner") || path.contains("chest") || path.contains("barrel") ||
					path.contains("shulker") || path.contains("dispenser") || path.contains("dropper") ||
					path.contains("hopper") || path.contains("furnace") || path.contains("smoker") ||
					path.contains("brewing") || path.contains("crafter") || path.contains("vault") ||
					path.contains("decorated_pot") || path.contains("chiseled_bookshelf")) {
					result = true;
				}
			}
		} catch (Exception ignored) {}
		if (!result) {
			String className = block.getClass().getSimpleName().toLowerCase();
			if (className.contains("spawner") || className.contains("chest") || className.contains("barrel") ||
				className.contains("shulker") || className.contains("dispenser") || className.contains("dropper") ||
				className.contains("hopper") || className.contains("furnace") || className.contains("smoker") ||
				className.contains("brewing") || className.contains("crafter") || className.contains("storage") ||
				className.contains("pot") || className.contains("bookshelf")) {
				result = true;
			}
		}
		IS_STORAGE_CACHE.put(block, result);
		return result;
	}

	private static final byte CAT_IGNORED = 1;
	private static final byte CAT_SLAB = 2;
	private static final byte CAT_STAIRS = 3;
	private static final byte CAT_TRAPDOOR = 4;
	private static final byte CAT_LOG = 5;
	private static final byte CAT_FENCE = 6;
	private static final byte CAT_CHORUS = 7;
	private static final byte CAT_FOLIAGE = 8;
	private static final byte CAT_LEAVES = 9;
	private static final byte CAT_CACTUS = 10;
	private static final byte CAT_MINERAL_STONE = 11;
	private static final byte CAT_TERRAIN_SOIL = 12;
	private static final byte CAT_FALLBACK = 13;

	private static final java.util.Map<Block, Byte> BLOCK_CATEGORY_CACHE = new java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap<>(512);
	private static final java.util.Map<net.minecraft.world.level.biome.Biome, Byte> BIOME_TYPE_CACHE = new java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap<>(128);

	private static byte getBiomeType(Holder<net.minecraft.world.level.biome.Biome> biomeHolder) {
		if (biomeHolder == null) return 0;
		net.minecraft.world.level.biome.Biome biome = biomeHolder.value();
		Byte cached = BIOME_TYPE_CACHE.get(biome);
		if (cached != null) return cached;

		ResourceLocation biomeKey = biomeHolder.unwrapKey().map(ResourceKey::location).orElse(null);
		String biomePath = biomeKey != null ? biomeKey.getPath().toLowerCase() : "";
		boolean deep = biomePath.contains("deep_backwoods") || biomeHolder.is(net.minecraft.tags.BiomeTags.IS_TAIGA) ||
			biomeHolder.is(net.minecraft.tags.BiomeTags.IS_JUNGLE) || biomePath.contains("spruce") || biomePath.contains("taiga") ||
			biomePath.contains("jungle") || biomePath.contains("swamp") || biomePath.contains("dark_forest") || biomePath.contains("snow") || biomePath.contains("cold");
		boolean thicket = !deep && (biomePath.contains("the_thicket") || biomeHolder.is(net.minecraft.tags.BiomeTags.IS_FOREST) ||
			biomePath.contains("forest") || biomePath.contains("birch"));

		byte result = deep ? (byte) 1 : (thicket ? (byte) 2 : (byte) 0);
		BIOME_TYPE_CACHE.put(biome, result);
		return result;
	}

	private static byte computeBlockCategory(Block block, BlockState state) {
		if (isAlreadyInfectedBlock(block)) return CAT_IGNORED;
		if (block == Blocks.ANCIENT_DEBRIS || isAncientDebris(state, block)) return CAT_IGNORED;
		if (isStorageOrSpawner(block)) return CAT_IGNORED;

		if (block instanceof net.minecraft.world.level.block.TorchBlock || block instanceof net.minecraft.world.level.block.WallTorchBlock ||
			block instanceof net.minecraft.world.level.block.RedStoneWireBlock || isCopperBulb(state, block) ||
			state.is(BlockTags.RAILS) || block instanceof net.minecraft.world.level.block.BaseRailBlock ||
			block == Blocks.RAIL || block == Blocks.POWERED_RAIL || block == Blocks.DETECTOR_RAIL || block == Blocks.ACTIVATOR_RAIL) {
			return CAT_IGNORED;
		}

		if (state.is(BlockTags.SLABS) || block instanceof net.minecraft.world.level.block.SlabBlock) return CAT_SLAB;
		if (state.is(BlockTags.STAIRS) || block instanceof net.minecraft.world.level.block.StairBlock) return CAT_STAIRS;
		if (state.is(BlockTags.TRAPDOORS) || block instanceof net.minecraft.world.level.block.TrapDoorBlock) return CAT_TRAPDOOR;
		if (state.is(BlockTags.LOGS) || block instanceof net.minecraft.world.level.block.RotatedPillarBlock) return CAT_LOG;
		if (state.is(BlockTags.FENCES) || block instanceof net.minecraft.world.level.block.FenceBlock || block == Blocks.IRON_BARS || block == Blocks.CHAIN) return CAT_FENCE;

		if (block == Blocks.CHORUS_PLANT || block == Blocks.CHORUS_FLOWER ||
			(BuiltInRegistries.BLOCK.getKey(block) != null && BuiltInRegistries.BLOCK.getKey(block).getPath().contains("chorus"))) {
			return CAT_CHORUS;
		}

		if (isFoliageOrDebris(state, block)) return CAT_FOLIAGE;
		if (isLeafBlock(block, state)) return CAT_LEAVES;
		if (isCactus(state, block)) return CAT_CACTUS;

		if (isCopperBlock(state, block) || isOreBlock(block, state) || isSandstone(state, block) || isStoneOrBrickOrMineral(state, block) ||
			state.getFluidState().is(FluidTags.LAVA) || block == Blocks.LAVA) {
			return CAT_MINERAL_STONE;
		}

		if (state.is(BlockTags.DIRT) || state.is(BlockTags.ICE) || block == Blocks.END_STONE ||
			block == Blocks.NETHERRACK || block == Blocks.CRIMSON_NYLIUM || block == Blocks.WARPED_NYLIUM ||
			block == Blocks.SOUL_SAND || block == Blocks.SOUL_SOIL || block == Blocks.MAGMA_BLOCK ||
			isAnySand(state, block) || block == Blocks.GRAVEL || block == Blocks.SUSPICIOUS_GRAVEL ||
			!state.getFluidState().isEmpty() || block == Blocks.WATER || block == Blocks.BUBBLE_COLUMN ||
			block == Blocks.SEAGRASS || block == Blocks.TALL_SEAGRASS || block == Blocks.KELP || block == Blocks.KELP_PLANT) {
			return CAT_TERRAIN_SOIL;
		}

		return CAT_FALLBACK;
	}

	private static byte getBlockCategory(Block block, BlockState state) {
		Byte cached = BLOCK_CATEGORY_CACHE.get(block);
		if (cached != null) return cached;
		byte cat = computeBlockCategory(block, state);
		BLOCK_CATEGORY_CACHE.put(block, cat);
		return cat;
	}

	private static boolean replaceBlockWithOakPlague(ServerLevel level, BlockPos pos, BlockState state, boolean breakBedrock) {
		return replaceBlockWithOakPlague(level, pos, state, breakBedrock, isCellularMembrane(pos.getX(), pos.getZ(), level.getSeed()));
	}

	private static boolean isGungnirBlock(Block block) {
		if (block == null) return false;
		try {
			var loc = BuiltInRegistries.BLOCK.getKey(block);
			if (loc != null) {
				String id = loc.toString().toLowerCase();
				if (id.contains("gungnir") || id.contains("shooting_star") || id.contains("shootingstar")) return true;
			}
			String cls = block.getClass().getName().toLowerCase();
			if (cls.contains("gungnir") || cls.contains("shooting_star") || cls.contains("shootingstar")) return true;
		} catch (Exception ignored) {}
		return false;
	}

	private static boolean replaceBlockWithOakPlague(ServerLevel level, BlockPos pos, BlockState state, boolean breakBedrock, boolean isCell) {
		if (state.isAir()) return false;

		Block block = state.getBlock();
		if (isGungnirBlock(block)) {
			level.setBlock(pos, getPetrifiedOakState(), INFECTION_BLOCK_FLAGS);
			return true;
		}

		if (isIndestructibleBlock(state, level, pos)) return false;
		byte cat = getBlockCategory(block, state);
		if (cat == CAT_IGNORED) return false;

		if (cat == CAT_SLAB) {
			byte bType = getBiomeType(level.getBiome(pos));
			boolean isDeepBackwoods = (bType == 1) || block == Blocks.SPRUCE_LOG || block == Blocks.SPRUCE_WOOD || block == Blocks.SPRUCE_LEAVES || block == Blocks.SPRUCE_PLANKS;
			Block targetSlab = isDeepBackwoods ? Blocks.SPRUCE_SLAB : Blocks.PETRIFIED_OAK_SLAB;
			if (block != targetSlab) {
				BlockState slab = targetSlab.defaultBlockState();
				if (state.hasProperty(net.minecraft.world.level.block.SlabBlock.TYPE)) slab = slab.setValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.SlabBlock.TYPE, state.getValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.SlabBlock.TYPE));
				if (state.hasProperty(net.minecraft.world.level.block.SlabBlock.WATERLOGGED)) slab = slab.setValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.SlabBlock.WATERLOGGED, state.getValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.SlabBlock.WATERLOGGED));
				level.setBlock(pos, slab, INFECTION_BLOCK_FLAGS);
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}
		if (cat == CAT_STAIRS) {
			byte bType = getBiomeType(level.getBiome(pos));
			boolean isDeepBackwoods = (bType == 1) || block == Blocks.SPRUCE_LOG || block == Blocks.SPRUCE_WOOD || block == Blocks.SPRUCE_LEAVES || block == Blocks.SPRUCE_PLANKS;
			Block targetStairs = isDeepBackwoods ? Blocks.SPRUCE_STAIRS : Blocks.OAK_STAIRS;
			if (block != targetStairs) {
				BlockState stairs = targetStairs.defaultBlockState();
				if (state.hasProperty(net.minecraft.world.level.block.StairBlock.FACING)) stairs = stairs.setValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.StairBlock.FACING, state.getValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.StairBlock.FACING));
				if (state.hasProperty(net.minecraft.world.level.block.StairBlock.HALF)) stairs = stairs.setValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.StairBlock.HALF, state.getValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.StairBlock.HALF));
				if (state.hasProperty(net.minecraft.world.level.block.StairBlock.SHAPE)) stairs = stairs.setValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.StairBlock.SHAPE, state.getValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.StairBlock.SHAPE));
				if (state.hasProperty(net.minecraft.world.level.block.StairBlock.WATERLOGGED)) stairs = stairs.setValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.StairBlock.WATERLOGGED, state.getValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.StairBlock.WATERLOGGED));
				level.setBlock(pos, stairs, INFECTION_BLOCK_FLAGS);
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}
		if (cat == CAT_TRAPDOOR) {
			byte bType = getBiomeType(level.getBiome(pos));
			boolean isDeepBackwoods = (bType == 1) || block == Blocks.SPRUCE_LOG || block == Blocks.SPRUCE_WOOD || block == Blocks.SPRUCE_LEAVES || block == Blocks.SPRUCE_PLANKS;
			Block targetTrapdoor = isDeepBackwoods ? Blocks.SPRUCE_TRAPDOOR : Blocks.OAK_TRAPDOOR;
			if (block != targetTrapdoor) {
				BlockState trapdoor = targetTrapdoor.defaultBlockState();
				if (state.hasProperty(net.minecraft.world.level.block.TrapDoorBlock.FACING)) trapdoor = trapdoor.setValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.TrapDoorBlock.FACING, state.getValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.TrapDoorBlock.FACING));
				if (state.hasProperty(net.minecraft.world.level.block.TrapDoorBlock.HALF)) trapdoor = trapdoor.setValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.TrapDoorBlock.HALF, state.getValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.TrapDoorBlock.HALF));
				if (state.hasProperty(net.minecraft.world.level.block.TrapDoorBlock.OPEN)) trapdoor = trapdoor.setValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.TrapDoorBlock.OPEN, state.getValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.TrapDoorBlock.OPEN));
				if (state.hasProperty(net.minecraft.world.level.block.TrapDoorBlock.POWERED)) trapdoor = trapdoor.setValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.TrapDoorBlock.POWERED, state.getValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.TrapDoorBlock.POWERED));
				if (state.hasProperty(net.minecraft.world.level.block.TrapDoorBlock.WATERLOGGED)) trapdoor = trapdoor.setValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.TrapDoorBlock.WATERLOGGED, state.getValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.TrapDoorBlock.WATERLOGGED));
				level.setBlock(pos, trapdoor, INFECTION_BLOCK_FLAGS);
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}
		if (cat == CAT_LOG) {
			byte bType = getBiomeType(level.getBiome(pos));
			boolean isDeepBackwoods = (bType == 1) || block == Blocks.SPRUCE_LOG || block == Blocks.SPRUCE_WOOD || block == Blocks.SPRUCE_LEAVES || block == Blocks.SPRUCE_PLANKS;
			Block targetLog = isDeepBackwoods ? Blocks.SPRUCE_LOG : Blocks.OAK_LOG;
			if (block != targetLog) {
				BlockState oakLog = targetLog.defaultBlockState();
				if (state.hasProperty(net.minecraft.world.level.block.RotatedPillarBlock.AXIS)) oakLog = oakLog.setValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.RotatedPillarBlock.AXIS, state.getValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.RotatedPillarBlock.AXIS));
				level.setBlock(pos, oakLog, INFECTION_BLOCK_FLAGS);
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}
		if (cat == CAT_FENCE) {
			byte bType = getBiomeType(level.getBiome(pos));
			boolean isDeepBackwoods = (bType == 1) || block == Blocks.SPRUCE_LOG || block == Blocks.SPRUCE_WOOD || block == Blocks.SPRUCE_LEAVES || block == Blocks.SPRUCE_PLANKS;
			Block targetFence = isDeepBackwoods ? Blocks.SPRUCE_FENCE : Blocks.OAK_FENCE;
			if (block != targetFence) {
				BlockState fence = targetFence.defaultBlockState();
				if (state.hasProperty(net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.properties.BlockStateProperties.NORTH)) fence = fence.setValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.properties.BlockStateProperties.NORTH, state.getValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.properties.BlockStateProperties.NORTH));
				if (state.hasProperty(net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.properties.BlockStateProperties.EAST)) fence = fence.setValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.properties.BlockStateProperties.EAST, state.getValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.properties.BlockStateProperties.EAST));
				if (state.hasProperty(net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.properties.BlockStateProperties.SOUTH)) fence = fence.setValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.properties.BlockStateProperties.SOUTH, state.getValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.properties.BlockStateProperties.SOUTH));
				if (state.hasProperty(net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.properties.BlockStateProperties.WEST)) fence = fence.setValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.properties.BlockStateProperties.WEST, state.getValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.properties.BlockStateProperties.WEST));
				if (state.hasProperty(net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.properties.BlockStateProperties.WATERLOGGED)) fence = fence.setValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.properties.BlockStateProperties.WATERLOGGED, state.getValue(net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.properties.BlockStateProperties.WATERLOGGED));
				fence = Block.updateFromNeighbourShapes(fence, level, pos);
				level.setBlock(pos, fence, 3);
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		if (cat == CAT_CHORUS) {
			level.setBlock(pos, getLignumCaroState(), INFECTION_BLOCK_FLAGS);
			return true;
		}

		if (cat == CAT_FOLIAGE) {
			level.setBlock(pos, Blocks.AIR.defaultBlockState(), INFECTION_BLOCK_FLAGS);
			return false;
		}
		if (cat == CAT_LEAVES) {
			level.setBlock(pos, getFalseOakPlanksState(), INFECTION_BLOCK_FLAGS);
			return true;
		}
		if (cat == CAT_CACTUS) {
			level.setBlock(pos, getSplinteredOakState(), INFECTION_BLOCK_FLAGS);
			return true;
		}

		if (cat == CAT_MINERAL_STONE || (block == Blocks.BEDROCK && breakBedrock && canInfectIndestructible(null))) {
			level.setBlock(pos, getPetrifiedOakState(), INFECTION_BLOCK_FLAGS);
			return true;
		}

		if (cat == CAT_TERRAIN_SOIL) {
			if (isCell) {
				level.setBlock(pos, getLignumCaroState(), INFECTION_BLOCK_FLAGS);
				return true;
			}

			BlockState aboveState = level.getBlockState(pos.above());
			boolean isUnderwater = !state.getFluidState().isEmpty() || !aboveState.getFluidState().isEmpty() ||
				block == Blocks.WATER || block == Blocks.SEAGRASS || block == Blocks.TALL_SEAGRASS || block == Blocks.KELP || block == Blocks.KELP_PLANT;

			boolean isSurface = aboveState.isAir() || isFoliageOrDebris(aboveState, aboveState.getBlock()) || !aboveState.getFluidState().isEmpty();

			byte bType = getBiomeType(level.getBiome(pos));
			boolean isDeepBackwoods = (bType == 1) || block == Blocks.SPRUCE_LOG || block == Blocks.SPRUCE_WOOD || block == Blocks.SPRUCE_LEAVES || block == Blocks.SPRUCE_PLANKS;
			boolean isTheThicket = !isDeepBackwoods && (bType == 2);

			BlockState chosen;
			if (isDeepBackwoods) {
				if (isUnderwater || isSurface) {
					chosen = Blocks.SPRUCE_PLANKS.defaultBlockState();
				} else {
					chosen = Blocks.SPRUCE_LOG.defaultBlockState();
				}
			} else if (isTheThicket) {
				if (isUnderwater) {
					chosen = getPetrifiedOakState();
				} else if (isSurface) {
					chosen = getOakPlanksState();
				} else {
					chosen = Blocks.OAK_LOG.defaultBlockState();
				}
			} else {
				if (isUnderwater) {
					chosen = Blocks.OAK_WOOD.defaultBlockState();
				} else if (isSurface) {
					chosen = getPetrifiedOakState();
				} else {
					chosen = Math.random() < 0.65 ? getPetrifiedOakState() : getLignumCaroState();
				}
			}

			level.setBlock(pos, chosen, INFECTION_BLOCK_FLAGS);
			return true;
		}

		level.setBlock(pos, isCell ? getLignumCaroState() : getPetrifiedOakState(), INFECTION_BLOCK_FLAGS);
		return true;
	}

	private static Block getRegisteredBlock(String id, Block fallback) {
		try {
			ResourceLocation loc = ResourceLocation.parse(id);
			if (BuiltInRegistries.BLOCK.containsKey(loc)) return BuiltInRegistries.BLOCK.get(loc);
		} catch (Exception ignored) {}
		return fallback;
	}

	private static boolean CITADEL_INITIALIZED = false;
	private static Class<?> CITADEL_MSG_CLASS = null;
	private static Class<?> CITADEL_MAIN_CLASS = null;
	private static java.lang.reflect.Method CITADEL_SEND_TO_PLAYER = null;
	private static java.lang.reflect.Method CITADEL_SEND_TO_ALL = null;

	private static void initCitadelReflection() {
		if (CITADEL_INITIALIZED) return;
		CITADEL_INITIALIZED = true;
		try {
			CITADEL_MSG_CLASS = Class.forName("com.github.alexmodguy.citadel.server.message.CameraShakeMessage");
			CITADEL_MAIN_CLASS = Class.forName("com.github.alexmodguy.citadel.Citadel");
			for (java.lang.reflect.Method m : CITADEL_MAIN_CLASS.getMethods()) {
				if (m.getName().equals("sendMSGToPlayer") && m.getParameterCount() == 2) {
					CITADEL_SEND_TO_PLAYER = m;
				} else if (m.getName().equals("sendMSGToAll") && m.getParameterCount() == 1) {
					CITADEL_SEND_TO_ALL = m;
				}
			}
		} catch (Throwable ignored) {}
	}

	private static void sendCitadelCameraShake(float intensity, int duration, float range) {
		sendCitadelCameraShakeAt(null, 0, 0, 0, intensity, duration, range, true);
	}

	private static void sendCitadelCameraShakeAt(ServerLevel level, double x, double y, double z, float maxIntensity, int duration, float maxRadius) {
		sendCitadelCameraShakeAt(level, x, y, z, maxIntensity, duration, maxRadius, false);
	}

	private static void sendCitadelCameraShakeAt(ServerLevel level, double x, double y, double z, float maxIntensity, int duration, float maxRadius, boolean broadcastAll) {
		try {
			if (!CITADEL_INITIALIZED) {
				initCitadelReflection();
			}
			if (CITADEL_MSG_CLASS == null || CITADEL_MAIN_CLASS == null) return;

			if (broadcastAll || level == null) {
				Object msgInstance = createCameraShakeMessage(CITADEL_MSG_CLASS, maxIntensity, duration, maxRadius);
				if (msgInstance != null && CITADEL_SEND_TO_ALL != null) {
					CITADEL_SEND_TO_ALL.invoke(null, msgInstance);
				}
				return;
			}

			double maxRadiusSq = maxRadius * maxRadius;
			for (net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer player : level.players()) {
				double dx = player.getX() - x;
				double dy = player.getY() - y;
				double dz = player.getZ() - z;
				double distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz;
				if (distSq <= maxRadiusSq) {
					double dist = Math.sqrt(distSq);

					float decay = (float) (1.0 - (dist / maxRadius));
					float scaledIntensity = maxIntensity * decay;
					if (scaledIntensity > 0.02F) {
						Object msgInstance = createCameraShakeMessage(CITADEL_MSG_CLASS, scaledIntensity, duration, maxRadius);
						if (msgInstance != null) {
							if (CITADEL_SEND_TO_PLAYER != null) {
								CITADEL_SEND_TO_PLAYER.invoke(null, msgInstance, player);
							} else if (CITADEL_SEND_TO_ALL != null) {
								CITADEL_SEND_TO_ALL.invoke(null, msgInstance);
								break;
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		} catch (Exception ignored) {}
	}

	private static Object createCameraShakeMessage(Class<?> msgClass, float intensity, int duration, float range) {
		for (java.lang.reflect.Constructor<?> c : msgClass.getConstructors()) {
			Class<?>[] p = c.getParameterTypes();
			try {
				if (p.length == 3) {
					if (p[0] == float.class && p[1] == int.class) return c.newInstance(intensity, duration, range);
					if (p[0] == int.class && p[1] == float.class) return c.newInstance(duration, intensity, range);
					if (p[0] == float.class && p[1] == float.class) return c.newInstance(intensity, (float) duration, range);
				} else if (p.length == 2) {
					if (p[0] == float.class && p[1] == int.class) return c.newInstance(intensity, duration);
					if (p[0] == int.class && p[1] == float.class) return c.newInstance(duration, intensity);
					if (p[0] == float.class && p[1] == float.class) return c.newInstance(intensity, (float) duration);
				}
			} catch (Exception ignored) {}
		}
		return null;
	}

	private static boolean isVerdantEngine(Entity entity) {
		if (entity == null) return false;
		String descId = entity.getType().getDescriptionId();
		String className = entity.getClass().getSimpleName();
		return descId.contains("verdant") || className.contains("Verdant");
	}

	private static boolean isVerdantEngineDamage(DamageSource damageSource) {
		return damageSource != null && (isVerdantEngine(damageSource.getEntity()) || isVerdantEngine(damageSource.getDirectEntity()));
	}

	private static void playEngineSound(ServerLevel level, double x, double y, double z, String soundId, float volume, float pitch) {
		try {
			ResourceLocation loc = ResourceLocation.parse(soundId);
			SoundEvent sound = BuiltInRegistries.SOUND_EVENT.get(loc);
			if (sound != null) level.playSound(null, x, y, z, sound, SoundSource.HOSTILE, volume, pitch);
		} catch (Exception ignored) {}
	}

	private static final Map<Block, Boolean> BURNT_CACHE = new java.util.IdentityHashMap<>();

	private static void processShockwaveDominoEffects(ServerLevel level, double ox, double oy, double oz, double radius, double expandSpeed, double scanTopY) {
		int samplePoints = Math.max(18, (int) (2 * Math.PI * radius * 0.6));
		double dr = Math.max(1.5, expandSpeed * 0.6);
		BlockPos.MutableBlockPos p = new BlockPos.MutableBlockPos();

		int lastChunkX = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
		int lastChunkZ = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
		LevelChunk chunk = null;

		for (int i = 0; i < samplePoints; i++) {
			double a = (2 * Math.PI / samplePoints) * i;
			double c = Math.cos(a);
			double s = Math.sin(a);

			for (double rOffset = -dr; rOffset <= dr; rOffset += 2.5) {
				double curR = radius + rOffset;
				if (curR < 1.0) continue;
				double px = ox + c * curR;
				double pz = oz + s * curR;
				int ipx = Mth.floor(px);
				int ipz = Mth.floor(pz);
				int cx = ipx >> 4;
				int cz = ipz >> 4;

				if (cx != lastChunkX || cz != lastChunkZ) {
					lastChunkX = cx;
					lastChunkZ = cz;
					chunk = level.getChunkSource().getChunkNow(cx, cz);
				}
				if (chunk == null) continue;
				int surfaceY = chunk.getHeight(Heightmap.Types.MOTION_BLOCKING_NO_LEAVES, ipx & 15, ipz & 15);
				double ceilY = findCeilingAbove(level, ipx, ipz, Math.max(level.getMinBuildHeight() + 2.0, oy));
				if (ceilY < surfaceY && ceilY > oy) {
					surfaceY = (int) findGroundBelow(level, ipx, ipz, Math.min(ceilY - 1.0, oy + 15.0));
				}

				for (int dy = -3; dy <= 4; dy++) {
					p.set(ipx, surfaceY + dy, ipz);
					if (!level.hasChunkAt(p)) continue;
